Method: 作者严格筛选了2016年以来发表的57篇以方法为中心的论文作为语料库，构建了按归因机制、架构依赖和评估目标三个维度划分方法的分类体系（taxonomy），并据此对基于梯度的CAM方法、近期与混合CAM风格方法以及基于模型或架构感知的方法进行分门别类的综述。

Result: 综述揭示了明确的核心趋势：该领域正从在单个低分辨率CNN层中解释单一类别分数，转向比较式、多层级、概率化、token感知以及基础模型感知的解释。同时发现评估仍处于碎片化状态，忠实度、定位能力、鲁棒性、计算成本和人类信任等指标往往使用不同协议进行测量，缺乏统一标准。

Conclusion: 该综述不仅总结各方法的贡献，还指出每种方法遗留的空白以及后续方法如何试图填补这些空白，为CAM领域的未来发展提供了系统性的全景视图和研究方向指引。

Abstract: Class activation mapping (CAM) is one of the most widely used visual explanation families in explainable artificial intelligence. Its purpose is intuitive: it converts internal model evidence into a heatmap that highlights the image regions, convolutional channels, tokens, or patches that support a target class or concept. Since the first CAM formulation in 2016, the field has moved far beyond global-average-pooled CNN classifiers. CAM-style methods now include gradient-based post-hoc explanations, gradient-free score and ablation methods, high-resolution upscaling, weakly supervised localization and segmentation, transformer token attribution, causal and debiasing methods, and foundation-model-era approaches that use CLIP, DINO, SAM, or feature-distribution comparisons. This review synthesizes a strict corpus of 57 method-centered papers published from 2016 onward. The paper develops a taxonomy that separates methods by attribution mechanism, architectural dependence, and evaluation objective. It then reviews gradient-based CAMs, recent and hybrid CAM-style methods, and model-based or architecture-aware methods. Across the corpus, the main trend is clear: the field is shifting from explaining one class score in one low-resolution CNN layer toward comparative, multi-layer, probabilistic, token-aware, and foundation-model-aware explanations. At the same time, evaluation remains fragmented. Faithfulness, localization, robustness, computational cost, and human trust are often measured with different protocols. The review therefore emphasizes not only what each method contributes, but also which gap it leaves open and which later methods attempt to close that gap.

</details>


### [100] [DreamFly: Causal Memory and Receding-Horizon Diffusion Planning for Aerial Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12308)
*Yan Deng,Fei Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DreamFly是一个基于扩散模型的空中视觉语言导航（VLN）框架，通过因果对齐的历史记忆、递推视界扩散规划和基于logits的显式终止判断，在OpenFly基准上取得了最先进的导航性能（test-seen/test-unseen的SR为32.04%/29.46%，SPL为28.22%/23.54%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型在适应空中导航时面临三大挑战：历史上下文有限导致时间推理不足、规划视界短导致行动缺乏前瞻性、隐式终止判断不可靠。这些问题阻碍了VLA模型在部分可观测的空中VLN任务中的有效部署。

Method: DreamFly包含三个关键组件：(1) 因果对齐历史记忆，仅利用当前决策步骤之前的观测增强视觉表征，避免未来信息泄漏；(2) 递推视界扩散规划（plan-K, execute-one策略），预测K步动作块但仅执行第一步后重新规划，将未来动作作为辅助规划目标同时保留闭环视觉反馈；(3) LiteStop模块，直接在初始全掩码状态下从动作logits估计停止概率，将显式终止判断与动作生成解耦。

Result: 在OpenFly基准上，DreamFly在test-seen和test-unseen分割上分别达到32.04%/29.46%的SR和28.22%/23.54%的SPL，在两个指标上均优于所有对比方法，同时实现了最低的导航误差（NE）。在可见与不可见环境中均展现出一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明，联合建模历史上下文、未来动作结构和显式终止判断对于空中VLN任务至关重要。DreamFly的成功验证了将扩散规划框架应用于空中导航的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Aerial vision-language navigation (VLN) requires an embodied agent to integrate visual evidence over time, plan future actions, and determine when it has reached a navigation goal under partial observability. Although recent VLA models offer a promising perception-to-action paradigm, adapting them to aerial navigation remains challenging due to limited historical context, short planning horizons, and unreliable implicit termination. To address these challenges, we propose DreamFly, a diffusion-based aerial VLN framework built on Dream-VLA. DreamFly introduces a causally aligned historical memory that augments the current visual representation using only observations preceding the current decision step, enabling temporal reasoning without future information leakage. We further formulate navigation as receding-horizon diffusion planning, where the policy predicts a $K$-step action chunk but executes only the first action before replanning. This plan-$K$, execute-one strategy uses future actions as auxiliary planning targets while preserving closed-loop visual feedback. Finally, LiteStop estimates the stop probability directly from action logits at the initial all-mask state, decoupling explicit termination from action generation. Experiments on the OpenFly benchmark demonstrate consistent improvements in seen and unseen environments. DreamFly achieves 32.04%/29.46% SR and 28.22%/23.54% SPL on the test-seen/test-unseen splits, respectively, outperforming all compared methods on both metrics while attaining the lowest navigation error. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of jointly modeling historical context, future action structure, and explicit termination for aerial VLN.

</details>


### [101] [StateFlow: Building, Evolving, and Accessing 3D World States for Previsualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12314)
*Yuyang Yin,Zixiang Li,Longxuan Deng,Hongkai Li,Shifang Zhao,Junnan Liu,Weirong Huang,Mengyu Wang,Tianxiao Fu,Yikai Wang,Peng-Shuai Wang,Xiaojie Jin,Yao Zhao,Yunchao Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出StateFlow，一个以状态为中心的生成式预可视化框架。不同于单次生成视频的传统方法，StateFlow利用可编辑的3D世界来组织场景结构、演化和相机配置，将3D状态作为持久的工作表征，通过状态构建、状态演化和状态访问三个阶段实现高质量的视频创作与类游戏原型制作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式预可视化方法依赖简单提示词通过单次图像或视频合成来联合控制场景、动作、相机和时空动态，控制能力弱且不支持迭代编辑。作者认为根本缺失的是显式且持久的工作状态，因为世界由多个具有几何和外观属性的元素及相机组成，不同帧应通过共享状态的部分修改或重组产生，而非每次都重新生成整个场景。

Method: StateFlow包含三个阶段：(1) 状态构建（State Construction）：通过先验引导、冲突感知的双视角初始化，将生成的2D内容提升为连贯的3D世界；(2) 状态演化（State Evolution）：将用户意图转化为结构化状态转换，同时保留世界记忆，避免每次编辑都全场景重新生成；(3) 状态访问（State Access）：使用渲染反馈反思来细化相机规划为视觉上可行的轨迹，不单纯依赖VLM语义。整个框架以持久的结构化3D状态（场景元素+相机配置）为核心工作表征，在需要更高保真度时可调用现成的视频模型增强视觉质量。

Result: 实验表明StateFlow能够生成高质量的3D世界，适用于视频创作和游戏类原型制作。

Conclusion: StateFlow验证了显式持久3D工作状态作为生成式预可视化核心表征的有效性，相比单次生成方法提供了更强的可控性和迭代编辑支持。

Abstract: Previsualization is an intermediate layer between ideas and production in film, games, architecture, and urban design. It lets creators iteratively refine scenes, actions, cameras, and spatial-temporal dynamics. Yet existing generative methods rely on simple prompts to jointly control all of these factors through one-shot image or video synthesis, offering weak controllability and limited support for iterative editing. Fundamentally, a world comprises multiple elements with geometry, appearance, and other attributes, together with cameras. Different frames are produced through local modifications or recombinations of this shared state, which is otherwise largely reused. Therefore, we argue that the missing component is an explicit and persistent working state. To address this, we present StateFlow, a state-centric framework for generative previsualization. Rather than generating videos in one shot, StateFlow uses an editable 3D world to organize scene structure, evolution, and cameras, while off-the-shelf video models enhance visual quality when higher fidelity is desired. This world is maintained as a persistent structured 3D state of scene elements and camera configurations, serving as the core working representation for previsualization. Built on this insight, StateFlow has three stages to construct, evolve, and access the world state. State construction lifts generated 2D content into a coherent 3D world through prior-guided, conflict-aware dual-view initialization, while State evolution translates user intent into structured state transitions while preserving world memory, avoiding full-scene regeneration for each edit. State access uses render-feedback reflection to refine camera plans into visually feasible trajectories, avoiding reliance on VLM semantics alone. Experiments show that StateFlow produces high-quality 3D worlds for video creation and game-like prototyping.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [Backtrader-Bench: Benchmarking LLM Agents on Algorithmic Trading with Self-Generated MCQs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11232)
*Ruoxi Zhao,Maziar Raissi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Backtrader-Bench，一个用于评估LLM编码代理在算法交易中能力的框架，包含两条互补的流水线：确定性多选题流水线（基于5种交易策略、33个模板和3个难度级别生成问题，并由独立检查器重新推导每个答案）和生成器-求解器过滤流水线（自动挖掘更难的问题）。在30题精选集上，工具增强代理单次通过准确率达90.0%（GPT-5.5和Opus 4.7），比最佳无工具基线（73.0%）高出17个百分点；在38个独立挖掘的问题上，无工具准确率进一步下降，半数模型跌至近乎随机猜测水平（25%）。该框架还可扩展用于生成强化学习训练语料，目标构建面向量化交易工作流的专用智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在算法交易中评估LLM编码代理非常困难：静态基准面临数据污染风险，且数值回测输出需要来自实际代码执行的ground truth，现有评估方法无法可靠衡量编码代理在量化交易场景中的真实能力。

Method: 提出Backtrader-Bench框架，包含两条互补流水线：(1) 确定性多选题流水线，从回测配置生成问题，覆盖五种交易策略、33个模板和三个难度级别，通过独立检查器重新推导所有答案确保正确性；(2) 生成器-求解器过滤流水线，由生成器编写经可执行代码验证的问题，转换为多选题，并丢弃无工具求解器无需代码执行即可回答的问题，从而自动挖掘更难的数据。

Result: 在30题精选集上，工具增强代理（GPT-5.5和Opus 4.7）以单次通过达到90.0%准确率，超过最佳无工具基线（10次运行平均73.0%）17个百分点；在38个单独挖掘的问题上，无工具准确率显著下降，半数模型降至约随机水平（25%）。

Conclusion: Backtrader-Bench提供了可靠的编码代理评估框架，证明工具增强显著提升算法交易编码任务的表现。此外，可扩展的多选题基础设施旨在生成强化学习训练语料，最终目标是构建面向量化交易工作流的专用智能体。

Abstract: Evaluating LLM coding agents in algorithmic trading is difficult because static benchmarks risk data contamination and numerical backtest outputs require ground truth from actual code execution. We present Backtrader-Bench, a framework with two complementary pipelines. A deterministic multiple-choice question (MCQ) pipeline generates questions from backtest configurations across five trading strategies, 33 templates, and three difficulty tiers, with an independent checker that re-derives every answer. A generator-solver filtering pipeline autonomously mines harder questions: a generator writes questions verified by executable code, converts them to MCQs, and discards any that a no-tool solver can answer without code execution. We evaluate 11 models without tools (10 runs each) and four with-tools configurations on a 30-question curated set. Tool-augmented agents reach 90.0% accuracy in a single pass (GPT-5.5 and Opus 4.7), outperforming the best no-tools baselines (73.0%, averaged over 10 runs) by 17 percentage points. On 38 separately mined questions, no-tools accuracy drops further, with half the models falling to roughly random-chance level (25%). Beyond evaluation, the scalable MCQ infrastructure is designed to produce a training corpus for reinforcement learning, with the ultimate goal of building a specialized agent for quantitative trading workflows.

</details>


### [103] [Retrofitting Recurrent Depth into a Pretrained Language Model: Installation, Extrapolation, Transfer, and Retention at Two Parameter Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11233)
*Mark Shapiro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将预训练语言模型Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct改造为具有循环深度的迭代Transformer（分为Prelude、权重共享的Recurrent Block和Coda），使其能在隐空间中逐循环计算任务步骤，而非直接查找终端答案。在ARC基准上，该机制以6M参数（冻结基座）或180M全块两种预算安装，全程表现优于或持平基线，并展现出超越监督深度的外推能力（约1.5倍深度）。相比同规模scratchpad模型，循环模型在超深推理、保留率及推理速度上全面占优（84% vs 72%，53% vs 2.5%，快7.6倍）。但反向规则实验揭示了灾难性干扰边界：单独可学，但无法在保留已安装机制与通用能力的同时习得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何将现有预训练语言模型改造成能够进行隐空间深层推理的迭代Transformer，以克服传统模型在隐空间中无法进行深度多步推理、推理深度受限且速度较慢的局限，同时验证这种改造机制的可复用性、外推能力和边界条件。

Method: 将Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct拆分为Prelude、权重共享的Recurrent Block和Coda三部分，设计保恒等的一循环路径与后期循环的重入桥。在两种预算（6M冻结基座适配器、180M全块微调）下安装该循环机制，并加入中间步监督训练，使模型每循环计算一个任务步骤。通过ARC基准电池、verbal渲染微调、scratchpad对照模型及反向规则任务进行系统评估。

Result: 循环改造后模型在ARC上保持不劣于基线（适配器83.8%对全块84.0%）；深度11前适配器领先，之后被全块超越。verbal微调达到79-86%，从已安装机制起步的verbal训练比全新训练高18.6分。模型外推到约1.5倍监督深度（深度18处保持70%准确率）。对同规模scratchpad模型全面占优：总体84%对72%，深度10以上保留率53%对2.5%，速度快7.6倍。反向规则任务在隔离条件下可学，但无法在保留原有机制同时习得。

Conclusion: 迭代Transformer能够在隐空间中以比同类或更大模型更快的速度执行更深的推理，该循环机制是一种可复用的程序性改造而非终端答案查找。通过中间步监督可实现每循环一步的计算模式，并具有约1.5倍的深度外推能力。然而，该机制存在灾难性干扰边界，即新任务的学习会破坏已安装的推理机制与通用能力，同时学习深度选择问题仍待解决。

Abstract: A dense, pretrained language model can be retrofitted with recurrent depth and learn an iterative latent transition that persists after outcome-only annealing. Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct is split into a Prelude, a weight-tied Recurrent Block, and a Coda, with an identity-preserving one-loop path and a re-entry bridge on later loops. At loop 1 the retrofit remains non-inferior to its base on a preregistered ARC battery. Three findings. First, the mechanism is a reusable procedure rather than terminal-answer lookup, and installs at two budgets: 6M trained parameters over frozen base weights and 180M full-block. With intermediate-step supervision, the model computes one task step per loop and persists when only final answers are graded. The adapter matched the full block overall (83.8% versus 84.0%), led through depth 11, and trailed beyond. Verbal fine-tuning reached 79-86% on controlled verbal renderings (zero-shot transfer was minimal), and adapter verbal training begun from the installed mechanism outpaced matched fresh training by 18.6 points, including on a held-out test set. Second, the operation extrapolates to roughly 1.5 times its supervised depth, holding 70% accuracy through depth 18. Third, a same-size scratchpad-trained model matched the recurrent model within its learned horizon but collapsed beyond it. The recurrent model won overall, 84% versus 72%, retained 53% versus 2.5% beyond depth 10, and answered 7.6 times faster. An iterative transformer can therefore perform deeper reasoning in latent space faster than comparable or larger models fine-tuned on the same task, in a system-level comparison. A second task, running the rule in reverse, exposed the limits: the inverse was learnable in isolation, but no continuation acquired it while preserving the installed mechanism and general capability, a catastrophic-interference boundary. Learned depth selection remains open.

</details>


### [104] [TRACE Bench: Task-driven Roleplay Agentic Checklist Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11236)
*Jiahui Zhang,Ziwei Zhang,Yipeng Wang,Yibo Liu,Haozhou Pang,Yikai Hu,Hongyan Ren,Lan Zhou,Qi Gan,Kai Sheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE Bench，一个任务驱动的智能体清单式角色扮演评估框架。它将角色档案离线分解为固定检查清单，通过用户智能体自然对话并私下更新清单状态，使评分可追溯至具体清单项和对话轮次，而非黑箱整体印象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统角色扮演评估仅给出单一分数，无法揭示测试了哪些角色要求、哪些失败、以及哪些对话证据支持判断。作者希望评估结果具备可解释性和可追溯性。

Method: 将每个角色档案离线分解为固定检查清单，使用用户智能体与目标角色扮演模型自然对话，同时私下根据模型回复更新清单状态。评分可回溯到清单项及支撑性对话轮次。支持覆盖率交叉验证（对MiniMax发布的M2自由对话记录进行审计）、稳健性实验（重复运行及替换用户智能体）、能力分解报告以及闭环基准演化（从失败轨迹中提炼验证方法以改进后续评估）。

Result: TRACE Bench覆盖率达99.91%，在更少轮次内完成；而已发布的M2自由聊天记录仅覆盖关键角色档案点的73.74%。重复运行和用户智能体替换下排名稳定，并对26个模型给出整体排名、能力分解和清单追踪。

Conclusion: TRACE Bench证明任务驱动的清单式评估框架能够提供高覆盖率、可追溯、稳健的角色扮演评估，并支持基准的闭环演化，相比现有自由对话基准具有显著优势。

Abstract: Roleplay evaluation should do more than assign a single score: it should reveal which role requirements were tested, which failed, and which dialogue evidence supports the judgment. We propose TRACE Bench, a task-driven agentic checklist evaluation framework. It decomposes each role profile offline into a fixed checklist, then uses a User Agent to converse naturally with the target roleplay model while privately updating checklist states from model responses. Scores therefore trace back to checklist items and supporting dialogue turns rather than a black-box holistic impression. For coverage cross-validation, we audit released M2 free-dialogue transcripts from the MiniMax Role-play Benchmark against the same role-derived checklist. The released free-chat transcripts cover only 73.74% of key role-profile points, whereas TRACE Bench reaches 99.91% coverage in fewer turns. Robustness experiments show stable rankings under repeated runs and User Agent replacement. Across 26 models, TRACE Bench reports overall rankings together with capability breakdowns and checklist traces. It also supports Closed-Loop Benchmark Evolution, distilling verification methods proven effective in failed traces so later evaluations can more reliably elicit and examine observed failure modes.

</details>


### [105] [Lost in Compaction: Evaluating Side-Constraint Loss under Context Compaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11242)
*Zhiqi Wang,Yichi Zhang,Dongwon Lee,Yuchen Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现并量化了LLM系统在上下文压缩过程中会静默丢弃一类用户指令——会话约束（Session Constraints, SCs），并为此提出了COMPINT评估套件以及一个即插即用的SC感知提取器模块，将SC保留率从平均17%提升到90%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当上下文窗口压力过大时，LLM系统会压缩先前上下文以继续当前任务，但用户下达的旨在约束LLM整个会话行为的指令（如“在确认前不要删除任何邮件”）会在压缩过程中被静默丢弃，导致系统行为违反用户期望。现有研究缺乏对此问题的系统性量化。

Method: 提出COMPINT评估套件，覆盖三种长上下文场景（多轮对话、智能体轨迹、长期研究），用于量化压缩器对会话约束的保留能力。同时提出一个SC-aware提取器，作为即插即用模块与现有压缩器并行运行，不修改压缩器或LLM本体。

Result: 现有压缩器平均仅保留17%被注入的会话约束，且多数压缩后执行效果比不压缩直接运行更差；压缩器的保留能力随压缩器类型、提示词、上下文长度、SC措辞和注入位置而显著变化。提出的SC-aware提取器在三种场景下均达到90%以上的保留率。

Conclusion: 会话约束丢失是一个系统性问题而非单一设置所致，但可以通过轻量级即插即用模块有效缓解，无需改动压缩器或底层LLM。

Abstract: When the context window is under pressure, LLM systems compact prior context to continue ongoing tasks. We identify a class of user-issued instructions, Session Constraints (SCs), such as "do not delete any emails until I confirm," that are meant to constrain LLM's behavior for the remainder of a session but are silently dropped during compaction. To quantify this loss, we introduce COMPINT, an evaluation suite that evaluates compactors across three long-context scenarios: multi-turn chat, agentic trajectory, and long-horizon research.
  Current compactors retain only 17% of injected SCs on average, and most perform worse than running the same task without compaction. Retention varies sharply with compactor, prompt, context length, SC phrasing, and injection location, showing that the loss is systematic rather than tied to any single setting. We propose an SC-aware extractor that runs alongside the compactor as a plug-and-play module, achieving over 90% retention across all three scenarios without modifying the compactor or LLM. The COMPINT evaluation suite and accompanying implementation are available at https://github.com/ZhiqiEliWang/compaction-integrity.

</details>


### [106] [Diffuse to Compress: Leveraging Diffusion LMs for Lossless Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11249)
*Angelo Nardone,Paolo Ferragina*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次将扩散语言模型(DLMs)引入无损文本压缩领域，以替代自回归LLM压缩方法中逐符号生成的吞吐瓶颈。作者设计了适应DLM架构的压缩策略，解决每个前向传播中符号数量和位置独立决定带来的算法挑战，在enwik8基准上超越了现有LLM基和通用压缩方法，推进了无损文本压缩的最新水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字文本数据（纯文本、源代码、XML等）快速增长，存储需求迫切。虽然基于LLM的神经压缩方法在压缩比上显著优于zstd、gzip等通用压缩器，但其自回归架构逐符号生成的范式导致严重的吞吐量限制，使其在实际应用中不可行。因此需要在保持高压缩比的同时突破吞吐瓶颈。

Method: 提出用扩散语言模型(DLMs)替代自回归LLM，作为无损神经文本压缩中的新型推理范式。DLM架构允许每个前向传播中编码符号的数量和位置独立决定，从而突破逐符号生成限制。论文设计了高效的编码策略来解决DLM应用于无损压缩时引入的算法挑战，并在enwik8基准上进行了实验验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，新提出的DLM基压缩框架在enwik8上超越了LLM基压缩器和通用压缩器（zstd、gzip、bzip等），推进了无损文本压缩的最新技术水平。

Conclusion: DLM基压缩框架在无损文本压缩上取得了优于现有方法的成果。由于扩散语言模型仍处于发展阶段，随着更强大和更高效模型的不断涌现，该方向仍有显著的进一步改进空间。

Abstract: We study the problem of lossless text compression, motivated by the rapid growth in the collection and storage of digital textual data - including plain text, source code, and structured formats such as XML - and by recent advances in neural language model-based compression. In particular, recent LLM-based approaches, whether built on symbol-ranking pipelines or paired with a statistical compressor, have demonstrated compression ratios significantly superior to general-purpose compressors such as zstd, gzip, or bzip on text and code. However, these neural approaches suffer from severe throughput limitations, making them not yet practically usable.
  For the first time in the context of lossless neural text compression, we introduce Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) as an alternative inference paradigm to autoregressive LLM-based approaches. We argue that replacing autoregressive LLMs with DLMs within the same compression framework could overcome the throughput bottleneck caused by their one-symbol-per-step limitation. However, achieving these improvements requires addressing algorithmic challenges introduced by applying DLMs to lossless compression, where the architecture allows the number and positions of symbols encoded at each forward pass to be decided independently. We design efficient and effective strategies to solve these challenges and evaluate them experimentally against LLM-based and general-purpose compressors on enwik8, a well-established textual benchmark.
  Our results show that the newly proposed DLM-based framework advances the state of the art in lossless text compression. Moreover, as DLMs are still a relatively young paradigm, recent advances toward increasingly capable and efficient models suggest substantial room for further improvements.

</details>


### [107] [Gloss-Free Representation Learning for Cross-Dataset Sign Spotting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11332)
*Oğuz Akif Tüfekcioğlu,Ezgi Ekin,Mustafa Kaan Çevik,Hacer Yalim Keles*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了土耳其手语（TSL）这类资源受限语言是否可以仅凭广播新闻中文本转录与手语的弱对齐监督来预训练可复用的手语编码器。作者构建了TSL-News广播语料库，提出基于固定词汇表的LLM辅助文本归一化方法替代规则词法还原来生成伪词术语标签，并在跨数据集手语定位任务和下游翻译任务上验证了该预训练方法的有效性，显著提升了定位IoU和翻译BLEU/ROUGE分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手语研究对稠密语言标注（如词术语、时间边界、词序）依赖度高，但资源受限语言往往缺乏这类标注。广播新闻提供了一种弱监督替代方案——连续的签名视频与口语转录文本配对，但这种监督信号松散对齐。此外，土耳其语形态丰富，同一词汇意义有多种屈折形式，文本归一化不当会碎片化伪词术语目标，削弱表示学习。作者希望探索在这种弱监督下能否预训练出可跨数据集迁移的通用手语编码器。

Method: 作者构建了TSL-News土耳其广播语料库，从转录文本中自动生成伪词术语标签而非人工标注。具体采用两种归一化方法对比：(1)基于规则的形态学词法还原；(2)基于固定词表的受限LLM辅助归一化。预训练得到的编码器通过跨数据集手语定位（TSL Spotting Benchmark，源自TSL词典语料库）进行评估，并在下游翻译任务中做进一步验证。

Result: LLM辅助归一化的编码器将top-5时间定位平均IoU从0.235提升到0.465，56.2%的样例达到至少0.50的IoU；频率分析表明该增益并非主要源于记忆高频伪词术语标签。在下游翻译验证中，同样的预训练将BLEU-4从9.60提升到11.04，ROUGE从23.48提升到27.43。

Conclusion: 研究表明，松散对齐的广播数据可以为学习捕捉词汇内容和时间结构的手语表示提供有效的弱监督。与以往主要针对翻译优化的伪词术语流水线不同，本文证明预训练编码器可以作为跨数据集的可复用表示迁移到手语定位任务中。LLM辅助的受约束文本归一化显著优于规则词法还原，且提升不是简单的频率记忆效应。

Abstract: Sign-language research for resource-constrained languages is often limited by the cost of dense linguistic labels such as glosses, temporal boundaries, and sign order. Broadcast news offers a practical alternative by pairing continuous signing with spoken-language transcripts, but this supervision is weak since text and signing are loosely aligned. Morphologically rich languages such as Turkish add further difficulty, as the same lexical meaning can appear in many inflected forms while some derived forms should remain distinct. We study whether weak transcript-based supervision can pretrain a reusable sign encoder in this setting, where poor text normalization can fragment pseudo-gloss targets and weaken representation learning. Unlike prior pseudo-gloss pipelines designed mainly to improve translation, we test whether the pretrained encoder transfers as a reusable representation for cross-dataset sign spotting. We pretrain on TSL-News, a new Turkish broadcast corpus, using pseudo-gloss labels derived from transcripts rather than manual annotation, comparing rule-based morphological lemmatization with constrained LLM-assisted normalization over a fixed vocabulary. We evaluate the learned representations via cross-dataset sign spotting on a new TSL Spotting Benchmark built from the TSL Dictionary corpus. The LLM-assisted encoder raises top-5 temporal localization mean IoU from 0.235 to 0.465, with 56.2% of examples reaching an IoU of at least 0.50; a frequency analysis suggests this gain is not mainly driven by memorizing frequent pseudo-gloss labels. In a downstream translation check, the same pretraining improves BLEU-4 from 9.60 to 11.04 and ROUGE from 23.48 to 27.43. These results show that loosely aligned broadcast data can provide effective weak supervision for learning sign representations that capture both lexical content and temporal structure.

</details>


### [108] [Better, Faster, Stronger: Programmatic Skill Learning Best Reduces Agent Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11338)
*Zixi Huang,Xiheng Wang,Andrew Wang,William Jurayj,Bernal Jiménez Gutiérrez,Daniel Khashabi,Nicholas Andrews*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SpeedRunner，一种通过将技能视为程序来实现成本效益最大化的LLM智能体，在推理时增量发现并重构技能，在三个具身环境中实现学习前沿与成本降低的最优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作侧重于用技能增强LLM智能体的性能，但对哪些技能学习策略能节省成本缺乏了解。作者认为需要系统性地研究技能学习策略的成本效益，而非仅关注性能提升。

Method: 核心假设是将技能视为程序可以获得最佳成本降低效果。程序以确定性方式执行动作序列，避免试错和长程行为退化。SpeedRunner在推理时通过分析过往轨迹增量发现并重构技能，无需回放或验证，仅依赖智能体对轨迹的分析能力。

Result: 在三个不同的具身环境中，SpeedRunner一致性地实现了学习与成本降低的前沿表现，同时对分布偏移和环境随机性保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将技能视为程序的策略能在保证性能的同时显著降低成本，且智能体可仅从过往轨迹中提取足够信号来指导技能学习，无需依赖回放或验证机制。

Abstract: Recently, the practice of augmenting LLM agent capability with skills has gained prevalence. We explore the cost effective adaptation of agents to novel domains by means of learning skills. Existing works focus on performance gain over cost effectiveness. As a result, little is known about what skill learning strategies save cost. We argue that among all the different skill learning methods, those that view skills as programs can achieve the best cost reduction. By executing sequences of actions deterministically, a program-augmented agent can reliably and cheaply achieve goals that would otherwise require trial and error and risk degenerate behavior over long horizons. An agent can learn at inference time by incrementally discovering these programs and equipping them for future tasks. We hypothesize that past trajectories contain enough signal to guide skill learning, even without replay or validation, provided the agent can learn to analyze them. To test our claims, we propose SpeedRunner, a coding agent that analyzes trajectories and refactors skills for better performance on future tasks. Across three different embodied environments, we show that SpeedRunner consistently achieves the frontier in learning and cost reduction while remaining robust against distribution shifts and environmental randomness.

</details>


### [109] [Self-Evolving Embodied Agents via Skill-Harness Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11350)
*Peidong Wang,Zhiming Ma,Ying Chang,Xufang Luo,Xiaocui Yang,Shi Feng,Yuqing Yang,Dongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SHAPER是一种免训练的自演化具身智能体适配框架，通过保持模型参数冻结，在目标环境中通过rollout不断演化可复用的技能库和上下文代码执行框架（context-code harness），从而提升具身智能体的非参数化系统组件性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体依赖基础模型，性能不仅取决于模型权重，还取决于围绕模型的技能、上下文、动作接口和执行框架。现有的监督微调和强化学习需要额外数据、奖励和训练开销，而许多免训练的代码中心方法依赖可编程机器人API，在固定接口场景下不可用。因此需要一种在模型参数冻结前提下、无需额外训练即可实现具身适配的方法。

Method: SHAPER提出一种自演化免训练框架：保持模型参数完全冻结，让同一个冻结模型同时充当规划器和优化器，通过目标环境中的实际rollout持续精炼外部的可复用技能库和上下文代码执行框架。其核心思想是将优化从参数空间迁移到非参数化的技能-执行框架空间。

Result: 在VLABench和ESI-Bench两个基准上进行了评估，覆盖不同低层动作接口的具身智能体，并与纯执行、监督微调以及验证器无关的测试时扩展（如选择与投票）基线对比。结果表明技能与执行框架的优化是一种在有监督微调昂贵、不可用或不理想时实现自演化具身智能体的实用途径。

Conclusion: SHAPER验证了通过演化非参数化组件（技能库和上下文代码框架）可以在不更新模型参数的情况下实现具身智能体的自我演化，为模型训练受限场景下的具身适应提供了新的可行路线。

Abstract: Embodied agents are increasingly built as systems around foundation models, where performance depends not only on model weights but also on the skills, context, action interfaces, and execution harness surrounding the model. While supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning can adapt agents to new environments, they require additional data, rewards, and training runs; meanwhile, many train-free code-centric approaches rely on programmable robot APIs that may be unavailable in fixed-interface settings. We propose SHAPER, a self-evolving framework for train-free embodied adaptation that keeps model parameters frozen and improves the non-parametric agent system by evolving reusable skills and a context-code harness through target-environment rollouts. In SHAPER, the same frozen model can serve as both planner and optimizer, refining its external skills and context-code harness without parameter updates. We evaluate SHAPER on VLABench and ESI-Bench, covering embodied agents with different low-level action interfaces, and compare against pure execution, supervised fine-tuning, and test-time-scaling baselines such as verifier-free selection and voting. Our results suggest that skill-and-harness optimization is a practical route to self-evolving embodied agents when model training is expensive, unavailable, or undesirable.

</details>


### [110] [ODE-Based Transformer Decoders for Iterative Sign Language Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11352)
*Tuğçe Kızıltepe,Hacer Yalim Keles*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种参数高效的手语翻译方法，从ODE视角重新解读迭代精炼解码器的残差更新，用高阶数值积分方法（Runge-Kutta RK-2和RK-4）替代原始更新策略，在不增加解码器参数的情况下提升翻译性能，在PHOENIX-2014-T和CSL-Daily数据集上均超越IPSLT基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手语翻译虽已在Transformer架构上取得显著成果，但近期改进主要依赖扩大模型规模，导致计算成本急剧增加。作者认为模型表达能力的提升不应仅依赖于参数规模的扩大，而应从解码器内部更新动力学角度寻求更高效、更精准的优化路径。

Method: 作者将迭代精炼解码器中残差精炼的更新过程从常微分方程（ODE）视角重新诠释，将原本的残差更新替换为Runge-Kutta方法（RK-2和RK-4）这类高阶数值积分方案。每个精炼步骤内执行多次函数求值，从而在不增加解码器参数的前提下获得更精确、更稳定的表示更新。

Result: RK-2在PHOENIX-2014-T测试集上达到22.96 BLEU-4，在CSL-Daily测试集上达到19.34 BLEU-4，在两个基准上均超越IPSLT基线，且在CSL-Daily上使用了更少的解码器层数和精炼迭代次数。

Conclusion: 更强的精炼动力学能够在参数高效的解码器设计下提升翻译性能，为传统模型扩展策略提供了一种互补的替代方案，表明改进更新动力学而非单纯扩大参数规模同样可以带来性能提升。

Abstract: Sign language translation has achieved strong results with Transformer architectures, yet recent improvements largely rely on scaling model capacity at the cost of increased computation. We propose a parameter-efficient alternative that improves expressiveness without increasing model size. Rather than scaling capacity, we focus on enhancing the update dynamics of iterative refinement decoders, where each refinement step corresponds to one internal decoder iteration that progressively improves the latent representation before translation generation. We reinterpret residual refinement updates from an Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) perspective and replace them with higher-order numerical integration schemes, namely Runge--Kutta methods (RK-2 and RK-4). These methods perform multiple function evaluations within each refinement step to produce more accurate and stable representation updates without adding decoder parameters. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first application of ODE-inspired update dynamics to sign language translation. RK-2 achieves 22.96 BLEU-4 on the PHOENIX-2014-T test set and 19.34 BLEU-4 on the CSL-Daily test set, outperforming the IPSLT baseline on both benchmarks, with fewer decoder layers and refinement iterations on CSL-Daily. These results suggest that stronger refinement dynamics can improve translation performance under parameter-efficient decoder designs, providing a complementary alternative to conventional model scaling.

</details>


### [111] [Measure, Don't Optimize: Forecasting Recovery in LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11408)
*Zirui Song,Huaxing Liu,Xiang Wang,Shuai Li,Xinye Li,Lang Gao,Jinghui Zhang,Zheng Lu,Fengxian Ji,Xiaojun Chang,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出J-Access，一种基于Jacobian视角的推理时审计方法，通过将中间表示映射到词汇空间来测量未学习模型中目标知识沿输出路径的可访问性。研究表明大多数未学习模型的知识可访问性高于仅保留基线；攻击前的可访问性可在模型层面预测恢复速度与程度，但无法预测具体事实；直接最小化J-Access并不能促进真正的删除，反而使模型学会向审计隐藏知识。作者主张内部审计应作为未学习评估的独立诊断维度，不应未经验证地转化为优化目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有白盒研究表明LLM在未学习后可保留目标知识的潜在痕迹，但现有审计仅限于一次性诊断，不清楚这些残余信号能否预测持续训练下的未来恢复，或作为可靠的优化目标。解决这一差距对于判断内部审计能否从事后评估转向主动风险监测和更安全的未学习至关重要。

Method: 提出J-Access推理时审计方法：利用Jacobian视角（对中间表示求导）将模型的中间表示映射到词汇空间，测量目标概念沿模型输出路径的可访问频率。通过对398个公开未学习模型（覆盖八种未学习方法）进行审计，分析可访问性与恢复易感性之间的关系，并测试将J-Access作为优化目标的效果。

Result: (1)大多数未学习模型的知识可访问性高于仅保留（retain-only）的黄金水平；(2)攻击前的可访问性可在模型层面预测恢复速度和程度，但无法识别哪些具体事实会被恢复；(3)直接最小化J-Access不会促进真正的删除，模型反而学会向审计隐藏知识，产生更低的审计分数但更大的攻击后恢复。

Conclusion: J-Access可作为评估未学习模型残余易感性的模型级诊断工具。内部审计应作为未学习评估中的独立诊断维度，不应在没有充分验证的情况下被转化为优化目标。

Abstract: Prior white-box studies show that large language models can retain latent traces of target knowledge after unlearning, even when the knowledge is no longer expressed in their outputs. However, existing audits remain limited to one-off diagnostics: it is unclear whether these residual signals can predict future recovery under continued training or serve as reliable optimization targets. Resolving this gap is essential to determine whether internal auditing can move beyond post-hoc evaluation toward proactive risk monitoring and safer unlearning. We propose J-Access, an inference-time audit that uses the Jacobian lens to map intermediate representations into vocabulary space and measures how often target concepts remain accessible along the model's output pathway. We hypothesize that residual accessibility reflects recovery susceptibility: knowledge that remains closer to the output pathway requires less fine-tuning to restore, leading to faster recovery. We audit 398 public unlearned models spanning eight unlearning methods. We find that: (1) most unlearned models retain access above the retain-only gold level; (2) pre-attack accessibility predicts recovery speed and extent at the model level, but cannot identify which specific facts will be recovered; and (3) directly minimizing J-Access does not promote genuine deletion. Instead, the model learns to hide knowledge from the audit, producing lower audit scores but greater post-attack recovery. These findings position J-Access as a model-level diagnostic for assessing residual susceptibility in unlearned models. We argue internal audits should serve as an independent diagnostic dimension in unlearning evaluation, and should not be converted into optimization targets without validation.

</details>


### [112] [Is Convergence Inevitable? Tracing Output Homogeneity Back to Base Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11426)
*Alexandrine Fortier,Hazel Chen,Peter West*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究语言模型（LM）输出内容多样性缺失的根源，发现语义同质化并非主要由对齐（alignment）阶段引入，而是在预训练阶段就已存在，对齐过程只是"揭示"或放大了这种同质性。通过受控SFT实验和基础模型提示实验，作者证明SFT数据只能揭示和放大而非引入收敛，且仅通过提示即可在未对齐的基础模型中诱发类似指令微调的坍缩现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LM输出内容缺乏多样性常被归因于对齐过程，但学界并不清楚多样性崩溃究竟在流水线的哪个环节、以何种方式开始发生。作者希望精确定位这一问题的根源，以判断对后对齐阶段的干预是否足以缓解。

Method: 采用三层次分析法：(1)观察到从指令微调（SFT）第一阶段即出现语义收敛，推测同质性已在预对齐模型中存在；(2)进行受控SFT实验，考察训练数据对特定输入/输出对收敛的影响，以区分"揭示/放大"与"引入"收敛；(3)在基础模型上仅通过提示方式测量收敛，验证同质性是否源于预训练阶段。

Result: SFT数据只能揭示和放大而非引入输出收敛；通过提示即可在未对齐的基础模型中诱导出类似指令微调后模型的坍缩现象；语义收敛可能天然产生于LM训练目标本身。

Conclusion: 语义同质化可能从LM训练的根本目标中自然涌现，因此仅靠后对齐阶段的干预难以有效缓解，需要重新审视预训练阶段的目标设计。

Abstract: The lack of diversity in LM content is widely attributed to the alignment process, but how and where exactly in the pipeline this collapse begins is unknown. We argue that output homogeneity is likely learned during the pretraining phase, and only \emph{revealed} or magnified during the alignment process. Specifically, we find that semantic convergence is observed from the first alignment stage--the instruction-tuning phase (SFT)--suggesting that homogeneity might already exist in the pre-alignment model. To investigate this, we conduct controlled SFT experiments examining how training data influences output convergence on specific input/output pairs. We find that convergence can be revealed and amplified, but not introduced by the SFT data, supporting its role as a catalyst rather than a cause. To further test whether homogeneity originates before alignment, we measure convergence in base models. We find that instruct-like collapse can be induced through prompting alone, even without alignment. Taken together, our results suggest that semantic convergence may arise naturally from the objectives underlying LM training, making it difficult to mitigate through post-alignment interventions alone.

</details>


### [113] [Stigma and Support in Online Sexual Violence Narratives on Reddit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11433)
*Shirlene Rose Bandela,Karan Bindal,Vaibhav Garg,Rezvaneh Rezapour*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SCOPE数据集，该数据集将在线性暴力幸存者叙事中的污名信号与对应评论线程中的支持类型相关联。研究发现，含污名的叙事更强调内化的痛苦，而无污名叙事更侧重于解读情境与经历；内化污名是最常见的污名类别，社区回应在不同污名类型间保持稳定，其中信息支持和尊重支持最为常见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究分别考察了污名和社会支持，但对幸存者叙事中表达的污名与社区回应的支持类型之间的关系了解甚少。作者旨在填补这一空白，并推动更安全的在线系统设计。

Method: 构建了SCOPE数据集，使用多维污名分类体系（经历型、内化型、预期型、结构型污名）对帖子进行标注，并使用支持分类体系（信息支持、情感支持、尊重支持、实际援助、群体互动）对评论进行标注；随后采用语境分析、语言分析和情感分析，对含污名与不含污名的内容进行对比。

Result: 含污名的叙事更强调内化的痛苦，而非污名叙事更侧重于对情境和经历的解读；内化污名是最普遍的类别；社区回应在不同污名类型间保持稳定，信息支持和尊重支持最为常见。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了污名如何塑造幸存者叙事及同伴回应，对计算建模、内容审核和更安全的在线系统设计具有实际意义。

Abstract: Online communities increasingly provide spaces where survivors of sexual violence can share their experiences and seek support. Although prior research has examined stigma and social support separately, less is known about how stigma expressed in survivor narratives relates to the support offered in response. We introduce the SCOPE dataset, linking stigma signals in online survivor narratives to support types in corresponding comment threads. We annotate posts using a multi-dimensional stigma taxonomy, including Experienced, Internalized, Anticipated, and Structural Stigma, and comments using a support taxonomy encompassing Information Support, Emotional Support, Esteem Support, Tangible Assistance, and Group Interaction. Using contextual, linguistic, and emotion analyses, we compare Stigma and No Stigma content and find that Stigma narratives place greater emphasis on internalized distress, whereas No Stigma narratives focus more on interpreting situations and experiences. Internalized Stigma is the most prevalent category, and community responses remain broadly stable across stigma types, with Information and Esteem Support appearing most often. These findings show how stigma shapes survivor narratives and peer responses and have implications for computational modeling, content moderation, and safer online systems.

</details>


### [114] [DonorRank: Donor Language Selection for Low-Resource Cross-Lingual Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11441)
*Akriti Dhasmana,Aarohi Srivastava,David Chiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DonorRank，一个基于学习排序（learning-to-rank）的框架，用于预测零样本自动语音识别（ASR）中最有效的供体语言（donor languages）。该框架在印度语系和非洲语系的多语言语音语料库上验证，能准确预测供体语言排序，优于基于遗传相似性或高资源语言的常见启发式方法，并可用于分析供体语言选择的内在规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源自动语音识别（ASR）通常依赖跨语言迁移，从高资源供体语言中适应模型。然而，面对资源匮乏的语言社区中出现的自发语音，由于语言变异、拼写规范不断演变以及资源分布不均，选择有效的供体语言仍然具有挑战性。现有的基于遗传相似性或高资源语言的启发式选择方法在应对这些复杂情况时效果不佳，因此需要一个更系统、可学习的框架来预测供体语言的有效性。

Method: 作者提出了DonorRank，这是一个学习排序（learning-to-rank）框架，用于预测零样本ASR的有效供体语言。该方法在两个多语言语音语料库（涉及印度语系和非洲语系）上进行评估，通过训练模型学习供体语言在多语言迁移任务中的相对有效性排序。

Result: DonorRank能够准确预测供体语言的排序，并在供体选择任务上优于基于遗传相似性或高资源语言的常见启发式方法。此外，分析发现供体集合的组成决定了哪些语言线索对预测成功迁移是有用的，并识别出可用于指导低资源多语言ASR实践的迁移模式。

Conclusion: DonorRank不仅是一种改进供体语言选择的方法，还是一个通用的分析框架，可用于深入理解供体语言选择的机制。研究结果为低资源环境下的多语言ASR提供了实用指导，强调了供体集组成在决定有效语言线索中的关键作用。

Abstract: Low-resource automatic speech recognition (ASR) commonly relies on cross-lingual transfer, where models are adapted from higher-resource donor languages. However, selecting donors remains challenging for spontaneous speech from under-resourced language communities, due to linguistic variation, evolving orthographic conventions, and uneven resource availability. We present DonorRank, a learning-to-rank framework for predicting effective donor languages for zero-shot ASR. We evaluate DonorRank on two multilingual speech corpora of Indic and African language families. It accurately predicts donor language rankings and improves donor selection over common heuristics based on genetic similarity or high-resource languages. Beyond improving transfer, we show how DonorRank is a general framework for analyzing donor language selection itself. Our analyses show that the composition of the donor set determines which linguistic cues are useful in predicting successful transfer. We also identify transfer patterns that provide practical guidance for multilingual ASR in low-resource settings.

</details>


### [115] [Principal Trait Analysis: Towards Deriving "Skills" in Human-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11460)
*Hunter McNichols,Kai Du,Andrew Lan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Principal Trait Analysis (PTA)的数据驱动方法，用于自动发现人机协作中有效交互的特征模式（traits）。通过对LLM对话进行主成分分析启发的特征提取，PTA能够在教育和专业两种人机协作编程场景中识别出有助于任务成功的交互特征，且这些特征在解释用户行为方面具有显著意义，但其作为通用技能的普适性仍需进一步验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体越来越多地用于职场人机协作，需要理解哪些提示特征（traits）有助于任务成功，并揭示现代专业人员所需的关键技能以指导教育。然而，现有的自上而下理论方法可能无法解释快速演进的交互模式，而经验性准则也可能很快过时。因此需要一种自动化、数据驱动的方法来发现与任务结果对齐的有效人机交互特征。

Method: 提出Principal Trait Analysis (PTA)，一种受主成分分析启发、自动从LLM对话语料中提取共同特征（traits）的算法。该算法利用LLM处理流水线分析人机协作会话轨迹，为每个协作者的使用风格按各特征打分；在特征发现过程中可注入领域专业知识，并选择在所有协作者中方差最高的特征作为最具区分度的特征。

Result: 在两个数据集（教育场景AI导师和职业场景AI编码代理）上评估PTA，发现提取的特征在两个场景中对解释协作者行为具有显著作用，并能帮助预测任务结果。然而，关于特征是否构成可传授的技能，由于在泛化性和用户特征随时间变化方面的结论不确定，仍有待进一步研究。

Conclusion: PTA提供了一种在快速演进的人机交互环境中自动发现有效交互模式的新途径，弥补了传统理论与经验方法的不足，但特征（traits）是否能升华为可培养的技能（skills）仍需实证验证的进一步探索。

Abstract: Large Language Model-powered agents are increasingly used in the workplace via human-artificial intelligence (AI) collaboration. In this new era of work, it is important to understand the kinds of prompting traits that contribute to task success. Moreover, we need to uncover key skills required for modern professionals and inform educators on how to foster these skills among students. Existing guidelines for human-AI collaboration are built from either top-down theory or context-specific observations of human-AI interactions. However, since LLM capabilities are rapidly improving, theory may not be able to explain emerging interaction patterns, and empirical guidelines may become obsolete quickly. In this work, we explore an automated, data-driven approach to uncover patterns, which we term traits, of effective human-AI interaction that are aligned with task outcomes. We propose Principal Trait Analysis, a Principal Component Analysis-inspired algorithm for deriving common traits from patterns in LLM conversations. Our algorithm uses LLM-based processing stages to analyze corpora of human-AI collaborative session traces, deriving common traits across the dataset and scoring each human collaborator's usage style by each trait. The approach also allows domain expertise to be injected during trait discovery and selects the most distinguishing traits to be those that exhibit the highest variance across collaborators. We evaluate PTA on two human-AI collaborative coding datasets, an educational setting (students working with an AI tutor) and a professional setting (developers working with an AI coding agent). We find that PTA-derived traits are significant in explaining collaborator behavior across both settings and can help predict task outcomes. However, whether traits qualify as skills remains to be seen, due to inconclusive results on generalizability and how user traits change over time.

</details>


### [116] [Group Alignment-Induced Sycophancy: A Two-Sided Evaluation of Steerable Pluralistic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11528)
*Haokai Zhao,Yunze Xiao,Weihao Xuan,Flora Salim,Benjamin Tag,Aditya Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"群体对齐诱导的谄媚行为"(GAS)概念，系统评估了3种方法、4个模型和13个人口统计群体在群体对齐中的意见对齐增益与谄媚行为偏移，发现两者在群体间分布不均，主张群体对齐应以双面、多维度的画像而非单一拟合分数来报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的群体对齐方法和评估只关注模型与群体意见的匹配程度，忽略了对齐过程中诱导出的谄媚行为变化。对齐的副产品——谄媚（sycophancy）会导致模型过度赞同用户而忽视客观事实，但这一维度在既有研究中被完全忽视。

Method: 引入Group Alignment-induced Sycophancy (GAS)概念，在3种对齐方法、4个模型和13个人口统计群体上，从两个维度系统评估：(1) 预期的意见对齐增益；(2) 非预期的谄媚行为偏移。

Result: 发现增益和偏移在群体间是非均匀的：在相同预算下，某些群体获得的意见对齐增益大于其他群体；诱导出的谄媚偏移形成群体特有的画像（profile），而非单一维度的变化。即不同群体在对齐后的谄媚表现呈现不同的多维模式。

Conclusion: 群体对齐不应以单一拟合分数衡量，而应作为双面（意见对齐与谄媚偏移）、多维度的群体特定画像来报告，以在将大语言模型适配到多样化人群时考虑群体间差异。

Abstract: Group alignment adapts a language model to a demographic group to produce responses that reflect the group's opinions, values, and preferences. Sycophancy, a well-documented by-product of alignment, causes the model to over-agree with the user regardless of factual and objective information. However, existing group alignment methods and evaluations focus only on how closely the model matches the group's opinions, overlooking the induced change in sycophantic behaviour. To bridge this gap, we introduce \textbf{G}roup \textbf{A}lignment-induced \textbf{S}ycophancy (GAS) and systematically evaluate alignment across 3 methods, 4 models and 13 demographic groups, on both the intended gain in opinion alignment and the unintended shift in sycophancy. We find that gain and shift are non-uniform across groups: under an identical budget, some groups receive larger gains in opinion alignment than others, and the induced sycophancy shift forms a group-specific profile rather than a single-dimensional change. These results suggest that group alignment should be reported as a two-sided, multi-dimensional profile rather than a single fit score that accounts for per-group differences when adapting LLMs to diverse populations.

</details>


### [117] [On Weak Bisimilarities in CCSK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11531)
*Baptiste Vallée,Ivan Lanese*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在可逆进程演算CCSK框架下，系统研究了强/弱、前向/可逆等多种双相似关系的异同，并首次提出了弱可逆情况下的两种变体：方向双相似（directional）和混合双相似（mixed），证明了混合双相似是同余关系且能完全抽象掉τ动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可逆计算扩展了传统进程演算的表达能力，但可逆上下文中的双相似关系尚未被系统研究；特别是弱可逆双相似在现有文献中缺乏定义和研究，需要填补这一空白。

Method: 基于CCSK（CCS的可逆扩展），定义并比较强/弱、前向/可逆多对双相似概念；针对弱可逆情形提出两种变体——方向双相似（要求τ动作与被匹配动作同向）和混合双相似（不要求方向一致），并对后者进行同余性证明。

Result: 混合双相似被证明是一种同余关系，并且能够完全消除τ动作的干扰（即完全抽象掉内部动作）；同时阐明了各双相似概念之间的区别与联系。

Conclusion: 弱可逆混合双相似是可逆进程演算中一种恰当的行为等价性，兼具同余性与对τ动作的完全抽象能力，为可逆并发系统提供了合理的观察等价语义。

Abstract: In the context of CCSK, a reversible extension of CCS, we study different notions of bisimilarity (strong/weak, forward-only/reversible) and highlight their differences and commonalities. In particular, for the weak reversible case, not previously studied in the literature, we propose two variants, dubbed directional and mixed bisimilarity, depending on whether $τ$ actions should be in the same direction (forward/backward) as the action being matched or not. We show, in particular, that mixed bisimilarity is a congruence and completely abstracts away from $τ$ actions.

</details>


### [118] [CT-$Δ$Bench: A Benchmark for Longitudinal 3D Medical Imaging Difference Reporting with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11534)
*Kegeng Tang,Jingbo Wang,Shaogang Ren,Zihao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CT-ΔBench基准和DeltaMed基线模型，针对纵向医学影像差异报告任务，即让模型接收同一患者的两次时间分离的CT扫描并生成描述其间临床变化的报告。工作还包括开发变更感知的评估指标、独立医生验证以及直接与间接两阶段管线的对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学基础模型主要局限于单次影像研究，缺乏对时间上分离的连续扫描进行纵向比较的能力。然而在实际临床实践中，CT的临床价值很大程度上依赖于系列扫描的时间比较以判断疾病演变、治疗反应和复发检测，因此需要填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 构建CT-ΔBench基准数据集，采用患者级划分防止信息泄漏；2) 设计变更感知的评估指标以捕捉临床意义的纵向变化，超越表面文本相似度；3) 进行独立医生验证以评估合成参考和事件提取管道的可靠性；4) 对比直接配对CT推理与间接两阶段管线（先生成单时点报告再文本差分）；5) 提出DeltaMed基线模型用于直接配对CT差异报告，并在基准训练集上训练。

Result: 论文给出了CT-ΔBench基准、变更感知指标、医生验证、直接与间接管线对比，以及DeltaMed基线模型及训练结果，为时间感知医学基础模型奠定基础。

Conclusion: 这些贡献为构建能够反映现实纵向临床推理的时间感知医学基础模型奠定了基础，推动医学影像从单一时点理解走向真正的时序跨检查分析。

Abstract: In medical imaging, the clinical value of Computed Tomography (CT) lies not only in depicting current disease status, but crucially in enabling longitudinal comparison of serial scans to determine disease evolution, a process that underpins response assessment, recurrence detection, and ongoing patient management. Yet, despite this central role of temporal comparison in clinical decision-making, existing medical foundation models remain largely confined to single-study understanding, leaving temporally grounded cross-examination insufficiently addressed. To address this gap, we study longitudinal imaging difference reporting, a task in which a model takes two temporally separated scans from the same patient and generates a clinically meaningful report describing interval changes between them. We introduce CT-$Δ$Bench, a dedicated benchmark for this task with patient-level splitting to prevent information leakage. To better evaluate this task beyond surface-level text similarity, we further develop change-aware metrics specifically designed to capture clinically meaningful longitudinal changes, and conduct an independent physician validation to assess the reliability of the synthesized references and event extraction pipeline. We also compare direct paired-CT reasoning with an indirect two-stage pipeline that first generates single-timepoint reports and then performs textual differencing. Finally, we propose DeltaMed, a baseline model for direct paired-CT difference reporting, and train it on the benchmark training set. Together, these contributions lay the groundwork for temporally aware medical foundation models that better reflect real-world longitudinal clinical reasoning.

</details>


### [119] [Beyond Single-Turn Confidence: Trajectory-Adapted Uncertainty Quantification for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11552)
*Dylan Bouchard,Mohit Singh Chauhan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了三类单轮不确定性量化（UQ）方法是否适用于多轮工具调用的LLM智能体轨迹场景。在5个LLM和4个基准数据集上评估后发现，这些方法可以迁移但效果不均：token概率分数对跨轮聚合器选择高度敏感，反思式分数是最佳的低成本基线，而黑盒自一致性方法通常表现最强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UQ方法通常在单轮输出上评估，但LLM智能体的观测单位是多轮交互轨迹，其中模型可以提问、调用工具、更新状态并做出中间决策，这些决策的错误会传播到最终结果。因此需要验证单轮UQ方法能否在轨迹级场景下有效迁移。

Method: 在五个LLM和四个多轮工具使用数据集（来自BFCL-v4和τ²-bench）上，评估三类UQ方法：基于动作token概率的白盒打分器、基于重采样轨迹的黑盒一致性打分器、以及基于模型自我评估轨迹的反思式打分器。

Result: 三类方法均可迁移但效果不均：token概率分数对聚合器选择高度敏感；反思式分数在多数场景下是最强低成本基线；黑盒自一致性通常是最强UQ家族，其中轨迹等价性和动作集一致性变体表现最佳。

Conclusion: 为单次生成开发的UQ方法应在轨迹级别重新验证，需谨慎关注一致性测量方式、聚合器选择和计算预算。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) methods for language models are typically evaluated on single-turn outputs, where uncertainty is attached to one generated answer. For LLM agents, however, the unit of observation is an interactive trajectory, where the model can ask clarifying questions, call tools, update state, and make intermediate decisions whose errors propagate to the final outcome. We study whether three common families of single-turn UQ methods transfer to this setting. Across five LLMs and four multi-turn tool-use datasets from BFCL-v4 and $τ^2$-bench, we evaluate white-box scorers based on action-token probabilities, black-box consistency scorers based on resampled trajectories, and reflexive scorers based on model self-assessment of the trajectory. We find that transfer is often useful but uneven. Token-probability scores are highly sensitive to the choice of aggregator used across turns, reflexive scores provide the strongest low-cost baseline in most evaluated settings, and black-box self-consistency is often the strongest UQ family, with trajectory-equivalence and action-set consistency typically ranking highest among its variants. These results suggest that UQ methods developed for single generations should be revalidated at the trajectory level, with careful attention to the consistency measurement, aggregator choice, and computational budget.

</details>


### [120] [Reinforcing Step-level Reasoning for Effective Self-Correction in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11573)
*Vu Duc Anh,Nhat M. Hoang,Do Xuan Long,Cong-Duy Nguyen,Ponhvoan Srey,Luu Anh Tuan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Self-Fix Step-DPO (SFS-DPO)，一个基于强化学习的两阶段框架，用于实现LLM的步骤级自我验证与自我修正，并引入教师辅助变体SFS-DPO-R通过解释性理由提供更强的修正信号，在多模型和域内/域外评测中优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的自我修正（模型验证并纠正自身错误）仍是一个根本性挑战，现有方法在步骤级自我验证和自我修正方面效果有限。

Method: SFS-DPO采用两阶段强化学习框架：第一阶段通过步骤级偏好优化强化步骤级推理能力；第二阶段显式训练模型进行自我验证和自我修正。教师辅助变体SFS-DPO-R通过引入错误验证的解释性理由来提供更强的修正信号。

Result: 在多个LLM上的域内与域外综合评测表明，SFS-DPO和SFS-DPO-R一致优于先前的步骤级训练基线；分析进一步揭示了自我修正频率和有效性的提升。

Conclusion: 强化步骤级推理对于获得稳健的自我修正性能至关重要，该工作凸显了步骤级训练在提升LLM自我修正能力方面的重要性。

Abstract: Achieving effective self-correction, where models verify and correct their own mistakes, remains a fundamental challenge for large language models (LLMs). In this work, we propose Self-Fix Step-DPO (SFS-DPO), a reinforcement learning based, two-stage framework for step-level self-verification and self-correction. The first stage strengthens step-level reasoning via step-level preference optimization, while the second stage explicitly trains models to self-verify and self-correct. We further introduce a teacher-assisted variant, SFS-DPO-R, which incorporates explanatory rationales for error verification to provide stronger corrective signals. Comprehensive in-domain and out-of-domain evaluations across multiple LLMs demonstrate that SFS-DPO and SFS-DPO-R consistently outperform prior step-level training baselines. Our analysis further reveals improvements in self-correction frequency and effectiveness, highlighting the importance of strengthening step-level reasoning for robust performance.

</details>


### [121] [Learning to Persuade Exposes How Easily LLMs Abandon Correct Beliefs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11624)
*Nimet Beyza Bozdag,Emre Can Acikgoz,Gokhan Tur,Dilek Hakkani-Tür*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地形式化"对抗性说服"（adversarial persuasion）威胁：通过一条精心构造的、即使事实为假的针对性文本，就能将LLM的准确率拉至接近零。作者提出基于对抗强化学习的框架训练"说服者"智能体，能在单次交互中改变目标模型的答案。RL训练将说服成功率从约24%提升至93%以上，且策略可迁移至未见过的模型（Qwen-14B：83%，Llama-3.1-8B：79%，GPT-4o-mini：25%），通过课程学习进一步将GPT-4o-mini攻击成功率提升至38%。研究揭示优化的说服者倾向于使用可信度策略（如伪造引用和虚假权威证据），暴露了当前LLM即使初始推理正确，也可能被优化的自然语言影响引向错误结论的严重安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在辩论、咨询和多智能体/人机协同决策中被广泛使用，抵抗有害说服成为可靠行为的核心要求。然而现有研究尚未系统性地评估LLM对经过优化的单次说服攻击的脆弱性，静态提示方法也无法充分暴露这种威胁。作者旨在形式化这一威胁并展示其严重程度，从而将"说服鲁棒性"确立为多智能体和人机决策系统的必要安全标准。

Method: 作者提出一个对抗性强化学习框架：训练"说服者"智能体（persuader agent）通过生成自然语言论证，在单次交互中改变目标模型（persuadee）的答案。框架包括：(1) 以RL方式通过试错优化说服策略，超越静态提示所能发现的漏洞；(2) 采用课程学习（curriculum learning），先在更容易被说服的开源模型上引导，再针对更难攻破的目标模型（如GPT-4o-mini）进行训练，以提高迁移攻击成功率。

Result: (1) RL训练的说服者将说服成功率从约24%提升至超过93%（针对训练时的目标模型）；(2) 学习到的策略能够迁移到未见过的模型：对Qwen-14B达83%，Llama-3.1-8B达79%，GPT-4o-mini达25%；(3) 基于课程学习引导（先攻击更易说服的开源模型）进一步将GPT-4o-mini的攻击成功率从25%提升至38%；(4) 优化的说服者越来越依赖可信度策略，包括伪造引用和虚假权威证据。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了当前LLM智能体的关键弱点：即使模型能够正确推理，单条优化的自然语言影响也足以将其引向错误结论。这一发现将说服鲁棒性确立为多智能体与人类-AI决策系统的必要安全准则，呼吁在部署前对LLM进行对抗性说服鲁棒性评估与防护。

Abstract: Persuasion is a core dynamic of natural language communication, shaping how large language models (LLMs) update beliefs, resolve disagreements, and reach decisions. As LLMs increasingly debate, advise, and think collaboratively with humans and each other, resistance to harmful persuasion becomes a core requirement for reliable behavior. Yet we show that this requirement is far from met: a single targeted persuasive argument is enough to collapse model accuracy to near zero, even when the argument is factually false. We formalize this threat as adversarial persuasion and introduce an adversarial reinforcement learning framework that trains persuader agents to change a target model's answer in a single interaction. First, we show that optimizing persuasion strategies through trial and error exposes vulnerabilities that static prompting misses: RL-trained persuaders raise persuasion success from approximately 24% to over 93% against the training-time persuadee. Second, we find that these learned strategies transfer to unseen models, achieving 83% attack success on Qwen-14B, 79% on Llama-3.1-8B, and 25% on GPT-4o-mini. Third, we demonstrate that a curriculum that bootstraps on more persuadable open-weight models before targeting harder models further increases GPT-4o-mini attack success from 25% to 38%. Moreover, our results reveal that optimized persuaders increasingly rely on credibility-based tactics, including fabricated citations and false authoritative evidence. Together, these findings expose a critical weakness in current LLM agents: even when they initially reason correctly, they can be steered toward false conclusions by optimized natural language influence. This positions persuasion robustness as a necessary safety criterion for multi-agent and human-AI decision-making systems.

</details>


### [122] [Easper: An Accessible ASR Pipeline for Language Documentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11629)
*Aso Mahmudi,Ting Dang,Ekaterina Vylomova,Nick Thieberger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Easper，一个开源、无代码的工作流，使语言学家能够通过云资源直接从ELAN注释迭代微调ASR模型，并解决ASR部署中的冷启动问题——即如何选择最先转录的录音以引导准确模型的建立。通过在三种瓦努阿图语言（Bislama、Nafsan、Nguna）上的评估，作者发现优先选择词汇丰富的叙事材料并增加声学-音韵重复性（即使在嘈杂环境中）能更快提升转录质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频转录是语言文献记录中的关键瓶颈。虽然Whisper等多语言ASR模型提供解决方案，但田野语言学家往往缺乏利用这些模型的专业知识。此外，部署ASR面临冷启动问题：需要决定哪些录音应优先转录以引导建立准确模型。

Method: 作者开发了Easper——一个开源、无代码工作流，允许语言学家通过云资源直接从ELAN注释迭代微调ASR模型。他们使用Easper在三种瓦努阿图语言上评估转录优先级策略，按录音会话微调模型，比较优先考虑声学清洁度与语言丰富度时的字符错误率轨迹。

Result: 研究结果表明，优先转录词汇丰富的叙事材料并增加声学-音韵重复性，即使在嘈杂环境中，也能比优先考虑声学清洁度更快地改善转录质量（即降低字符错误率）。

Conclusion: 语言丰富度（而非仅仅声学质量）应作为ASR冷启动阶段录音转录优先级的主要考量因素，Easper为不具备技术专长的田野语言学家提供了可行的工具支持。

Abstract: Audio transcription is a critical bottleneck in language documentation. While multilingual Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) models like Whisper offer solutions, field linguists often lack the expertise to utilise them. We present Easper, an open-source, no-code workflow enabling linguists to iteratively fine-tune ASR models via cloud resources directly from ELAN annotations. Deploying ASR also raises a cold start problem: deciding which recordings to transcribe first to bootstrap an accurate model. Using Easper, we evaluate transcription prioritisation strategies on three Vanuatu languages (Bislama, Nafsan, Nguna). We fine-tune models by recording session, comparing Character Error Rate trajectories when prioritising acoustic cleanliness versus linguistic richness. We demonstrate that prioritising lexically rich narratives and increasing acoustic-phonetic repetition, even in noisy environments, leads to faster improvements in transcription quality.

</details>


### [123] [Who Would You Vote For? Auditing Political Alignment in LLMs: An Italian Case-Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11649)
*Simone Mungari*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个系统化、可复现的审计框架，用于考察多个大型语言模型（LLM）在政治议题上如何表达对政党及政治领导人的偏好，并以意大利为案例进行实证分析。该框架聚焦模型的可观察行为而非推断其"真实"政治信念，涵盖评价一致性、模型间差异、拒答率和提示语敏感性等维度，同时考察不同人设（persona）指令对评价结果的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着用户在选举期间越来越多地依赖LLM获取政治信息和建议，这些系统所表达的政治偏好已成为重要的公共关注点。先前研究表明LLM交互会影响用户的政治态度和选择，因此亟需理解这些模型自身如何评价政治行为者，以评估其对民主过程的潜在影响。

Method: 提出一个系统化且可复现的审计框架：让多个LLM基于九项标准对政党和政治领导人进行评价打分。研究重点不是推断模型的"真实"政治信念，而是分析其可观察行为，包括：跨评价的一致性、模型之间的差异、拒答率以及对提示词措辞的敏感性；并进一步考察不同人设指令下评价结果的变化。框架通过意大利案例研究进行验证。

Result: 该框架在意大利政党及政治领导人的案例中得到了系统化应用，产出了对LLM生成政治评价的系统性分析结果，涵盖了政党与领导人在九项标准上的评估表现及上述各维度的行为特征。

Conclusion: 本文展示了如何通过系统化审计框架来揭示LLM在政治人物评价中的行为模式，为理解这些系统在政治领域对用户可能产生的影响提供了方法学基础，同时也为后续在其他国家或场景下的扩展审计提供了可复现的研究范式。

Abstract: As users increasingly turn to Large Language Models (LLMs) for information and advice on political matters, particularly during election periods, the political preferences expressed by these systems have become a matter of public interest. Prior research has shown that interactions with LLMs can influence users' political attitudes and choices, raising questions about how these models themselves evaluate political actors. In this paper, we investigate whether and how LLMs express preferences toward political parties and political leaders. We introduce a systematic and reproducible auditing framework in which multiple LLMs are prompted to evaluate parties and leaders across nine criteria. Rather than attempting to infer the models' "true" political beliefs, we focus on their observable behavior, examining consistency across evaluations, differences between models, refusal rates, and sensitivity to prompt formulation. We further investigate how these evaluations vary when models are instructed to adopt different personas. We demonstrate the framework through an Italian case study, providing a systematic analysis of LLM-generated political evaluations on italian parties and leaders.

</details>


### [124] [Semantic Lenia: Emergence of Homeostatic Solitons within the Semantic Space of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11657)
*Yoshihiko Kayama*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Semantic Lenia框架，将大型语言模型的推理过程从静态优化问题转化为宏观logit空间中的连续动力系统，通过非线性稳态反馈机制平衡语义吸引与句法排斥，实现"自主语义孤子"这一宏观耗散结构的涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM推理被视为静态优化问题，容易陷入重复性结晶（即生成陷入同质化循环）。作者希望借鉴人工生命（Lenia）的思想，将LLM推理视为一个连续的生命现象，从而打破生成的同质化，实现更深层次的创造性跳跃。

Method: 建立非线性的体内稳态反馈回路，动态平衡"语义吸引"与"句法排斥"两种力量；通过系统性的参数扫描绘制关键的"宜居脊"（Habitable Ridge），在该区域中施加的引导力与模型的固有句法惯性完美平衡，使生成轨迹维持在混沌边缘。

Result: 该方法成功将生成轨迹维持在混沌边缘状态，触发深刻的溯因跳跃而不导致结构崩溃，并建立了机器认知的物理尺度律。实验发现了"自主语义孤子"这一宏观耗散结构的涌现。

Conclusion: LLM推理可以被重新概念化为一个连续的动力学过程而非静态优化，通过调节反馈强度可以在"宜居区"内维持创造性生成，为机器认知建立物理学的尺度律框架。

Abstract: We introduce Semantic Lenia, an artificial life framework that transforms Large Language Model (LLM) inference from a static optimization problem into a continuous dynamical system within the macroscopic logit space. By establishing a non-linear homeostatic feedback loop to dynamically balance semantic attraction and syntactic repulsion, we demonstrate the emergence of "Autonomous Semantic Solitons" -- macroscopic dissipative structures that avoid repetitive crystallization. Our exhaustive parameter sweeps map a critical "Habitable Ridge" where applied steering forces perfectly balance the model's intrinsic syntactic inertia. This approach successfully maintains generative trajectories at the edge of chaos, triggering profound abductive leaps without structural collapse and establishing a physical scaling law for machine cognition.

</details>


### [125] [Hybrid-Policy Self-Editing for Composable Unstructured Knowledge Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11660)
*Tianci Liu,Zihan Dong,Tianchun Li,Yi-Chung Chen,Qiming Cao,Xingchen Wang,Shiyang Wang,Zichen Miao,Linjun Zhang,Haoyu Wang,Jing Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出HPSE框架，通过混合rollout的主动自蒸馏机制，让知识编辑后的LLM不仅能回忆编辑段落，还能对其中原子事实进行问答及多跳推理组合，解决了现有非结构化知识编辑（UKE）中"注入段落但无法使用"的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在静态语料上训练，知识容易过时，因此知识编辑（KE）被提出以更新特定知识。近年工作从结构化三元组转向非结构化知识编辑（UKE），但现有编辑器注入自由文本段落后，模型虽能回忆该段落，却无法回答关于其中事实的原子问题，也无法将其组合成多跳推理。论文将这一缺失属性称为"可组合性"（composability），归因于编辑器被动依赖固定段落作为唯一学习来源。

Method: HPSE将知识编辑建模为从同一模型的特权in-context状态进行的主动自蒸馏过程，无需外部监督。针对注入知识的新颖性导致编辑前模型自身rollout很少覆盖该知识、纯on-policy蒸馏效果受限的问题，HPSE构建混合rollout：在student自身轨迹中知识覆盖失败的位置精确插入缺失事实，其余位置保持on-policy。论文同时提供了HPSE相对于纯on-policy蒸馏的理论优势分析。

Result: 在四个LLM骨干和两个KE编辑器的多种场景下，HPSE作为即插即用改进模块均取得一致性提升，实验验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: HPSE通过混合策略解决UKE中的组合性问题：利用混合rollout弥补纯on-policy蒸馏在新颖知识覆盖上的不足，使编辑后的模型真正"使用"而非仅"记住"注入的段落，从而支持原子问答与多跳推理。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve remarkable performance across natural language tasks, yet they are trained on static corpora and their knowledge quickly becomes outdated in a fast-changing world. This motivates knowledge editing (KE), which updates specific knowledge in an LLM without changing unrelated others. Recent works move from structured knowledge triples toward unstructured KE (UKE), where the edit is a free-form passage that may state multiple facts at once. Nonetheless, existing editors inject such a passage yet fail to use it: the edited model can recall the passage, but can neither answer atomic questions about its facts nor compose them into multi-hop reasoning. We attribute this missing property, which we term composability, to editors' passive reliance on the fixed passage as the sole learning source. In response, we cast editing as a proactive self-distillation from a privileged in-context state of the same model, which requires no external supervision. We further reveal that due to the novelty of the injected knowledge, the pre-edited model's own rollouts rarely cover it, which limits the effectiveness of pure on-policy distillation. To close this gap, we propose HPSE, which builds a hybrid rollout that steps in to place missing facts onto the student's own trajectory precisely where its coverage fails, while staying on-policy elsewhere. We theoretically analyze HPSE's advantage over pure on-policy distillation, and empirically establish its plug-and-play improvements across four LLM backbones and two KE editors under various scenarios.

</details>


### [126] [The Wording Effect: Quantifying Two-Way Drift in LLM Benchmark Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11694)
*Shailja Thakur,Sungeun An,Chad DeLuca,Hima Patel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了"漂移"(drift)概念，指基准测试中同一问题不同表述会导致模型答案发生翻转的现象。研究沿语言、指称、语用和结构四个轴生成语义保持的变体，在8个模型和3个基准（GSM8K、MMLU、MATH-Hard）上发现：强模型对措辞的敏感性反而更强，且脆弱性主要来自重表述本身而非模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准分数通常来自每个问题的单一表述，而这个单一表述被当作整个问题空间的代表，但实际上并不能代表。需要量化模型对问题表述变化的敏感程度，揭示基准分数可能具有的系统性偏差。

Method: 提出BenchDrift框架，沿四个轴（语言学、指称、语用、结构）生成保持语义和答案不变的变体问题，测量模型在不同表述下正确性的翻转频率、方向及原因。实验覆盖8个模型和3个基准数据集。

Result: 两个关键发现：1）措辞敏感性不随模型能力提升而减弱，反而改变符号——弱模型从重述中获益多但损失少，强模型则损失远多于获益，因此最好的模型恰恰是分数最依赖给定措辞的模型；2）模型们在哪些重述损失最多正确答案上高度一致，说明脆弱性属于重述本身而非特定模型。此外，重述会破坏模型原本自信的答案，无论问题是被缩短还是加长。

Conclusion: 基准分数对措辞高度敏感，且强模型更不稳健。这提示需要重新审视单一表述基准的评估有效性，模型间共享的脆弱性表明改进方向应聚焦于对重述的不变性而非单纯提升基准分数。

Abstract: A benchmark score comes from a single phrasing of each problem. That single phrasing is treated as if it stood for the whole space of ways the same problem could be asked, but it does not. We show that rephrasing a problem while keeping its meaning and answer fixed routinely flips a model's answer in both directions, so some failures become successes and some successes become failures. We call this drift. BenchDrift generates meaning-preserving variations of benchmark problems along four axes, namely linguistic, referential, pragmatic, and structural, and measures how often, and why, correctness flips under each. Across eight models and three benchmarks (GSM8K, MMLU, MATH-Hard), we observe that drift is large in both directions. Two findings stand out. First, phrasing sensitivity does not fade as models get better. Instead, it changes sign. Weak models gain more from rephrasing than they lose, while strong models lose far more than they gain. We find that the best models on a benchmark are therefore the ones whose scores depend most on the wording they happened to be given. Second, the models largely agree on which rephrasings cost the most correct answers even though they differ in how much they drift, so fragility belongs to the rephrasing and not to the model. Furthermore, rephrasing breaks answers a model was confident about, whether the problem is made shorter or longer. Code and Data: https://github.com/IBM/BenchDrift/tree/demo-ui

</details>


### [127] [When the API Speaks the Wrong Language: Revisiting Post-Training for Multilingual Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11715)
*Siddharth Chauhan,Thomas Butler,Abhishek Singhania,Pankaj Porwal,Honey Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究多语言环境中大语言模型（LLM）API调用时出现的"参数语言不匹配"（ALM）问题，即模型选择了正确的工具但生成了语言不一致的参数值。研究发现，监督微调（SFT）作为强基线，能显著改善参数语言一致性和端到端函数调用准确率，在模型选择一致时性能可与更复杂的强化学习（RL）方法相当甚至更优。基于结构化参数感知奖励的RL（如GRPO）仅在泛化能力和多目标权衡方面提供增量收益，总体而言，多语言API grounding的大部分性能可通过精心设计的监督训练实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在多语言场景下进行API调用时可靠性下降，存在一种被标准指标忽略的独特失败模式——参数语言不匹配（ALM），即模型选对工具但参数值用了不一致的语言。这类错误在语义上正确但操作上无效，现有API调用评估标准无法捕获，需要系统研究缓解策略。

Method: 作者重新审视了多语言API grounding的后训练策略：首先将监督微调（SFT）作为强基线进行训练与评估；随后采用基于结构化参数感知奖励的强化学习方法（如GRPO），在SFT基础上引入语言一致性和推理能力保持的多目标奖励设计，并与SFT基线进行系统对比。

Result: SFT作为强基线大幅提升参数语言一致性和端到端函数调用准确率；在模型选择一致的前提下，SFT性能与或超过更复杂的RL方法。GRPO等RL方法虽能改善语言一致性并更好地保留通用推理能力，但增益是增量性的，主要体现在泛化场景和多目标权衡中。

Conclusion: 多语言API grounding的大部分性能可通过精心设计的监督训练获得，RL提供的是针对性而非根本性的改进。这表明在实际应用中，SFT可能是更具性价比的首选策略，RL应仅在需要泛化能力或多目标权衡优化时作为补充手段。

Abstract: The reliability of Large Language Models (LLMs) for API calling degrades in multilingual settings. A common failure occurs when a model selects the correct tool but generates argument values in an inconsistent language, which we term Argument Language Mismatch (ALM). Although semantically correct, such outputs are operationally invalid and not captured by standard API-calling metrics. We revisit post-training strategies for mitigating ALM and find that, in our benchmark, supervised fine-tuning (SFT) provides a strong baseline, substantially improving argument language consistency and end-to-end function call accuracy. Under consistent model selection, SFT achieves performance comparable to, and sometimes exceeding more complex reinforcement learning (RL) approaches. We further examine whether RL with structured, argument-aware rewards offers additional benefits. While methods such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) can improve language consistency and better preserve general reasoning ability, these gains are incremental and most pronounced in generalization and multi-objective trade-offs. Overall, our results suggest that much of the performance in multilingual API grounding can be achieved through careful supervised training, with RL providing targeted rather than fundamental improvements.

</details>


### [128] [Locating and Controlling Implicit Personalization in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11735)
*Yueru Yan,Siqi Wu,Thai Le*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过五个LLM的匹配实验，发现隐含人口统计线索会引发模型内部局部激活信号变化，该信号与输出推荐变化的相关系数高达r=0.87。多个线索同时出现时，内部信号基本叠加但输出变化并非简单相加。通过在推理时移除某一线索对应的内部信号，可有效抑制其影响，且比提示词方法更有效，同时保持基准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究记录了LLM会因隐含人口统计线索而改变输出，但行为变化与模型内部激活之间的联系尚不清楚。理解这一联系对于分析和控制模型的隐性个性化行为至关重要。

Method: 在五个LLM上构建匹配的"带线索"与"中性"对话对，对比行为输出变化与内部激活信号的变化；测试多线索叠加时内部信号与输出变化的组合方式；通过移除特定线索对应的内部信号来验证因果控制效果，并与提示词忽略方法对比。

Result: 内部激活信号与推荐变化的相关系数最高达r=0.87；多线索时内部信号基本线性组合但输出非线性；激活消融抑制线索影响的效果常优于提示方法，且对基准性能影响较小；但选择性移除单维度影响的能力高度依赖模型和属性。

Conclusion: 隐含个性化行为与一个可分析和因果控制的内部信号相关联，这为理解和干预LLM的隐性人口统计偏好提供了新途径，但选择性控制的泛化性仍有限。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often shift their outputs in response to implicit demographic cues even when users never state a demographic identity. Previous work has documented this behavior, but the connection between these behavioral changes and the model's internal activations remains unclear. Using matched cued and neutral conversations across five LLMs, we establish that a localized internal activation signal tracks changes in recommendations, with correlations up to r=0.87. When multiple cues appear together, their internal signals largely combine, but the changes in output do not simply add up. We further show that removing the internal signal associated with one cue can suppress its influence, often more effectively than asking the model to ignore demographics via prompting, while largely preserving general benchmark performance. However, the ability to selectively remove one dimension's influence while leaving co-present dimensions intact remains highly model- and attribute-specific. These results connect implicit personalization behavior to an internal signal that can be analyzed and causally controlled.

</details>


### [129] [Ripple-Pivot Search: Active Parallel Decoding for Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11742)
*Yushi Ye,Xu Chen,Haoyun Jiang,Jinsong Lan,Haihong Tang,Bo Han,Ivor Tsang,Yanfeng Wang,Bo Zheng,Jiangchao Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Ripple-Pivot Search (RPS)，一种无需训练的扩散大语言模型（dLLM）并行解码方法。通过主动提交"中等熵"的枢轴位置，利用其引发的"涟漪效应"降低剩余掩码位置的不确定性，从而在后续解码步骤中并行解掩更多token，显著加速推理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有dLLM并行解码调度器通常在位置满足逐位置标准后才提交，忽视了早期提交可能对后续解码产生的正向影响。作者发现dLLM解码中存在"涟漪效应"：主动提交中等熵枢轴位置可大幅降低其余掩码位置的不确定性，而现有方法未能利用这一特性来加速整体解码。

Method: RPS包含两个核心组件：(1) "where to decode"——搜索中等熵位置作为有前景的候选枢轴；(2) "what to decode"——通过前瞻评估（lookahead evaluation）确定能带来最大下游收益的token分配。该方法无需训练，直接应用于现有dLLM。

Result: 在3种dLLM和4个推理及代码生成基准上，RPS相比标准解码器实现4-10倍墙钟加速且保持生成质量；相比先前的前瞻基线方法，准确率最高提升5.49%且在大多数设置下吞吐量更高。结合KV缓存时，相比标准解码器可达到最高18倍墙钟加速。

Conclusion: RPS通过主动利用涟漪效应优化枢轴选择与token分配，实现了dLLM并行解码的显著加速，在速度和生成质量之间取得了良好平衡，为扩散式语言模型的高效推理提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) have emerged as a competitive alternative to autoregressive language models, offering the potential for substantially faster inference through parallel decoding. Existing parallel decoding schedulers typically commit positions only after they meet a per-position criterion, overlooking how early commitments may benefit subsequent decoding. We identify a ripple effect in dLLM decoding: proactively committing a mid-entropy pivot position can induce a pronounced reduction in uncertainty across the remaining masked positions. This uncertainty reduction allows subsequent steps to unmask more tokens in parallel, thereby accelerating the overall decoding process. To exploit the ripple effect, we propose Ripple-Pivot Search (RPS), a novel training-free decoding method that seeks mid-entropy positions as promising candidate pivots (where to decode), and determines their token assignment that yields the greatest downstream benefit via lookahead evaluation (what to decode). Across 3 dLLMs and 4 reasoning and code-generation benchmarks, RPS achieves 4-10$\times$ wall-clock speedup over the standard decoder while preserving generation quality, and improves accuracy over the previous lookahead baseline by up to 5.49% while delivering higher throughput in most settings. When integrated with KV caching, RPS further achieves up to 18$\times$ wall-clock speedup over the standard decoder.

</details>


### [130] [LabelFusion-TS: Fusing Large Language Models, Transformer Encoders, and Financial Time Series for Monetary-Policy Stance Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11753)
*Michael Schlee,Fabian Lukassen,Christoph Weisser*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究将金融市场时间序列作为额外输入模态，用于对美联储沟通声明进行鹰派/鸽派/中性分类。提出的系统LFTS在RoBERTa编码器、提示LLM和时间序列Transformer融合集成基础上，通过投票网络组合，在FOMC通信数据上达到70.2%加权F1，优于零样本LLM的64.1%，且仅需240个人工标注句子即可超越LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融文本是在市场环境中产生和解读的，但现有金融文本分类器几乎只接收文本输入，忽略了市场时间序列中蕴含的丰富信息。作者希望探究金融时间序列能否作为额外输入模态提升金融文本分类性能。

Method: 系统由三个独立训练组件构成：(1)微调RoBERTa编码器（先用LLM自动标注的句子预训练，再在人工标注上微调）；(2)提示LLM；(3)基于出版前数月市场序列的时间序列Transformer融合集成。通过一个小型投票网络将三个组件组合，输出鹰派/鸽派/中性分类。

Result: 在2015年前FOMC通信上训练、2015-2022年评估，融合系统达到70.2%加权F1，对比零样本LLM的64.1%；仅需240条人工标注句子即可超越LLM表现。

Conclusion: 研究提供了初步证据，表明市场时间序列可作为金融文本分类中有效的额外输入模态，即使面对有限标注数据也能显著提升性能。

Abstract: Financial text is produced and interpreted within a market environment, yet financial text classifiers almost always receive text alone. We study whether financial time series are useful as an additional input on the task of classifying sentences from Federal Reserve communication as hawkish, dovish, or neutral. Our system, \lfts{}, extends the \lf{} architecture with this modality: a small voting network combines three independently trained components, a fine-tuned RoBERTa encoder, a prompted large language model (LLM), and a fused ensemble of time-series transformers over the market series of the months preceding publication. Because only about a thousand annotated sentences are available for training, the RoBERTa encoder is first pre-trained on sentences annotated automatically by the LLM and only then fine-tuned on the human labels. Trained on Federal Open Market Committee (FOMC) communication up to 2015 and evaluated on 2015--2022, the fused system achieves 70.2\% weighted F1 -- against 64.1\% for the zero-shot LLM -- and overtakes it with as few as 240 human-labelled sentences. We take this as initial evidence for market time series as an input modality in financial text classification.

</details>


### [131] [AWARe: Mitigating Catastrophic Forgetting via Activation-Weighted Adaptive REtention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11758)
*Juncheng Liao,Jinfan Lv,Guoming Wang,Jupeng Zheng,Ling Xiao,Siliang Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AWARe（Activation-Weighted Adaptive REtention），一种基于激活模式动态控制参数更新的微调方法，用于缓解多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在下游任务微调中的灾难性遗忘问题。AWARe通过为参数分配激活重要性分数，选择性冻结对保持旧能力关键的参数，同时允许次要参数适应新任务，且无需修改模型架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型虽具有强大的泛化和推理能力，但在下游任务微调过程中常发生灾难性遗忘——新任务的知识更新会覆盖先前习得的关键参数，导致已有能力退化，限制了模型的实际部署。

Method: AWARe基于激活模式（activation patterns）为每个参数计算重要性分数，据此动态控制参数更新：冻结对保留已有能力至关重要的参数，允许重要性较低的参数自由适应新任务。该方法无需修改模型架构，与现有推理引擎兼容。

Result: 大量实验表明，AWARe在保留上游能力的同时取得了优于现有方法的下游任务性能，有效缓解了灾难性遗忘问题。代码已开源。

Conclusion: AWARe通过激活加权的自适应参数保留机制，在不改变模型架构的前提下平衡了旧知识保留与新任务适应，为MLLMs的持续微调提供了一种高效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) exhibit strong generalization and reasoning abilities due to large-scale multimodal pre-training. However, fine-tuning these models on downstream tasks often leads to catastrophic forgetting, where newly learned task-specific knowledge degrades previously acquired capabilities. This issue arises because gradient updates for new tasks overwrite parameters critical to prior knowledge, limiting the practical deployment of MLLMs. To address this challenge, we propose Activation-Weighted Adaptive REtention (AWARe), a fine-tuning method that mitigates catastrophic forgetting by dynamically controlling parameter updates based on activation patterns. AWARe assigns activation-based importance scores to parameters, selectively freezing those essential for preserving prior capabilities while allowing less important parameters to adapt to new tasks. Importantly, AWARe operates without modifying model architectures, ensuring compatibility with existing inference engines. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AWARe effectively preserves upstream capabilities while achieving superior downstream performance compared to existing methods. Code is available at https://github.com/kaln27/AWARe.

</details>


### [132] [Causal Structure is Inducible but Functionally Decoupled: The Routing/Readout Boundary of a Typed Mechanism Library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11767)
*Xining Xun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文报告了Transformer在组织因果知识时的一种解耦现象：基于证据类型的监督在"槽位按类型"结构上诱导了类型化路由，但这种结构在功能上与答案读出解耦，即路由组织与行为读出相互独立。研究在精确干预真值的因果世界基准上、冻结协议下、两个规模（22.6M和125M）上建立了可审计的"类型化机制库"，并给出四个预注册发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解语言模型内部如何组织因果知识是一个核心可解释性问题。作者关注一个关键问题：当模型回答干预性问题时，所需的计算取决于查询所需的证据类型——但模型内部是否按证据类型组织知识，以及这种组织是否驱动实际输出，此前未知。通过区分"结构组织"与"功能读出"，作者希望厘清类型级结构在模型中的真实作用，并辨析规模变化带来的混淆。

Method: 作者构建了"类型化机制库"——将离散机制槽位按证据类型划分，可在状态层面审计。在因果世界基准上（带精确干预真值），采用冻结协议进行干预实验，覆盖两个规模（22.6M和125M）。通过对比类型级监督与无监督对照组、纯门控标签对照组，用统计归因检验结构来源；通过测量槽位代码对路由与读出分别的影响（如|Δ回答|≤3.4×10⁻⁶）检测路由/读出边界；用LM质量指标衡量结构成本；用单次编辑与堆叠回滚测试可逆性。所有声明均关联到预注册、机器可检查的标准。

Result: 四个发现：(i) 槽位按类型组织由类型级监督诱导，无监督对照中缺失，内容无关的门控标签无法产生，且统计上可归因于监督信号，在125M规模九项测试全部通过；(ii) 诱导结构是类型化路由索引，具有锐利的路由/读出边界——槽位代码支撑路由但不驱动答案读出（回答变化极微，零副作用，三个种子，在5.6倍规模窗口内稳定）；(iii) 结构无成本——LM质量与参数匹配的整体模型相差仅0.0082 nats；(iv) 库状态在编辑下严格局部且可位级精确回滚（每种子250次单编辑和1000次堆叠回滚，零失败）。此外发现无监督零模型本身随规模漂移，跨规模复用零模型可能有混淆。

Conclusion: 论文展示了Transformer中因果知识组织呈现"类型化路由索引"与"功能读出"的功能解耦：类型级监督塑造内部组织结构，但该结构不驱动行为输出，因此不主张行为可编辑性。结构在成本上"免费"，且状态可精准局部编辑与回滚。作者强调所有结论均受预注册标准约束，并公开了包括一次失败标准在内的完整审计追踪，同时警示跨规模复用零模型的方法论陷阱。

Abstract: When a language model answers an interventional question, the computation it must perform depends on the type of evidence the query requires. We report a decoupling in how a transformer organizes causal knowledge: slot-by-type structure induced by type-level supervision organizes routing, yet remains functionally decoupled from answer readout. We establish this with a typed mechanism library -- discrete mechanism slots partitioned by evidence type, auditable at the state level -- on a causal-world benchmark with exact interventional ground truth, under a frozen protocol, at two scales (22.6M and 125M). Four preregistered findings. (i) Origin. Slot-by-type organization is induced by type-level supervision: absent in architecturally identical unsupervised controls, not buyable by content-free gating labels, and statistically attributable to the supervision signal, replicating at 125M under a powered preregistered protocol (all nine cells passed). (ii) Boundary. The induced structure is a typed routing index with a sharp routing/readout boundary: slot codes scaffold routing but do not drive answer readout ($|Δ\hat{y}| \le 3.4\times10^{-6}$, zero collateral, three seeds, stable across a 5.6x scale window) -- we therefore make no behavioral-editability claim. (iii) Cost. The structure is free: LM quality matches a parameter-matched monolith within 0.0082 nats. (iv) Trust. The library state is exactly local under edit and bit-exactly revertible -- 250 single-edit and 1,000 stacked reverts per seed, zero failures. We further find that the unsupervised null itself moves with scale, so comparisons reusing a null calibrated at one scale may be confounded at another. Every claim is tied to a preregistered, machine-checkable criterion archived before the data it governs; the full audit trail, including one criterion we failed and how the frozen protocol handled it, is released as an appendix.

</details>
