### [133] [Diagnosis Before Recovery: Turning Agent Failures into Selective Self-Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11772)
*Pan Wang,Yihao Hu,Hang Wang,Zirui Lv,Xin Zhang,Jianshe Li,Jiang-Ming Yang,Wei Wu,Yongqi Tong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DARC 是一种诊断引导的恢复框架（diagnosis-guided recovery harness），通过开发集失败模式剖析、从共享恢复库中剪除不匹配的干预手段、并冻结验证器选择的成功-成本策略，使自纠错从"提示词扩展"转变为"恢复接口设计"。在 ALFWorld、AppWorld 和 XBRL Finance 三个基准上，该方法相比基线智能体和通用恢复手册，在提升平均任务性能的同时减少了环境步数或检索预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在编程智能体中，编译器、测试和执行轨迹能将大量失败转化为类型化的恢复信号，自纠错非常有效；但广泛的语言智能体任务通常只暴露粗粒度的任务失败。通用恢复手册会拓宽智能体的上下文，恰好与系统需要更窄修复接口的需求相矛盾，混合了无效动作、缺失程序、严格格式错误等不兼容信号。因此需要一种方法来恢复缺失的诊断支撑基质，使修复具有选择性。

Method: DARC 采用以下三步因果顺序流程：(1) 剖析任务族的失败模式（profiling task-family failure modes）；(2) 从共享恢复库中剪除不匹配的干预手段（pruning mismatched interventions）；(3) 冻结一个由验证器选择的成功-成本策略用于部署（freezing a verifier-selected success-cost policy）。该顺序使纠错具有选择性：先确定何种失败可被修复，再决定投入多少恢复证据。

Result: 在 ALFWorld、AppWorld 和 XBRL Finance 三个环境中，同一协议分别产生了一个动作有效性恢复框架、一个程序性恢复回退策略和一个格式精度检索策略。在每个评估场景中，相比基础智能体和通用恢复手册，该方法都提升了平均任务性能，同时减少了环境步数或检索预算。实验表明失败不应必然触发均匀的上下文扩展。

Conclusion: DARC 将自纠错从提示词扩展转变为恢复接口设计，为在缺乏编译器类反馈的领域中实现更可靠的智能体提供了实用路径——在使上下文变大之前，先使失败变得可操作。

Abstract: Self-correction is particularly useful when a failure constrains the next repair. Coding agents benefit from this property because compilers, tests, and execution traces turn many failures into typed recovery signals, but broad language-agent tasks often expose only a coarse task failure. This creates a tension for generic recovery playbooks: they broaden the agent's context precisely when the system needs a narrower repair interface, mixing incompatible signals for invalid actions, missing procedures, and strict-format errors. Our insight is that development-set failures can recover part of the missing diagnostic substrate by deciding which recovery interventions are admissible before test-time correction. We propose DARC, a diagnosis-guided recovery harness that profiles task-family failure modes, prunes mismatched interventions from a shared recovery library, and freezes a verifier-selected success-cost policy for deployment. This causal order makes correction selective: the harness first determines what kind of failure can be repaired, then decides how much recovery evidence to spend. In ALFWorld, AppWorld, and XBRL Finance, the same protocol yields an action-validity harness, a procedural-recovery fallback, and a format-precision retrieval policy; in each evaluated setting it improves average task performance over base agents and broad playbooks while reducing environment steps or retrieval budget. Our experiments show that failures need not trigger uniformly more context: DARC turns self-correction from prompt expansion into recovery-interface design. DARC provides a practical route toward more reliable agents in domains where compiler-like feedback is absent: making failures actionable before making contexts larger.

</details>


### [134] [Language-Conditional Dequantization: Recovering What Quantization Steals from Non-English Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11786)
*Nirmal Thomas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出语言条件反量化（LCD）方法，为已量化模型的多语言能力损伤提供后处理修复。该方法为每个语言添加秩为2的LoRA校正模块（每语言仅增加0.12%参数，单GPU训练不足20分钟），可恢复非拉丁文字语言70-83%的困惑度差距及17-28%的GlobalMMLU准确率差距，显著优于语言无关校正和数据无关低秩基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激进量化（如sub-4B INT3 GPTQ）对多语言能力造成不成比例的损害，非英语语言的困惑度退化比英语严重2-4倍。现有的量化后处理方法缺乏语言感知性，无法有效缓解这一语言间的不均衡损伤。

Method: 提出语言条件反量化（Language-Conditional Dequantization, LCD）：一种后处理方法，在已量化模型的线性层上附加按语言划分的秩-2 LoRA校正。每语言仅增加0.12%参数，单GPU上训练时间少于20分钟。同时提出层受限变体用于验证误差传播机制。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-3B和Llama-3.2-3B上，LCD恢复了非拉丁文字语言70-83%的困惑度差距和17-28%的GlobalMMLU准确率差距；相比等容量的语言无关校正，在类型学上遥远的语言上提升3-9个点；相比数据无关低秩基线（LQER）提升一个数量级。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了困惑度-准确率之间的脱节现象，并将其归因于量化损伤的位置差异：Llama模型的早期深度误差会向下游传播且难以局部修正，而Qwen模型的晚期深度误差则不会如此。层受限的LCD变体直接验证了这一机制，表明量化损伤的深度分布是设计多语言后处理修复策略的关键因素。

Abstract: Aggressive quantization disproportionately harms multilingual capability: in the sub-4B INT3 GPTQ regime, we measure 2-4x larger perplexity degradation on non-English languages than on English. We propose Language-Conditional Dequantization (LCD), a post-hoc method that attaches per-language rank-2 LoRA corrections to the linear layers of an already-quantized model, adding 0.12% parameters per language and training in under 20 minutes on a single GPU. Across Qwen2.5-3B and Llama-3.2-3B, LCD recovers 70-83% of the perplexity gap for non-Latin script languages and 17-28% of the GlobalMMLU accuracy gap, outperforming a language-agnostic correction of equal capacity by 3-9 points on typologically distant languages and a data-free low-rank baseline (LQER) by an order of magnitude. We further identify a perplexity-accuracy disconnect and trace it to where quantization concentrates damage: early-depth errors (Llama) propagate downstream and resist local correction, while late-depth errors (Qwen) do not. A layer-restricted variant of LCD validates this mechanism directly.

</details>


### [135] [GRPO for Financial Advice Generation: Outperforming Commercial LLMs under CATE Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11787)
*Ofir Ben Shoham,Shrutendra Harsola,Vignesh Subrahmaniam,Shravan Mohan,Yakov Gazman,Oded Vainas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将财务建议生成建模为强化学习问题，使用GRPO微调开源语言模型，以LLM-as-a-judge评分作为奖励并加入安全门控，同时引入独立于评判者的双重稳健CATE估计器进行因果审计，结果表明训练模型在估算毛利提升上约为最强商业基线的两倍，且因果审计捕捉到了评判者遗漏的信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从商业记录生成可执行的财务建议需要模型整合数值推理、领域知识和判断力，同时避免可能损害业务的建议。直接监督存在困难：历史决策不一定最优，高质量自由文本标签获取成本高昂。因此需要一种无需依赖昂贵人工标注的优化方法，并验证优化效果是否真正反映业务价值而非仅仅是适应评测器。

Method: 将财务建议生成形式化为强化学习问题，使用GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）微调开源权重语言模型。奖励函数采用LLM-as-a-judge评分标准，从多个二元维度评估建议质量，并辅以安全门控防止有害建议。为验证改进的真实业务价值，引入基于双重稳健条件平均处理效应（CATE）估计器的观测性离策略审计作为独立的评判维度。

Result: 训练后的LLM在估算毛利提升上约为最强商业基线的两倍（0.0228 vs 0.0104），同时具有最低的下行率和最小的负尾部风险。值得注意的是，两种评估对基线的排序不一致：未训练的基座模型在评判者评分中垫底，但在因果审计中排名第二，表明审计捕捉到了评判者未察觉的信号。

Conclusion: GRPO配合金融领域导向的奖励信号能够产生比商业LLM更有用的商业建议；独立于评判者的因果审计是LLM-as-a-judge评估的有价值补充，而非简单确认，在财务NLP中具有重要意义。

Abstract: Generating actionable financial advice from business records demands that models integrate numerical reasoning, domain knowledge, and sound judgment, while avoiding recommendations that could harm the business. Direct supervision is difficult: historical decisions are not necessarily optimal, and high-quality free-form labels are expensive to obtain. We formulate financial advice generation as a reinforcement learning problem and fine-tune an open-weight language model using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Our reward is an LLM-as-a-judge rubric that scores each recommendation across multiple binary dimensions of advice quality, augmented with a safety gate for harm prevention.
  Since LLM-based evaluation alone cannot confirm whether improvements reflect genuine business value rather than adaptation to the judge, we complement it with a judge-independent audit based on a standard doubly-robust Conditional Average Treatment Effect (CATE) estimator. Under this observational off-policy audit, our trained LLM achieves approximately twice the estimated gross-profit lift of the strongest evaluated commercial baseline ($0.0228$ vs.\ $0.0104$), together with the lowest downside rate and the least negative tail risk of any policy evaluated. Notably, the two evaluations do not rank the baselines identically: the untrained base model places last on the judge rubric but second on the causal audit, indicating that the audit captures a signal the judge does not. Our results demonstrate that GRPO with a finance-grounded reward signal can produce substantially more useful business recommendations than commercial LLMs, and that a judge-independent causal audit is a valuable complement to, rather than a confirmation of, LLM-as-a-judge assessment in financial NLP.

</details>


### [136] [TELLME: Test-Enhanced Learning for Language Model Enrichment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11788)
*Minjun Kim,Inho Won,Hyeonseok Lim,MinKyu Kim,Junghun Yuk,Wooyoung Go,Jongyoul Park,Jungyeul Park,KyungTae Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TELLME方法，将测试增强学习（TEL）原理与持续预训练（CPT）结合，通过训练过程中加入测验来提升大语言模型领域适配的效率。实验表明，TELLME在金融领域超越了现有方法最高达23.6%，并在长期记忆保持方面提升了9.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续预训练（CPT）虽被广泛用于大语言模型的领域适配，但面临两大挑战：难以获取大规模领域特定数据集，以及高昂的计算成本。作者旨在解决这些问题，提出一种更高效的领域适配方法。

Method: TELLME利用测试增强学习（TEL）原理，在训练过程中引入测验（quizzes）来提升模型的学习效率。该方法将TEL与CPT相融合，以促进高效的领域特定知识获取和长期记忆保持。

Result: 实验结果显示，TELLME在金融领域相较现有方法性能提升了最高23.6%，在长期记忆保持方面实现了9.8%的提升。

Conclusion: TELLME通过引入测验机制的持续预训练，有效缓解了传统CPT在数据获取困难和计算成本高方面的不足，验证了在领域适配中提升学习效率与记忆保持的可行性。

Abstract: Continual pre-training (CPT) has been widely adopted as a method for domain adaptation in large language models. However, CPT has consistently been accompanied by challenges, such as the difficulty of acquiring large-scale domain-specific datasets and high computational costs. In this study, we propose a novel method called Test-Enhanced Learning for Language Model Enrichment (TELLME) to alleviate these issues. TELLME leverages the TestEnhanced Learning (TEL) principle, whereby the model's training efficiency is improved using quizzes during training. It integrates this principle with CPT, thereby promoting efficient domain-specific knowledge acquisition and long-term memory retention. Experimental results demonstrate that TELLME outperforms existing methods by up to 23.6% in the financial domain and achieves a 9.8% improvement in long-term memory retention.

</details>


### [137] [Hybrid Gated Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11805)
*Zekun Zhou,Ruobing Xie,Lanrui Wang,Weixuan Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出HyGA框架，通过三种门控策略（利用注意力多个阶段的多样信息，构建逐元素/逐头门控）增强注意力表达能力，并引入低秩矩阵分解和学习型注意力汇聚点以提升训练效率与稳定性，在多个基准上超越Gated attention，取得更好的效率-性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 门控注意力是缓解注意力汇聚点（attention sinks）并增强注意力表达能力的一种有效方法，但现有方法在有效性与效率的帕累托边界（Pareto frontier）上仍有进一步提升空间，需要更全面的门控机制来捕捉多头交互信息。

Method: 提出混合门控注意力（HyGA）框架，包含三种类型的门控策略，分别利用注意力不同阶段的多样信息，协作构建逐元素级（element-wise）和逐头级（head-wise）门控，同时捕获头内（intra-head）与跨头（cross-head）信息交互；引入低秩矩阵分解与可学习的注意力汇聚点（learnable attention sink）提升训练效率与稳定性。

Result: 在多个广泛使用的基准和不同骨干网络上评估，HyGA相比Gated attention在训练损失和各类下游任务性能上均有全面提升；在不同计算成本下均取得最佳性能，并通过全面的模型分析提供了更深入的理解。

Conclusion: HyGA展示了更有效、更高效且更稳定的注意力机制，为注意力门控设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Gated attention is an effective approach to mitigate attention sinks and enhance the representational capacity of attention. To further extend its effectiveness-efficiency Pareto frontier, we propose a Hybrid Gated Attention (HyGA) framework that contains three types of gating strategies. Specifically, these gates leverage diverse information from multiple stages of attention, and collaboratively build element-wise/head-wise gating from multiple perspectives, capturing intra-head and cross-head information interactions. Through our hybrid gating components, HyGA could provide multi-source modulation signals, enabling more comprehensive control over information flow and improving the representational capacity of attention. We also introduce low-rank matrix decomposition and learnable attention sink to further enhance training efficiency and stability. In experiments, we evaluate HyGA on widely-used benchmarks based on different backbones. The experimental results show that our HyGA comprehensively improves both training loss and various downstream performances compared with Gated attention. HyGA has also been verified to achieve the best performance at different computation costs, with comprehensive model analyses for better understanding. The proposed HyGA sheds light on a more effective, efficient, and stable attention mechanism.

</details>


### [138] [Located but Not Releasable: Silent Gate Inversion and Bounded Linear Release](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11822)
*Xining Xun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文对"检测-定位-释放"（detect-localize-release）这一完整流程进行了完全预注册的压力测试，对象是一个25.7M规模的Transformer因果证据判别模型。结果显示：定位阶段的干预措施在中层观察证据通道上成功恢复了目标行为（配对释放优势0.563和0.854，97.5%置信区间排除零），但门控器在分布外完全失效（在需要释放的2400个生成样本上触发率为0，却在分布内核外样本上误触发6.9-7.3%），且线性释放存在上限（剂量反应曲线远低于预注册释放阈值）。两个失败可分离，且均不推翻定位的成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究表明语言模型中存在任务相关的潜在结构但模型行为上并未使用这些结构（即抑制现象）。作者希望回答一个更根本的尚未被端到端检验的问题：一旦定位到这类潜在结构，它是否真的能转化为可用的行为表现？这需要将"检测、定位、释放"三阶段完整管线置于严格的预注册压力测试之下。

Method: 作者在25.7M参数的Transformer模型上进行因果证据判别训练，复现已知的"潜在因果结构存在但行为未使用"的抑制现象。整个实验采用完全预注册设计：所有阈值、声明模板、决策树分支在任何数据产生之前被哈希归档。实验分为三阶段：定位（在中间层的观察证据通道施加干预以恢复目标行为）、门控（训练检测器在需要释放的分布外场景触发）、线性释放（移除门控，无条件注入线性方向并测量剂量反应）。所有数字均追溯到审计链中的哈希工件。

Result: 三个主要发现：(i) 定位成功——对中间层观察证据通道的干预在原本受抑制的世界中恢复了目标行为（配对释放优势分别为0.563和0.854，97.5%置信区间排除零，最佳位点释放率0.889）；(ii) 门控在分布外失败——检测器在零个分布外校准世界上触发，在留出的分布内生成样本上触发6.9-7.3%，但在实际需要它的2400个留出生成样本上触发率为零，形成完全反转，静默地将门控管线降级为基础模型；(iii) 线性释放受限——移除门控后无条件注入每实例线性方向产生单调剂量反应，但平台期远低于预注册释放阈值（截距从0.382→0.311→0.264，阈值≤0.08），每实例自适应增益不足±0.03。失败被双重定位：检测器在OOD层面完全反转，该位点全族线性释放方向远离充分性边界。两个失败可分离，均不推翻定位的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，即使潜在结构的定位完全成功，从结构到行为转化的完整管线仍可能在门控和释放两个独立环节崩溃：门控检测器在分布外发生"反向失效"（OOD-inverted），而线性释放方向族在该位点被系统性限制（bounded away from sufficiency）。两个失败点是可分离的，且不影响定位阶段的成功结论。该工作强调了全流程端到端验证的必要性——仅定位到潜在结构远远不够，检测与释放环节都可能成为静默的瓶颈。

Abstract: A growing body of work reports that language models represent task-relevant latent structure that they fail to use. Whether such structure, once located, can be converted into behavior is a separate question that is rarely tested end to end. We submit the complete pipeline -- detect, localize, and release -- to a fully preregistered stress test on a 25.7M transformer trained on causal-evidence discrimination, where a known suppression phenomenon (latent causal structure present but behaviorally unused) has previously been documented. Every threshold, claim template, and decision-tree branch was hashed and archived before any corresponding data existed. Three findings. (i) Localization succeeds: interventions at observation-evidence channels of mid layers restore target behavior on otherwise-suppressed worlds (paired release advantages $0.563$ and $0.854$, 97.5% CIs excluding zero; best-site release rate $0.889$). (ii) Gating fails out of distribution: a detector calibrated to trigger on zero out-of-distribution calibration worlds triggers on 6.9-7.3% of held-out in-distribution generations and on zero of the 2,400 held-out generations that actually need it -- a complete inversion that silently reduces the gated pipeline to its base model. (iii) Linear release is capped: removing the gate and injecting a per-instance linear direction unconditionally yields a monotone dose-response that plateaus far below the preregistered release margin (intercept $0.382 \to 0.311 \to 0.264$ vs. threshold $\le 0.08$); per-instance adaptivity adds less than $\pm 0.03$. The failure is doubly located: the detector is OOD-inverted, and the entire family of linear release directions at this site and resolution is bounded away from sufficiency. The two failures are dissociable, and neither overturns localization. Every number traces to a hashed artifact in the released audit chain.

</details>


### [139] [When the Knowledge Base Becomes the Gold Standard: Measuring Resource-Shared Evaluation Loops in Entity-Level Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11843)
*Jinhyung Bae,Dain Kil,Seongmin Oh,Seungmin Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个自指涉评估循环：研究者用与注入系统相同的知识库（KB）来评估人名翻译质量，导致得分反映的是指令遵从度而非翻译质量。通过使用国家韩国史研究所的专家标注作为独立金标准，作者发现仅31.1%的专家标注样本真正独立于注入管线，且重叠区间的注入正确率（97.8%）远高于独立区间（70.1%），证明"看起来最健康"的部分恰恰是循环在支撑的。四种模型的差分分析表明KB注入的收益仅限于与注入资源共享金标准的片段，独立区间的收益为零或负值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 承政院日记（Seungjeongwon Ilgi）仅有37.4%被翻译，人名误译会破坏史实。低资源历史领域缺乏专家金标准，实践者用与注入系统相同的知识库替代金标准进行评分，造成自指涉评估循环——指标衡量的是指令遵从度而非翻译质量。作者旨在量化这一循环的规模与影响。

Method: 使用韩国国家历史研究所的专家人名标注作为独立于注入管线的金标准，固定实体集合，仅改变正确读法的来源（注入管线内vs独立）。对527个专家标注提及进行分析，将样本分为重叠区间（金标准与注入管线共享资源）和独立区间。采用差分分析（difference-in-differences）比较四种模型在KB注入前后的表现，并通过移除构建过滤器构建独立样本进行复测验证。

Result: 527个专家标注提及中仅31.1%（约164个）真正独立于注入管线。重叠区间中，注入读法与人类翻译一致率高达97.8%，而独立区间仅为70.1%。四种模型的KB注入增益完全局限于重叠区间；独立区间增益为零或负。后注入保持率集中在0.910-0.996的窄带内，尽管基线能力相差五倍——即报告增益是先前表现的补数，较弱模型显得提升更大。独立样本测试显示指标在模型内可复现（区间重叠），在模型间可区分（区间不重叠）。

Conclusion: KB注入评估存在不可忽视的自指涉循环：不仅独立金标准占比较低（31.1%），且循环在模型层面系统性抬高了重叠区间的分数，造成"能力强"的假象。报告的性能增益本质上是KBsacrificial补数而非真实翻译质量的改善。作者呼吁评估低资源历史领域的实体翻译时，必须采用独立于注入管线的金标准，否则指标无法区分"遵从指令"与"正确翻译"。

Abstract: The Seungjeongwon Ilgi, a UNESCO Memory of the World record, is only 37.4% translated, and the most conspicuous failure mode in automatic translation is the person name -- a misread name corrupts the historical fact rather than merely the surface. Low-resource historical domains have no expert gold standard for entity translation, so practitioners substitute a knowledge base (KB) for the gold. That KB is the same resource injected into the system: scoring becomes self-referential and the metric measures instruction compliance rather than translation quality.
  We measure this loop. Using expert person-name annotations from the National Institute of Korean History as a gold independent of the injection pipeline, we hold the entity set fixed and vary only the provenance of the correct reading. Of 527 expert-annotated mentions, only 31.1% lie outside the injection pipeline, and the residual loop is not uniform -- in the overlapping segment the injected reading agrees with the human translation 97.8% of the time against 70.1% in the independent one, so the segment that looks healthiest is the one the loop is holding up.
  Across four models, a difference-in-differences analysis shows the gain from KB injection is confined to the segment whose gold shares the injected resource; in the independent segment it is at or below zero. Post-injection preservation clusters in a narrow 0.910-0.996 band even though baseline capability differs fivefold, so the reported gain is the complement of prior performance and weaker models appear to improve more dramatically. On an independent sample built by removing the construction filter, the measure replicates within model (overlapping intervals) while discriminating between models (non-overlapping intervals) -- it reflects a property of the model, not of the sample.

</details>


### [140] [Total Recall at What Cost? Benchmarking the Serving Cost of Agentic Memory Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11879)
*Natchanon Pollertlam,Witchayut Kornsuwannawit*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统化地对三种长期对话代理的内存系统（Mem0、Hindsight、Mastra观测记忆）进行了服务成本基准测试，与两种参考策略（固定大小滚动窗口、重发完整对话记录）对比，使用两个骨干模型和最长400轮的对话，并对665个LoCoMo问题的答案准确性进行了配对评估。研究发现：内存系统的服务成本无法仅凭对话长度和消息大小预测（回归偏差18-69%）；盈亏平衡分析显示内存系统何时比完整记录更便宜高度依赖于系统和骨干模型（从最初几十轮到400轮内从未实现）；没有系统在成本和准确性两个维度上同时胜出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期运行的对话代理越来越依赖内存系统来避免每轮都重发整个对话，但内存系统的服务成本长期以来缺乏系统性的基准测试，导致从业者难以做出合理的架构选择。

Method: 作者对三种内存系统（Mem0、Hindsight、Mastra观测内存）与两种参考策略（固定大小滚动窗口和重发完整记录）进行了对比实验，覆盖两个骨干模型和最长400轮的对话，每项成本测量都与665个LoCoMo问题的答案准确性配对，并进行了回归分析和盈亏平衡分析。

Result: 研究发现三点：一是内存系统的服务成本无法仅由对话长度和消息大小预测，内部内存行为是主要成本驱动因素（回归偏差18-69%）；二是内存系统是否及何时比完整记录更便宜高度依赖系统类型和骨干模型，从最初几十轮到400轮内从未实现不等；三是没有任何系统在成本和准确性上同时占优，准确性范围在21%-54%，骨干模型的选择对成本的影响与内存系统本身相当。

Conclusion: 内存系统的服务成本与准确性之间存在复杂的权衡关系，不能简单用对话规模预测成本，需要针对具体系统、骨干模型和应用场景进行逐案例的成本-性能分析。

Abstract: Long-running conversational agents increasingly rely on a memory system to avoid resending the whole conversation each turn, yet how much that costs to serve has received little systematic benchmarking. We compare three memory systems (Mem0, Hindsight, and Mastra Observational Memory) against two reference strategies -- a fixed-size rolling window and resubmitting the full transcript -- across two backbones and conversations of up to 400 turns, pairing every cost measurement with answer accuracy on 665 LoCoMo questions. First, a memory system's serving cost cannot be predicted from conversation length and message size alone: a regression that tracks the two reference strategies closely misses the memory systems by 18-69%, their cost driven instead by internal memory behavior. Second, a break-even analysis shows that whether -- and when -- a memory system becomes cheaper to serve than the full transcript is highly sensitive to the system and the backbone, from the first tens of turns for the cheapest to never within 400 turns for the most expensive. Third, no system wins on both axes: accuracy spans 21-54%, and the backbone choice drives cost as much as the memory system does.

</details>


### [141] [LazyTrain: Limited-resource Allocation toward Zero-waste Yield Optimization in Large Language Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11919)
*Xiaojun Wu,Cehao Yang,Honghao Liu,Xueyuan Lin,Xuhui Jiang,Chengjin Xu,Jia Li,Jian Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LazyTrain是一个构建在层流式执行器之上的优化层，将检查点选择、激活放置、重计算和CPU-GPU-NVMe通信重叠统一建模为混合整数调度问题，并引入混合8-bit算子（8-bit优化器状态结合快速梯度裁剪），在H800和RTX 3090等有限硬件上实现约1.24倍的持续TFLOPS提升，并在Qwen3.6-27B上达到219.95 TFLOPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限硬件上训练大语言模型越来越受限于GPU算力、主机内存、PCIe传输和存储带宽之间的调度瓶颈。现有卸载系统（如MegaTrain）虽能在单GPU上训练大模型，但固定检查点策略和放置启发式仍使通信暴露在关键路径上，导致训练效率低下。

Method: LazyTrain将检查点选择、激活放置、重计算和CPU-GPU-NVMe通信重叠形式化为混合整数调度问题（MILP），在执行训练前求解最优策略并执行。同时提出混合8-bit算子：将8-bit优化器状态与快速梯度裁剪耦合为一个单一操作，状态压缩减少优化器内存占用，快速裁剪抵消CPU侧更新开销。

Result: 在H800上从Qwen2.5-3B到Qwen3.6-27B的实验显示，LazyTrain相比匹配基线持续TFLOPS提升约1.24倍；RTX 3090实验在每个模型规模上最大可行batch size增加1。主要Qwen3.6-27B H800 MetaMathQA运行中达到219.95 TFLOPS和1361 tokens/s（batch size 72），峰值GPU内存68.84GB，完整评估集上精确匹配准确率95.42%。

Conclusion: LazyTrain通过将调度决策形式化为可求解的混合整数规划问题并结合混合8-bit算子，有效消除了关键路径上的通信暴露，在有限硬件条件下显著提升了大模型训练的吞吐与可行性，验证了统一建模调度优化的有效性与硬件约束下训练的实用性。

Abstract: Training large language models on limited hardware is increasingly a scheduling problem across GPU compute, host memory, PCIe transfer, and storage bandwidth. Existing offloading systems reduce GPU residency, and MegaTrain shows that a CPU-master layer-streaming executor can train large models on a single GPU, but fixed checkpointing and placement heuristics still leave communication exposed on the critical path. We propose LazyTrain, an optimization layer over a layer-streaming executor. LazyTrain formulates checkpoint selection, activation placement, recomputation, and CPU-GPU-NVMe communication overlap as a mixed-integer scheduling problem, then executes the solved policy during training. It further couples 8-bit optimizer states with fast gradient clipping as a single Hybrid 8-bit operator: state compression reduces optimizer-state memory, while fast clipping counteracts the additional CPU-side update overhead. Across H800 experiments from Qwen2.5-3B to Qwen3.6-27B, LazyTrain improves sustained TFLOPS over matched baselines runs by approximately 1.24$\times$; RTX 3090 experiments likewise increase the maximum feasible batch size by one at each model scale. In the primary Qwen3.6-27B H800 MetaMathQA run, LazyTrain reaches 219.95 TFLOPS and 1361 tokens/s at batch size 72, peaks at 68.84\,GB of GPU memory, and obtains 95.42\% exact-match accuracy on the full evaluation split. The source code is available at https://github.com/DataArcTech/LazyTrain.

</details>


### [142] [LODESTAR: Trustworthy Entropy Is Navigated, Not Merely Measured -- Reinforced Polarizer Keeps a Frozen LLM from Being Confidently Misled by the Wrong Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11922)
*Po-Jen Ko,Che-Cheng Wu,Hung-Chun Hsu,Li-Yang Chang,Chuan-Ju Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LODESTAR，一种通过强化学习训练"极化器"（polarizer）——一段插入到冻结响应LLM提示中的固定自然语言字符串——来干预文本上下文，从而在检索增强问答中选择最佳候选答案的方法。在5,008个问题上，LODESTAR的平均F1达到0.5339，优于所有推理就绪的选择器（包括最低熵规则0.5148），并在70个方法-数据集组合中全部取胜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于熵的选择规则（如最低答案token熵）在检索增强问答中虽有效，但存在关键缺陷：误导性段落会使响应LLM自信地错误（熵降低），恰好在信号看似最可信处失效。作者意识到失效根源在于响应LLM读取的段落上下文，而该上下文是可干预的输入，从而激发了对文本干预进行打分的新思路。

Method: LODESTAR使用强化学习离线训练一个"极化器"——插入到冻结响应LLM提示中的短固定自然语言字符串（不修改权重）。训练标签由金答案和两个LLM评委离线构建，推理时不读取金答案和评委。其核心思想是：通过测量第三方冻结响应LLM在某个问题的候选答案间被干预文本所诱导出的不确定性来打分并选择候选答案。

Result: 在5,008个问题上，LODESTAR达到最高平均F1（0.5339，对比最低熵规则的0.5148）、最高精确匹配（0.4136）和最高GPT-4o评委得分（0.6435）。三种子均值在70个方法-数据集F1单元中全部击败14个已发表配置，且各项对比均达到配对显著性。域内与域外均有效，消融实验显示极化器使误导性段落的读取率从30.3%降至26.0%。

Conclusion: LODESTAR证明了通过文本干预（而非权重修改）来引导冻结LLM的读取行为是可行且有效的检索增强问答选择策略，尤其解决了最低熵规则在误导性段落面前失败的根本问题。该方法无需金答案和LLM评委即可在推理时运行，具有良好的泛化能力（域内域外均有效）。

Abstract: Predictive-distribution entropy makes a strong selection rule in retrieval-augmented question answering: across five QA benchmarks, keeping the candidate answer that a frozen respondent LLM produces with the lowest answer-token entropy lifts mean answer $F_1$ from 0.4769 to 0.5148 over the retriever's top-ranked passage, with no gold answers. Yet this lowest-entropy rule, which prior entropy-based selectors adopt, fails in a specific and consequential way: a misleading passage makes the respondent confidently wrong, driving its entropy down precisely where the signal looks most trustworthy. We show that the failure comes from the passage the respondent reads -- and the context that passage is read in is an input we can intervene on. We introduce LODESTAR, to our knowledge the first method to score a text intervention by the uncertainty it induces in a third-party frozen respondent, compared across one question's candidates. LODESTAR uses reinforcement learning to train, once and offline, a polarizer -- a short fixed natural-language string inserted into the respondent's prompt and never into its weights; its training labels are built offline from gold answers and two LLM judges, and inference reads neither. Evaluating every competing selector under the same frozen respondent and the same candidate pools on 5,008 questions, LODESTAR attains the highest mean $F_1$ of any inference-ready selector (0.5148 to 0.5339), the highest exact match (0.4136), and the highest GPT-4o judge score of the frozen-respondent configurations judged (0.6435); its three-seed mean wins all 70 method-by-dataset $F_1$ cells against fourteen published configurations while remaining paired-significant against every one. The gain holds both in-domain and out-of-domain, and ablating the polarizer shows it is what makes the respondent read a misleading passage less often (26.0% against 30.3%).

</details>


### [143] [Spark-to-Paper: End-to-End Research Paper Generation as a Composable Skill](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11924)
*Zhuoyang Qian,Biao Wu,Yiran Wang,Chris D Yan,Desan Dai,Liangwei Zheng,Jin Jiang,Junsheng Zhang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Spark-to-Paper，一个在现有编码助手中实现的端到端研究论文生成系统，包含13个可组合技能，将模型判断与确定性操作分离，实现从文献检索、实验设计到论文写作的完整流程，在八个受控研究主题上实现99.5%引用有效性和96.4%图表可编辑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将研究想法转化为完整论文需要的不只是文本生成，还需要文献检索、实验设计与执行、依据证据修正论断、生成可发表图表以及在整个长生成过程中保持一致性。现有系统通常依赖独立的智能体平台或编排服务，缺乏轻量级的端到端解决方案。

Method: Spark-to-Paper在现有编码助手中实现为十三个可组合技能，无需独立的智能体平台。系统核心设计包括：(1) 将基于模型的判断与可直接执行和检查的确定性操作分离；(2) 将实验规划与报告分离，在观察结果之前明确所需证据，并根据测量结果修订稿件论断；(3) 结合确定性完整性检查与自我批判，并通过界定Self-Refutation Loop这一失败模式提升长研究轨迹的可靠性；(4) 通过程序化绘图生成可编辑的矢量图，通过基于代码的重建生成方法示意图。

Result: 在八个受控研究主题上，系统实现99.5%引用有效性、96.4%图表可编辑性。受控消融实验将造假检测率从单次草稿的14%提升到完整完整性及审查栈的92%，对抗性审查达到74%精确率。完整系统使用1190万tokens，每篇论文成本8.1美元，平均耗时3.2小时。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，端到端研究论文生成可以在现有编码助手中以轻量级、可组合的工作流方式实现，同时保持实验证据在论断被接受、修订或放弃过程中占据核心地位。

Abstract: Turning a research idea into a complete paper requires more than text generation: the system must retrieve literature, design and execute experiments, revise claims according to evidence, produce publication-ready figures, and maintain consistency across a long generation process. We present Spark-to-Paper, an end-to-end research paper generation system implemented as thirteen composable skills inside an existing coding assistant, without requiring a separate agent platform or orchestration service. Spark-to-Paper separates model-based judgment from deterministic operations that can be directly executed and checked. It further separates experiment planning from reporting, so that required evidence is specified before results are observed and manuscript claims are revised according to measured outcomes. To improve reliability over long research trajectories, the system combines deterministic integrity checks with self-critique and bounds a failure mode we call the Self-Refutation Loop, in which repeated experiments continue to reject the original research objective. Spark-to-Paper also produces editable vector figures through programmatic plotting for experimental results and code-based reconstruction for generated method diagrams. Across eight controlled research topics, Spark-to-Paper achieves 99.5% citation validity and 96.4% figure editability. A controlled ablation increases fabrication detection from 14% for a single-pass draft to 92% with the full integrity and review stack, while adversarial review achieves 74% precision. The full system uses 11.9M tokens, costs $8.1 per manuscript, and requires 3.2 hours on average. These results show that end-to-end research paper generation can be implemented as a lightweight, composable workflow inside existing coding assistants while keeping experimental evidence central to how claims are accepted, revised, or abandoned.

</details>


### [144] [Accuracy and Order Sensitivity Diverge Under Label-Free Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11947)
*Karl Hanna,Chen Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型在多项选择题中选项顺序偏差的问题。作者测试了两种避免模型看到选项标签的策略：生成后匹配和独立打分，但两者均未可靠提升准确率。分解分析表明瓶颈在于隐藏选项本身而非匹配步骤。唯一与基线一致的是展示所有选项并由LLM匹配器的配置，但消除位置影响仍不带来稳定增益，而循环排列反而常能提升性能。此外，两种衡量去偏效果的指标均未能可靠展示去偏效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多项选择题基准广泛用于评估LLM，但MCQ分数混淆了知识与对选项顺序的敏感性，使其不能可靠衡量模型知识。作者希望检验在模型作答时隐藏选项标签是否能消除位置影响并提升性能。

Method: 作者评估两种去偏策略：(1) 生成后匹配策略（generation-then-matching），即模型先生成答案再与选项匹配；(2) 独立打分策略，选项单独评分，构造上天然无位置偏差。同时进行完整分解以定位瓶颈，并采用两种指标（聚合的回忆不平衡度量和逐题顺序敏感性度量）评估两阶段提示的去偏效果。

Result: 两种去偏策略均未可靠提升准确率。分解显示瓶颈在隐藏选项而非匹配步骤。唯一与基线一致的配置是展示所有选项并由LLM匹配器。完全消除位置影响不会可靠带来准确率提升，而循环排列反而常改善表现。两种去偏评估指标均未显示可靠去偏效果。

Conclusion: 隐藏选项标签并不能有效改善LLM在MCQ任务上的表现，消除位置偏差并不直接转化为准确率提升；相反，循环排列选项在某些情况下反而有益。这挑战了通过隐藏选项来消除位置偏差即能提升模型知识评估可靠性的假设。

Abstract: Multiple-choice benchmarks are widely used to evaluate large language models, but MCQ scores conflate knowledge with sensitivity to option order, which makes them unreliable measures of model knowledge. In this paper, we test whether preventing a model from seeing option labels while committing to an answer removes positional influence and, in turn, improves performance. We evaluate two different strategies for mitigating bias. The first uses a generation-then-matching approach, and the second scores options in isolation, which is positionally unbiased by construction. Neither reliably improves accuracy. A complete decomposition shows that the bottleneck is withholding options, not the matching step. The only configuration that consistently matches the baseline is the one that shows the model all options paired with an LLM matcher. However, eliminating positional influence entirely still does not reliably yield accuracy gains, while cyclic permutation often improves them. For two-stage prompting, an aggregate measure of recall imbalance and a direct per-question measure of order sensitivity both fail to show reliable debiasing.

</details>


### [145] [Benchmarking Trustworthiness of SLMs: Pre-trained vs. Compressed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11981)
*Haokun Lin,Kaijie Zhu,Haobo Xu,Yichen Wu,Zhichao Lu,Qingfu Zhang,Zhenan Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了小型语言模型（SLMs）在公平性、鲁棒性、隐私和伦理等多个维度的可信度，发现量化比剪枝更能保持可信度，且通过量化压缩可靠的大模型比从头训练小模型能获得更可信、适应性更强的SLMs，知识蒸馏可进一步增强其可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SLMs因其高效性在资源受限场景中具有巨大潜力，但随着语言模型在真实应用中日益普及，可信度已成为关键问题。然而，如何构建可信的SLMs仍是一个未被充分探索的问题，现有研究缺乏对SLM可信度的系统评估。

Method: 作者从多个维度（公平性、鲁棒性、隐私、伦理）对SLMs进行综合评估；对比了两种构建SLM的路径（从头训练 vs 压缩大模型），并分别考察剪枝和量化两种压缩方法对可信度的影响；进一步研究了可靠教师模型的知识蒸馏对SLM可信度的增强作用。

Result: 研究发现：量化在保持可信度方面显著优于剪枝；通过量化压缩可靠的大模型比从头训练小模型能获得更优的可信度和适应性；从可信教师模型进行知识蒸馏可以进一步提升SLMs的可靠性。

Conclusion: 该研究为构建和部署可信的小型语言模型提供了实证指导和理论基础，表明压缩策略的选择（量化优于剪枝）以及教师模型的可靠性对最终SLM的可信度有重要影响。

Abstract: Small Language Models (SLMs) have emerged as a more efficient alternative to traditional Large Language Models (LLMs), offering promising potential in resource-constrained scenarios. Existing approaches to building SLMs typically follow two paths: training compact models from scratch, or compressing larger pre-trained models using methods such as pruning, quantization, or distillation. As language models become increasingly integrated into real-world applications, ensuring their trustworthiness has become a critical concern. However, how to build trustworthy SLMs remains an underexplored question. In this work, we present a comprehensive evaluation of SLM trustworthiness across multiple dimensions, including fairness, robustness, privacy, and ethics. We first examine the effects of pruning and quantization, and find that quantization is significantly more effective in preserving trustworthiness compared to pruning. More importantly, we demonstrate that compressing a reliable large model via quantization can produce SLMs with superior trustworthiness and adaptability compared to using small models trained from scratch. Furthermore, knowledge distillation from trustworthy teacher models can further enhance the reliability of SLMs. We hope our findings provide practical guidance and a foundation for future research into the development and deployment of trustworthy small language models.

</details>


### [146] [Asymptotic Risk Calibration for Selective Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12008)
*Shufan Lin,Sijin Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出A-CRC-QA，一种后处理校准框架，用于不确定性感知的选择性问答。该框架将选择条件误差控制重新表述为线性期望约束，应用基于单调化经验风险校准的程序（受共形风险控制启发），实现对问答系统中接受答案的错误率的（渐近）统计控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型可能生成流畅但错误的答案，不确定性量化对可靠问答至关重要。但启发式不确定性分数无法完美区分正确与错误的预测，且直接应用固定不确定性阈值无法对接受答案的错误率提供统计控制。

Method: A-CRC-QA将选择条件误差控制重构为线性期望约束，并应用受共形风险控制启发的单调化经验风险校准程序。该方法模型无关、无需额外训练，可与不同不确定性估计器结合，针对实例级损失关于接受阈值的非单调性，目标是渐近而非有限样本风险控制。

Result: 在CoQA和MedMCQA上的实验表明，该方法适用于开放式和封闭式问答任务，相较于未校准和基于置信度边界的基线，在接受答案可靠性与答案保留率之间实现了更好的权衡。

Conclusion: A-CRC-QA为不确定性感知的选择性问答提供了一种有效的后处理校准方案，在保证答案可靠性统计控制的同时，兼顾了答案保留率，具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) may generate fluent but incorrect answers, making uncertainty quantification important for reliable question answering. However, heuristic uncertainty scores cannot perfectly distinguish correct predictions from incorrect ones, and directly applying a fixed uncertainty threshold provides no statistical control over the error rate among accepted answers. To address this limitation, we propose A-CRC-QA, a post-hoc calibration framework for uncertainty-aware selective question answering. The proposed method reformulates selection-conditioned error control as a linear expectation constraint and applies a monotonized empirical-risk calibration procedure inspired by conformal risk control. Since the resulting instance-wise loss is generally non-monotone with respect to the acceptance threshold, our framework targets asymptotic rather than finite-sample risk control. A-CRC-QA is model-agnostic, requires no additional training, and can be combined with different uncertainty estimators. Experiments on CoQA and MedMCQA demonstrate its applicability to both open-ended and closed-ended question answering, achieving a favorable trade-off between accepted-answer reliability and answer retention compared with uncalibrated and confidence-bound-based baselines.

</details>


### [147] [Poly-Dialectal Neural Machine Translation System for Bangla Regional Dialects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12018)
*Rakib Ullah,Ruhul Islam Rahul,Tanbir Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的孟加拉语多方言神经机器翻译系统，能够在12种孟加拉方言之间进行多向翻译，无需经标准语言中转。作者构建了迄今最大的孟加拉多方言平行语料库（51,531对平行句，含2,500对专家验证的双向平行句），并通过DoRA微调BanglaT5模型达到了SOTA翻译性能（29.26 BLEU，57.26 chrF++），超越了NLLB-200和mBART-50。最后部署了INT8量化模型作为开放网络应用，数据集已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉语拥有超过2.4亿使用者，存在显著的区域方言变体，与标准口语孟加拉语（SCB）在音系、形态和词汇上差异巨大。然而现代NMT架构和LLM普遍假设语言分布同质，导致在翻译低资源区域方言时性能严重退化，急需一个面向多方言的统一翻译系统。

Method: 1）构建迄今最大的孟加拉多方言平行语料库，包含12种方言共51,531对非空平行句，其中2,500对为5种此前未涉足方言的专家验证双向平行句；2）采用序列到序列架构，使用Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation (DoRA)对BanglaT5模型进行高效微调，实现12种方言间的多向翻译（不经标准语作为中间枢纽）；3）进行了跨方言迁移分析和数据集规模扩展研究，确立低资源方言适应的经验阈值；4）将最优模型量化为INT8并部署为开放网络应用。

Result: 微调后的BanglaT5模型在12种孟加拉方言上取得SOTA翻译性能：BLEU 29.26，chrF++ 57.26，超越NLLB-200（615M）和mBART-50（611M），同时保持了形态连贯性。系统还建立了跨方言迁移和数据集规模方面的经验阈值。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了在资源匮乏的多方言场景下，结合DoRA微调策略的BanglaT5能够有效实现多方言聚合翻译，无需标准语言中转。通过开源数据集和部署量化模型为边缘化方言社群提供数字包容性，为该领域的后续研究提供了数据基础和实证参考。

Abstract: Regional dialectal variation poses a fundamental challenge to natural language processing (NLP) in Bangla, where over 240 million speakers communicate across diverse regional variants that diverge significantly from Standard Colloquial Bangla (SCB) in phonology, morphology, and lexicon. Contemporary neural machine trans- lation (NMT) architectures and large language models (LLMs) predominantly as- sume a homogeneous language distribution, resulting in severe performance degra- dation when translating low-resource regional dialects. In this work, we present a unified Poly-Dialectal Neural Machine Translation System capable of multi-directional translation across 12 Bangla regional dialects without routing through an inter- mediary standard pivot. We compile the largest multi-dialect parallel corpus for Bangla to date, comprising 51,531 non-null parallel sentence pairs across 12 di- alects, incorporating 2,500 expert-verified, bidirectional parallel sentence pairs for five previously unaddressed dialects. Evaluating sequence-to-sequence architec- tures under Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation (DoRA), our fine-tuned BanglaT5 model achieves state-of-the-art translation performance (29.26 BLEU, 57.26 chrF++), outperforming NLLB-200 (615M) and mBART-50 (611M) while preserving morphological coherence. Furthermore, we conduct a systematic cross- dialectal transfer analysis and dataset scaling study, establishing empirical thresh- olds for low-resource dialect adaptation. Finally, we deploy the optimized INT8- quantized model as an open-access web application to promote digital inclusion for marginalized dialect communities. The complete dataset is publicly available at Mendeley Data (https://data.mendeley.com/datasets/v9cf66fk2t/2).

</details>


### [148] [Preference Tree Optimization: Enhancing Goal-Oriented Dialogue with Look-Ahead Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12062)
*Lior Baruch,Moshe Butman,Kfir Bar,Doron Friedman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出偏好树优化（PTO）框架，通过结合前瞻性偏好树数据生成方法和直接偏好优化（DPO），实现面向目标的多轮对话系统代理模型的迭代改进，并在动机性访谈（MI）领域通过虚拟患者和预言器评估器验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多轮、面向目标的对话系统中，尤其是在数据稀缺的专业领域，开发有效的对话系统仍面临重大挑战。现有方法难以在有限数据条件下提升代理模型的决策能力，需要一种能够利用偏好数据迭代优化模型的框架来解决数据稀缺问题。

Method: 提出PTO框架，核心包含两个部分：(1) 偏好树与前瞻（Preference Tree with Look-Ahead）方法，利用虚拟患者和预言器评估器模拟对话生成丰富的偏好数据集；(2) 结合直接偏好优化（DPO）算法，在迭代训练周期中增强代理的决策能力。

Result: 实验表明，PTO框架显著提升了对话代理在动机性访谈领域的目标导向对话性能。训练模型在会话满意度和工作联盟等关键指标上持续优于基线；引入前瞻模拟改善了长期规划和对话策略的有效性，更深的前瞻配置产生最稳定且高分的表现。

Conclusion: PTO框架有效解决了面向目标对话系统中数据稀缺的挑战，通过偏好数据生成与DPO的结合，能够迭代提升代理模型在专业领域（如动机性访谈）中的对话决策能力，为开发更细致有效的目标导向对话系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: Developing dialogue systems capable of engaging in multi-turn, goal-oriented conversations remains a significant challenge, especially in specialized domains with limited data. This research proposes a novel framework called Preference Tree Optimization (PTO), designed to iteratively improve agent models in such dialogue systems, by generating preference data using a method called Preference Tree with Look-Ahead. Focusing on Motivational Interviewing (MI) -- a counseling technique aimed at facilitating behavioral change -- we leverage virtual patients and an oracle evaluator to simulate conversations and generate rich preference datasets. By combining this method with Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), we aim to enhance the agent's decision-making capabilities over iterative training cycles. The proposed framework addresses data scarcity and advances the development of more nuanced and effective dialogue systems in goal-oriented domains. Experimental evaluations demonstrate that the PTO framework enhances dialogue agents' performance in goal-oriented conversations within the domain of Motivational Interviewing (MI). Models trained with PTO consistently outperformed the baseline in key metrics such as session satisfaction and working alliance. Additionally, incorporating look-ahead simulations led to improved long-term planning and more effective conversational strategies, with deeper look-ahead configurations yielding the most stable and high-scoring results.

</details>


### [149] [Structuring the Space of Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12113)
*Agnese Daffara,Sebastian Padó,Tanise Ceron*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了NLP领域中"视角"（perspective）的相关概念，定义了一组区分这些概念的属性，提出了一个沿单一轴向线性组织的概念层级，并展示了该层级如何帮助研究者根据研究目标选择合适的视角操作化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLP多个领域（从文本分析到算法优化）都涉及"视角"这一概念，但用于捕捉文本视角的操作性概念（如立场、情感、框架、论点等）之间的关系尚不明确，缺乏理论性的理解会阻碍相关研究的深入发展。

Method: 作者通过综述NLP中视角研究的全貌，定义了一组有助于区分不同视角相关概念的属性，并基于这些属性提出了一个沿单一轴线性组织的概念层级体系。

Result: 提出了一个能够将立场、情感、框架、论点等视角相关概念沿单一轴线性排序的层级结构，并论证了该层级可以指导研究者选择合适的视角操作化方法。

Conclusion: 这一原则性的概念层级为NLP视角研究提供了理论基础，帮助研究者在纷繁复杂的相关概念中找到清晰的定位，从而更有效地开展研究。

Abstract: The same event can be reported from different perspectives depending on the experiences, background, and beliefs of the writer or speaker. A variety of NLP areas engage with perspectives, spanning from text analysis to algorithm optimization. A wide range of operative concepts (such as stances, sentiment, frames, and arguments) has been used to capture perspectives in texts, however the precise relationships among those concepts remain unclear. Arguably, a deeper theoretical understanding of these concepts would empower more effective research on perspectives. In this paper, we address this gap by reviewing the space of perspectives in NLP and defining a set of properties that help distinguishing perspective-related concepts. Our analysis leads us to posit a hierarchy which organizes these concepts linearly along a single axis. Finally, we show how this principled conceptual hierarchy can help researchers navigate the field and select operationalizations of perspective that align with their specific research objectives.

</details>


### [150] [QV-PIC: Query-Aware Visual Position-Independent Caching for Efficient RAG Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12121)
*Yilin Liu,Rui Meng,Wangze Ni,Jianxin Yan,Heng Cao,Libin Zheng,Peng Cheng,Jinfei Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出QV-PIC，一种查询感知的双分辨率PIC（位置无关缓存）复用框架，通过模型原生模板离线编译视觉缓存、在线利用累积查询相关性分数在低分辨率全局上下文与高分辨率细粒度文本证据之间权衡，在保持视觉压缩效率的同时显著提升RAG中渲染图像PIC的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG中重复的文本块前缀填充导致冗余计算。位置无关缓存（PIC）通过跨位置复用KV对缓解这一问题，但效率受文本token数量限制；将文本渲染为图像可压缩token数，但渲染图像的PIC相比文本PIC存在更严重的质量退化，根源在于独立编译缓存间的上下文不匹配以及视觉压缩导致的细粒度文本证据丢失。现有PIC修复方法主要针对前者做选择性重算，但带来在线计算开销且无法恢复丢失的文本细节。

Method: QV-PIC采用查询感知的双分辨率PIC复用框架：(1) 离线阶段，在模型原生chat-template前缀下编译视觉缓存（而非独立编译），从而在不引入在线重算的情况下提升PIC质量；(2) 在线阶段，以低分辨率保留全局上下文，并通过累积查询相关性分数在高分辨率预算内恢复细粒度文本证据，兼顾视觉压缩带来的效率优势与文本细节恢复。

Result: 在六个任务上，QV-PIC相较原始渲染图像PIC平均F1提升21.6分，接近文本PIC水平，并超越优化后的文本PIC 2.58个F1点，同时TTFT降低17.2%；相对全量预填充，TTFT降低83.8%。

Conclusion: QV-PIC证明了通过查询感知的双分辨率策略结合模型原生模板离线编译，可以有效弥合渲染图像PIC与文本PIC之间的质量差距，同时保持视觉压缩带来的显著推理效率优势，为RAG中的高效KV复用提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) repeatedly prefills identical text chunks across queries, incurring redundant computations. Position-Independent Caching (PIC) mitigates it by reusing precomputed Key-Value (KV) across positions, but its efficiency is constrained by the large volume of text tokens. Rendering text chunks as images can compress the text into fewer visual tokens, but the rendered-image PIC suffers more severe quality degradation than the text PIC. This representation-specific gap primarily arises from contextual mismatches across independently compiled caches and the loss of fine-grained textual evidence during visual compression. Existing PIC repair methods mainly address the former through selective recomputation, but they incur online computation and cannot recover lost textual details. We propose QV-PIC, a query-aware dual-resolution PIC reuse framework guided by model-native templates. Offline, QV-PIC compiles visual caches under the model's native chat-template prefix, improving PIC quality without online recomputation. Online, it preserves global context with low resolution and restores fine-grained textual evidence within a high-resolution budget by cumulative query relevance scores, retaining the efficiency benefit of visual compression. Across six tasks, QV-PIC improves average F1 by 21.6 points over vanilla rendered-image PIC, closes the gap to vanilla text PIC, and surpasses optimized text PIC by 2.58 F1 while reducing TTFT by 17.2\%. Relative to full prefill, it cuts TTFT by 83.8%.

</details>


### [151] [SAG: SQL-Retrieval Augmented Generation with Query-Time Dynamic Hyperedges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12129)
*Yuchao Wu,Junqin Li,XingCheng Liang,Yongjie Chen,Yinghao Liang,Linyuan Mo,Guanxian Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SAG（SQL检索增强生成）提出了一种结构化检索架构，将文档组织为"事件-实体"索引而非构建全局知识图谱，每个文本块以"事件+实体对"形式表示，形成保留n元关系的潜在超边。查询时将共享实体作为连接键动态构建查询范围的邻居事件，全程保留原始文本块作为证据。在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA和MuSiQue上均取得最优检索与端到端QA性能，且在推理链越复杂的场景优势越明显（MuSiQue上Recall@5达80.36%，超最强基线11.52个点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流稠密检索在处理结构化约束和多跳推理方面存在天然局限；基于图的检索方法虽可解决此问题，但需要离线构建知识图谱，存在语义碎片化、维护成本高、增量更新复杂等缺陷。作者旨在设计一种无需全局知识图谱、同时能保留n元关系语义且易于增量更新的结构化检索架构。

Method: SAG将文档组织成事件-实体索引：每个文本块表示为一个语义完整的事件及其关联实体，构成保留n元关系的潜在超边，避免将关系分解为三元组造成语义损失。查询时以共享实体作为连接键（join key），动态连接相关文本块，形成查询作用域内的事件邻居集合。关键优势在于整个检索过程中每个证据始终保留原始chunk形式，不进行语义改写或分解。

Result: 在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA和MuSiQue三个基准上，SAG在检索和端到端QA任务上均取得最优性能。随着推理链复杂度增加，SAG的优势愈发明显。在最具挑战性的MuSiQue上达到80.36%的Recall@5，比最强基线高出11.52个百分点。

Conclusion: SAG为LLM智能体在持续增长的组织化知识上进行检索和推理提供了一种新的知识基础设施思路，在无需构建和维护全局知识图谱的前提下实现了高效的结构化多跳检索。

Abstract: While retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has proven effective at giving LLMs access to external knowledge, mainstream dense-retrieval implementations remain inherently limited in handling structured constraints and multi-hop reasoning. Graph-based methods address this by constructing knowledge graphs offline, but they often fragment semantics, incur high maintenance, and complicate incremental updates. We propose SAG (SQL-Retrieval Augmented Generation), a structured retrieval architecture that organizes documents into an event-entity index without building a global knowledge graph. SAG represents each chunk as a semantically complete event paired with its entities, forming a latent hyperedge that preserves n-ary relations without decomposing them into triples. At query time, SAG treats shared entities as join keys to connect related chunks. This dynamically yields a query-scoped neighborhood of events, and yet every piece of evidence remains the original chunk throughout. Experiments on HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and MuSiQue show that SAG achieves the best retrieval and end-to-end QA performance on every benchmark, with gains that widen as reasoning-chain complexity increases. On MuSiQue, where multi-hop evidence chaining is most demanding, SAG reaches 80.36% Recall@5, outperforming the strongest baseline by 11.52 points. This work paves the way for knowledge infrastructure that enables LLM agents to retrieve and reason over continually growing organizational knowledge.

</details>


### [152] [A corpus-specific clinical RAG system matches or outperforms newer frontier LLMs on HealthBench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12138)
*Praveen Reddy,Charuta Mandke,Suvrankar Datta,Sarah Khan,Siddharth Reddy Anthireddy,Shitij Arora,Vishal Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了VITA——一个针对印度及其他中低收入国家（LMIC）环境专门构建的检索增强生成（RAG）临床系统。在4,023道HealthBench英语题目上，VITA以51.9%的得分率领先于GPT-5.4等前沿大模型；在更新的模型和中性评判器下差距缩小至持平，但在准确性、完整性上仍保持优势，仅在沟通质量得分上较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往通用大语言模型与专门临床AI工具的对比大多基于高收入国家背景开发的基准和少数系统，缺乏对LMIC环境、领域特定检索增强系统与前沿LLM之间系统性的比较。作者希望通过专为印度等资源有限环境构建的VITA，检验领域特化RAG系统是否仍能与前沿大模型竞争。

Method: VITA从疾病特定指南、印度抗菌药物耐药数据、国家处方集约束和资源有限护理协议的精选语料库中进行检索增强生成；评测使用4,023道HealthBench题目（占82.5%），采用GPT-4.1评判器打分；另设500题子集，使用新一代模型（GPT-5.5、Claude Opus 4.8、Gemini 3.5 Pro、Grok 4.3）和与所有测试系统无血缘关系的中性开源评判器（DeepSeek-V4-Pro）进行稳健性验证。

Result: 在原始评测中VITA以51.9%得分率排名第一，领先GPT-5.4（46.1%）、o4-mini（44.3%）、Gemini 3.1 Pro（42.6%）和Claude Sonnet 4.6（37.3%），在45.4%的问题上得分最高。在更新模型和中性评判器下，VITA与GPT-5.5在每题平均分上统计上无显著差异，但在加权得分和获胜问题上领先；准确性、完整性优势得以保持，但沟通分数较低。

Conclusion: 专为临床场景构建的RAG系统在开放基准上仍能与前沿LLM保持竞争力，表明语料库特异性是提升接地准确性但以沟通流畅性为代价的设计变量。同时，基准和全部评估输出均公开，支持独立验证。

Abstract: General-purpose large language models (LLMs) have recently been reported to match or exceed specialized clinical AI tools on medical benchmarks, but such comparisons draw on a narrow set of systems and on benchmarks developed largely in high-income settings. We evaluate VITA, a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) system purpose-built for contextual knowledge retrieval in India and other low- and middle-income (LMIC) settings. VITA retrieves from a curated corpus of disease-specific guidelines, India-specific antimicrobial resistance data, national formulary constraints, and resource-limited care protocols; its architecture and corpus are proprietary, but the benchmark, the physician-written rubrics, and our full response and scoring outputs are public for independent verification. On 4,023 English-language HealthBench questions (80.5% of the benchmark), scored with a GPT-4.1 judge, VITA ranked first with 51.9% of possible rubric points, ahead of GPT-5.4 (46.1%), o4-mini (44.3%), Gemini 3.1 Pro (42.6%), and Claude Sonnet 4.6 (37.3%), and scored highest on 45.4% of questions. To test robustness to newer models and judge lineage, a 500-question subset was re-run against current-generation models (GPT-5.5, Claude Opus 4.8, Gemini 3.5 Pro, Grok 4.3) and graded by a neutral open-weight judge (DeepSeek-V4-Pro) sharing no lineage with any system tested. Here the gap narrowed to parity: VITA and GPT-5.5 were statistically indistinguishable on mean per-question score, while VITA led on points-weighted score and won the most questions. VITA's advantages in accuracy and completeness persisted under the neutral judge; its communication scores were lower. These results indicate that a purpose-built clinical RAG system remains competitive with frontier LLMs on an open benchmark, consistent with corpus specificity as a design variable that improves grounding at some cost to communication polish.

</details>


### [153] [Massive Activations in Hybrid Linear Attention Large Language Models: Pre-Attention Spikes and Inter-Spike Plateaus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12149)
*Zunhai Su,Bohan Sun,Xialie Zhuang,Shuibai Zhang,He Xiao,Jing Xiong,Hengyuan Zhang,Zhongzhu Zhou,Tiantian Zhang,Ngai Wong,Chuan-Wei Kuo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了层交错混合线性注意力（HLA）大语言模型中的大规模激活（MA），发现了两种与架构对齐的形态：全注意力层前的尖峰（PAS）和经线性注意力层持续的内尖峰平台（ISP）。随着全注意力层变密，PAS通过ISP连接，最终恢复全注意力LLM的稳定MA形态。该组织在五种线性注意力架构、六种混合配置、五个数据域及1.2B到397B参数的模型中均得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大规模激活（MA）在纯全注意力LLM中已被广泛研究，但混合线性注意力（HLA）架构中的MA行为尚未被系统探索。理解MA在HLA中的形态与生命周期机制，对于优化混合架构设计、理解注意力机制与门控交互具有重要意义。

Method: 作者对层交错HLA LLM进行系统性扫描，识别出两种MA形态（PAS和ISP）；通过在五种线性注意力架构、六种混合配置和多个数据域上进行大规模实验验证形态的普适性；对GDN混合模型进行多达1.3B参数的受控预训练，测试输出门控对MA的影响；并采用系统离群值分析揭示MA的生命周期机制（写入-汇-取消过程）。

Result: 发现两种架构对齐的MA形态：全注意力层前的PAS和跨线性注意力层持续的ISP；全注意力层密度增加时PAS通过ISP融合为稳定的全注意力型MA；输出门控能强烈衰减MA幅度但不破坏层间组织，而移除GDN门控仅产生相对温和的放大效应；机制分析表明PAS遵循局部的写入-汇-取消过程，ISP的持久性源于延迟取消，在全注意力极限下恢复稳定MA形态。

Conclusion: HLA LLM中的MA呈现出与架构层交错模式一致的独特双形态组织，其演化遵循共享的生命周期机制（由MA取消时机决定）。该发现揭示了混合注意力架构中信息处理的结构化路径，为未来HLA模型的设计与优化提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We present the first systematic study of Massive activations (MAs) in layer-interleaved HLA LLMs and uncover two architecture-aligned morphologies: MAs consistently spike immediately before full attention layers, forming pre-attention spikes (PAS), and can persist through intervening linear attention layers, giving rise to inter-spike plateaus (ISP). As full attention becomes denser, successive PAS become increasingly connected through ISP, ultimately recovering the stable MA morphology of full attention LLMs. We establish the recurrence of this organization across five linear attention architectures, six hybridization configurations, five data domains, and representative open-source hybrid models spanning 1.2B to 397B total parameters. Controlled pretraining of GDN-based hybrids at scales up to 1.3B shows that both morphologies emerge early and respond asymmetrically to output gating: full attention output gating strongly attenuates their absolute magnitudes without eliminating their layerwise organization, whereas removing GDN gates yields comparatively modest amplification. Mechanistically, our systematic-outlier analysis supports a shared lifecycle account governed by the timing of MA cancellation. PAS follows a localized write-sink-cancel process, while the extended persistence of ISP is consistent with delayed cancellation. At the full attention limit, this account recovers the stable MA morphology characteristic of full attention LLMs. Our code is available at https://github.com/StartluxLabs/Massive-Activations-HLA.

</details>


### [154] [Information Abundance Paradox: Long-Context Training Undermines Parametric Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12218)
*Arda Uzunoglu,Benjamin van Durme,Daniel Khashabi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"信息丰裕悖论"：训练上下文中存在丰富相关信息时，模型会降低将知识参数化编码的动机，转而依赖上下文。预训练中，随上下文窗口增大，语言建模、NLU和闭卷MCQA性能先升至一个中间最优值后持续下降；SFT中，更多任务相关训练上下文提升带支持上下文的表现，但降低对上下文缺失或误导的鲁棒性。机制上，长上下文训练将梯度压力从前馈网络（参数知识）转向注意力模块，增加推理时对上下文的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"训练上下文越长越好"的隐含假设，质疑长上下文训练仅通过暴露更丰富证据来帮助模型的普遍观点，探究上下文窗口如何改变模型的学习模式（参数化内化 vs 上下文化）。

Method: 通过预训练（长文档）和SFT两类实验，在不同上下文窗口规模下测试语言建模、NLU、闭卷MCQA等任务；结合复杂度分析（长上下文提供更低复杂度解）和因果干预（梯度压力从前馈网络向注意力模块转移）进行机制验证。

Result: 预训练中上下文窗口存在中间最优值，超出后性能持续下降；SFT中更多训练上下文提升伴随上下文的表现但削弱无上下文/误导上下文时的鲁棒性；梯度分析显示长上下文训练将梯度压力从前馈网络（参数知识）转移至注意力模块，因果干预证实推理时对上下文的依赖增强。

Conclusion: 研究支持信息丰裕悖论：趋向无限上下文并非简单的"提供更多数据"即可解决，即便高质量长上下文数据充足，模型也会因上下文提供的低复杂度解而减少参数化知识内化，提示上下文缩放需在参数化与情境化学习之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly trained and deployed with long contexts that span documents, code repositories, and interaction histories. This scaling reflects the implicit assumption that training on longer contexts will only help the model by exposing it to richer evidence. We challenge this view by studying how the context window shapes a model's mode of learning, shifting it between parametric internalization and contextualization. We propose the Information Abundance Paradox, which hypothesizes that abundant relevant information in the training context can reduce the incentive to encode that information parametrically, thereby increasing reliance on context. In pretraining with long documents, increasing the context window improves language modeling, natural language understanding, and closed-book MCQA only up to an intermediate optimum, after which performance consistently declines. In supervised fine-tuning, more task-relevant train-time context improves performance with supporting context, but reduces robustness when context is absent or misleading at test time. Our analysis suggests that this behavior arises when longer context provides a lower complexity solution. Mechanistically, training with informative context shifts gradient pressure from feed-forward networks, often linked to parametric knowledge, toward attention modules, and causal interventions show that this shift increases reliance on context during inference. Overall, these findings support the Information Abundance Paradox and suggest that scaling toward near-infinite context is not simply a matter of supplying more data, even when high-quality long-context data is abundant.

</details>


### [155] [One Frozen Simulator Is Not Enough: Simulator Collapse in Multi-Agent RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12253)
*Simon Yu,Nicholas Tomlin,Marwa Abdulhai,Ximing Lu,Derek Chong,Abe Hou,Dilara Soylu,Sergey Levine,Christopher D. Manning,Weiyan Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出多智能体强化学习在人类-AI交互场景中通常依赖单一LLM模拟用户，但该做法因"模拟器坍缩"（simulator collapse）而系统性泛化失败。作者提出两个互补方案：推理时的Verbalized Sampling和训练时的Co-Training，分别在多个基准和真实用户上验证了有效性，并开源SCOPE框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法使用单一LLM作为用户模拟器训练策略，但由于LLM的模式坍缩特性，训练出的策略会对模拟器的支配性模式过度拟合，难以泛化到未见过的模拟器和真实用户。作者希望从理论和实践上解决这一泛化失败问题。

Method: 作者从理论上形式化了模拟器坍缩问题，并提出两个互补方案：(1) 推理时的Verbalized Sampling，通过从言语化响应分布中采样来拓宽模拟器行为、减少模式坍缩；(2) 训练时的Co-Training，将策略与一群可训练的模拟器联合优化，防止策略对单一模拟器模式过拟合。

Result: 在Persuasion for Good、τ²-bench和CooperBench三个多轮基准上，Verbalized Sampling相比单模拟器RL将留出集成功率提升最多9%，Co-Training进一步将增益提升至14%；人类研究在真实用户上验证了相仿的增益。两种方案均保持了单模拟器RL下会坍缩的策略多样性。

Conclusion: 训练环境的多样性（而不仅仅是策略本身）对多轮RL向真实世界部署的泛化至关重要。作者开源了SCOPE（Population Co-Training多智能体RL框架）以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning for human-AI interaction typically relies on a single large language model to simulate user behavior. We show that this approach systematically fails to generalize, and trace the failure to simulator collapse: because the simulator LLM is mode-collapsed, an LLM policy trained against it overfits to narrow strategies that exploit the simulator's dominant mode, and such a policy transfers poorly to unseen simulators and real users. We formalize this collapse theoretically and propose two complementary solutions, one at inference time and one at training time. The inference-time solution, Verbalized Sampling, broadens the simulator's behavior by sampling from a verbalized response distribution, reducing mode collapse. The training-time solution, Co-Training, jointly optimizes the policy against a population of trainable simulators, preventing it from overfitting to any single simulator's mode. We validate both solutions on three multi-turn benchmarks: Persuasion for Good, $τ^2$-bench, and CooperBench. Verbalized Sampling improves held-out success by up to 9% over single-simulator RL, and Co-Training pushes gains further to 14%; the human study shows similar gain on real users. Both solutions preserve the policy diversity that collapses under single-simulator RL. To support further work in this direction, we release SCOPE, an open-source framework for Population Co-Training multi-agent RL. More broadly, our results suggest that the diversity of the training environment, not only the policy, is critical to the generalization of multi-turn RL to real-world deployment.

</details>


### [156] [A Cascaded Unsupervised-Supervised NLP Pipeline for Detecting Accusatory Language in Public Procurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12269)
*Bryan Torres,Daniel Riofrío,José Vega-Sánchez,Nathaly Orozco,Carla Parra,Karen Rosero,Felipe Grijalva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个混合NLP框架，结合无监督聚类(GMM)与有监督分类(Random Forest)，对厄瓜多尔公共采购系统SOCE中的参与者评论进行语义分析，以检测潜在的不规范采购流程。实验表明，领域训练的Word2Vec嵌入结合GMM聚类和RF分类器，能在严重类别不平衡下取得高精度和高召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共采购涉及大量财政资源，需要持续审计和监控，但利益相关者评论和公开政府数据往往未被充分利用。这些数据可能揭示程序性违规行为，因此有必要开发方法来自动分析这些文本数据以增强采购透明度和风险识别能力。

Method: 提出一种混合建模框架：(1) 使用Word2Vec、LLaMA和RoBERTa生成语义嵌入；(2) 采用高斯混合模型(GMM)进行无监督聚类以发现潜在模式；(3) 应用有监督分类阶段来识别指控性或举报风格的评论。全文在NLP流水线中完成。

Result: 实验结果表明，领域训练的Word2Vec嵌入、GMM聚类和Random Forest分类器的组合，在严重类别不平衡条件下仍能达到高精度和高召回率。

Conclusion: 轻量级、领域自适应的NLP架构能够有效支持公共采购系统的风险识别和透明度增强，无需大规模计算基础设施即可实现。

Abstract: Public procurement involves the allocation of substantial financial resources; therefore, continuous oversight through audits, controls, and monitoring mechanisms is essential. However, stakeholder comments and publicly available government data are often underutilized, despite their potential to reveal procedural irregularities. To address this gap, this paper analyzes metadata from Ecuador's Sistema Oficial de Contratación Pública (SOCE, Official Public Procurement System), with particular emphasis on participant comments generated during the pre-contractual phase. We propose a hybrid modeling framework that integrates unsupervised clustering and supervised classification within a natural language processing (NLP) pipeline to uncover latent patterns and detect potentially irregular procurement processes. Semantic embeddings are generated using Word2Vec, LLaMA, and RoBERTa, followed by Gaussian Mixture Models (GMMs) for unsupervised clustering. A supervised classification stage is then applied to identify accusatory or whistleblowing-style comments. Experimental results show that the combination of domain-trained Word2Vec embeddings, GMM-based clustering, and a Random Forest classifier achieves high precision and recall, even under severe class imbalance. These findings demonstrate that lightweight, domain-adapted NLP architectures can effectively support risk identification and enhance transparency in public procurement systems without requiring large-scale computational infrastructure.

</details>


### [157] [Structural Silence: When AI Infrastructure Fails Speakers of Underrepresented Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12278)
*Avijit Roy,Proma Roy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文以孟加拉语为案例，揭示AI教育工具在欠资源社区中存在的四大结构性障碍：网页内容占比失衡（<0.5%）、训练token赤字（67:1）、字母音节文字分词惩罚以及低连接性环境下的数字鸿沟，主张将数据集稀缺视为结构性壁垒而非孤立技术问题，并倡导离线优先设计作为公平导向的基础设施策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI教育工具被宣传为欠资源社区获取支持的规模化解决方案，但其底层基础设施（训练语料、分词方案、评估基准、部署架构）在模型训练之前就已系统性边缘化低资源语言使用者。本文旨在揭示这一被忽视的结构性不公正。

Method: 以孟加拉语作为案例研究，鉴于其作为全球使用最广泛语言之一的代表性；通过识别和剖析四类相互关联的失败机制——网络内容覆盖缺口、多语语料库训练token赤字、表音音节文字带来的分词惩罚（token fertility放大数据赤字效应）、以及城乡互联网渗透率差异造成的连接排斥——进行系统性分析。

Result: 孟加拉语占全球人口约4%，却仅占全球网络内容不足0.5%；英语与孟加拉语在主要多语语料库中的训练token比达67:1；孟加拉语的alphasyllabary文字导致更高的token fertility，进一步加剧数据赤字；农村个人互联网普及率（36.5%）远低于城市（71.4%），形成连接层排斥。

Conclusion: 数据集稀缺应被重新理解为结构性壁垒而非孤立的技术局限；离线优先设计应被视为促进公平性的基础设施策略；未来语言学和AI研究应致力于减少这些结构性不平等。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence tools for education and language support are increasingly framed as scalable responses to access gaps in under-resourced communities. Yet the infrastructure underlying these tools, including training corpora, tokenization schemes, evaluation benchmarks, and deployment architectures, can systematically disadvantage speakers of underrepresented languages before a model is trained.
  This paper examines these structural barriers through Bengali, one of the world's most widely spoken languages, focusing on AI-assisted education in low-connectivity environments. We identify four interlocking failures: a severe web presence gap, with Bengali accounting for less than 0.5% of global web content despite representing nearly 4% of the global population; a 67:1 training-token deficit between English and Bengali in major multilingual corpora; a tokenization penalty associated with Bengali's alphasyllabary script that compounds the data deficit through higher token fertility; and connectivity exclusion, with individual internet penetration at 36.5% in rural areas compared with 71.4% in urban areas.
  These failures reflect longstanding resource-allocation decisions, institutional priorities, and design defaults that did not center underrepresented languages in mainstream AI development. We argue that dataset scarcity should be understood as a structural barrier rather than an isolated technical limitation, and that offline-first design should be treated as an equity-oriented infrastructure strategy. We conclude with directions for linguistics and AI research aimed at reducing these structural inequalities.

</details>


### [158] [WavePhaseNet: A DFT-Based Method for Constructing Semantic Conceptual Hierarchy Structures (SCHS)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14419)
*Kiyotaka Kasubuchi,Kazuo Fukiya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过测度论和频率分析将Transformer/注意力机制中的幻觉问题理论化为一种结构性必然缺陷，提出WavePhaseNet方法：利用离散傅里叶变换构建语义概念分层结构（SCHS），将语义按频率分解（低频承载全局意义，高频承载局部句法）；通过1/f谱和累积能量分析得出GPT-4的24,576维嵌入空间可降至约3,000维的"完备表示"下界；基于Hodge理论的上同调正则化在降维空间中量化局部推理不一致性，实现全局一致的语义控制与幻觉抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图从根本上回答大语言模型中幻觉现象的成因，从传统上将其视为工程缺陷（如训练数据不足、解码策略不佳）转向理论层面，论证幻觉是Transformer架构在测度论和谱分析视角下的结构性内禀局限——嵌入空间作为σ-代数上的条件期望，无法与语义真值集同构，因此逻辑一致性断裂是不可避免的。基于此理论诊断，作者进一步提出一种可行的架构改进方案。

Method: 方法包含三个核心组成部分：(1) WavePhaseNet——沿序列维度施加DFT，将语义信息分解为不同频带（低频=全局意图，高频=局部句法），在对角化空间中实现语义的精确操纵；(2) 维度约简——利用1/f谱结构与Zipf定律，通过累积能量分析推导出约3,000维为"完备表示"的下界，将GPT-4的24,576维降至3,000维而不损失语义意图；(3) 上同调一致性控制——在降维空间中通过上同调正则化（在重叠局部窗口上构造图结构与上链复形）将局部推理间的不一致性量化为余边界损失，基于Hodge理论的调和投影提取最大一致的全局表示。

Result: 从理论上证明幻觉是Transformer嵌入空间结构缺陷的必然结果；推导出GPT-4嵌入空间约3,000维为保留完整语义的最小表示维度；通过频率分解实现了语义的分层分离与精确操控；利用Hodge理论的上同调框架将语义一致性转化为可计算的余边界正则化损失，实现对幻觉的结构性抑制。

Conclusion: WavePhaseNet将幻觉问题从"数据或训练层面的缺陷"重新定义为"架构层面的结构性约束"，并通过频率域分解、维度压缩（24,576→3,000）和上同调正则化的三重手段，在降低计算成本的同时增强了LLM的推理严谨性和语义一致性。

Abstract: This paper reformulates Transformer/Attention mechanisms in Large Language Models (LLMs) through measure theory and frequency analysis, theoretically demonstrating that hallucination is an inevitable structural limitation. The embedding space functions as a conditional expectation over a σ-algebra, and its failure to be isomorphic to the semantic truth set fundamentally causes logical consistency breakdown. WavePhaseNet Method The authors propose WavePhaseNet, which explicitly constructs a Semantic Conceptual Hierarchy Structure (SCHS) using Discrete Fourier Transform (DFT). By applying DFT along the sequence dimension, semantic information is decomposed into frequency bands: low-frequency components capture global meaning and intent, while high-frequency components represent local syntax and expression. This staged separation enables precise semantic manipulation in diagonalized space. Dimensionality Reduction GPT-4's 24,576-dimensional embedding space exhibits a 1/f spectral structure based on language self-similarity and Zipf's law. Through cumulative energy analysis, the authors derive that approximately 3,000 dimensions constitute the lower bound for "complete representation." This demonstrates that reduction from 24,576 to 3,000 dimensions preserves meaning and intent while enabling rigorous reasoning and suppressing hallucination. Cohomological Consistency Control The reduced embedding space, constructed via cohomological regularization over overlapping local windows, allows defining a graph structure and cochain complex. This quantifies inconsistencies among local inferences as coboundary-based losses. Applying harmonic projection based on Hodge theory positions cohomology as a computable regularization principle for controlling semantic consistency, extracting maximally consistent global representations.

</details>


### [159] [What Iterated Self-Feeding Probes of Language Models Measure, and a test that separates the construction from the model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10986)
*Nicolás Vera Zúñiga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究"语言模型探测自身输出"这类方法的测量含义。通过构造"token单元环在模型自身滑动条件概率下原位重采样"的Glauber动力学，并用公共随机数耦合两个仅差一个token的环，作者发现该探测同时测量两种看似相同实则不同的量：一类是纯由构造决定的运动学量（损伤光锥、token空间Lyapunov指数lambda_ca的半径标度在19个模型间不变），另一类才是真正追踪模型的量（lambda_ca在训练中出现可复现的过零点、吸引子份额对模型排序稳定）。二者混淆曾导致作者测出一个归因于探测器而非语言模型的相变（精确到三位小数）。文章给出区分二者的判据并报告了该纪律发现的四个估值器失效案例，整套方法学以软件包形式发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日益增长的方法（self-consistency、迭代精炼、智能体循环）通过给语言模型喂它自己的输出来探测模型，但这类"自环"探测究竟在测量什么并不清楚。作者用一个构造上尖锐的问题切入，目标是厘清这类探测读数中哪些是构造固有的运动学假象、哪些是模型真实的性质，从而为整个探测范式提供方法论基础。

Method: 采用token序列上的Glauber动力学（环状token单元在模型自身窗口化条件概率p_r(x_i|x_{i±r})下原位重采样）。核心改动在于耦合方式：用公共随机数推进两个仅差一个token的环，使未损伤副本精确零发散，从而将损伤扩散（damage spreading）从最大耦合给出的混合时间中分离出来、变为可测。用"固定构造变模型"与"固定模型变构造"的交叉判据区分两类读数，并以Domany-Kinzel损伤场的逐比特精确复现来验证仪器。

Result: 损害光锥是纯运动学的，token空间Lyapunov指数lambda_ca(r)的半径标度在19个模型、两个标度阶梯、70倍跨度下均模型不变；同时lambda_ca在训练中于可复现点过零、吸引子份额对模型排序稳定，这些是真正跟踪模型的量。区分二者的判据为：固定构造变模型或固定模型变构造看哪些读数移动。仪器通过Domany-Kinzel损伤场逐比特精确复现验证，并揭露了四个此前被当作测量的"撤回判决"估值器失效案例。

Conclusion: "模型探测自身输出"这类方法测量的其实是两种看似相同但来源不同的量：构造固有的运动学量和模型真实性质。如果不加区分，前者极易被误认为后者（作者自己就曾把属于探测器的相变测到三位小数并误归因于语言模型）。作者给出可操作区分判据，通过先复现再测量的纪律捕获了四个估值器失效，整套方法学以软件包形式交付。

Abstract: A growing class of methods probes a language model by feeding it its own output: self-consistency, iterated refinement, agentic loops. We ask what such a probe measures, in a construction chosen to make the question sharp: a ring of token cells resampled in place by the model's own windowed conditional p_r(x_i | x_{i+-r}). The substrate is Glauber dynamics on token sequences and is not new; what we change is the coupling. Advancing two rings that differ in one token under common random numbers makes undamaged copies diverge by exactly zero, so damage spreading becomes measurable where a maximal coupling gives mixing times instead. The answer is that it measures two different things at once, in readings that look alike. Some quantities are fixed by the construction: the damage light cone is kinematic, and the radius scaling of the token-space Lyapunov exponent lambda_ca(r) is model-invariant across 19 models and two scale ladders spanning 70x. Others genuinely track the model: lambda_ca crosses zero at a reproducible point in training, and the attractor share ranks models consistently however the lattice is built. Left undistinguished, the first kind is readily mistaken for the second -- we did so ourselves for four months, and report a phase transition we measured to three decimal places that belongs to the probe rather than to any language model. We give the test that separates them: hold the construction fixed and vary the model, or hold the model fixed and vary the construction, and see which readings move. We validate the instrument by reproduction first, recovering a Domany-Kinzel damage field bit-exactly against an independent prediction, and we report the estimator failures that this discipline caught -- four retracted verdicts, each on a quantity that looked like a measurement. The methodology ships as a package.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [160] [Adaptation of Generalist Robot Policies with Minimal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11363)
*Shreyas Kowshik,Sreyas Venkataraman,Leo Wang,Niharika Pant,Max Simchowitz,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MiDAS提出了一种"最小数据适应"范式，通过"单次/少量演示的行为克隆+价值型残差策略在线强化学习"的离线到在线配方，使预训练VLA策略仅凭一次演示即可完成新任务学习，并在LIBERO、RoboCasa和真实双机械臂平台上验证了其有效性，是首个从单次演示即可实现可靠机器人策略适应的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习的核心目标是从依赖任务特定人工数据收集，转向通过自主交互持续改进的系统。然而完全自主学习在当前策略下仍然困难：稀疏奖励和弱零样本探索使得机器人几乎不可能从零发现成功行为。因此，作者研究"最小数据适应"这一场景——预训练策略仅需一次演示加自主在线交互即可学习新任务，作为完全自主改进的最接近且可行的代理，以探讨最小人工引导能否启动自主学习及其所需算法要素。

Method: MiDAS采用一个简洁的离线到在线强化学习配方：(1) 首先通过行为克隆在单个/少量演示上对预训练的视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型进行锚定，使其聚焦于目标任务；(2) 随后基于残差策略参数化，通过基于价值的在线强化学习对策略进行改进。这种残差策略设计使在线RL在已有预训练先验基础上进行局部优化，降低学习难度。

Result: 在LIBERO和RoboCasa基准上，MiDAS从最少一次演示即可恢复强大任务性能，显著优于基线方法，并能泛化到超出演示条件的情景。此外，在双机械臂YAM平台上的真实评估中，MiDAS从单次演示获得的脆弱低成功率策略出发，经过约6小时在线交互即可提升鲁棒性并学会新的成功行为。

Conclusion: MiDAS首次证明了从单个任务演示出发进行可靠机器人策略适应的可行性。该工作表明，将少量人类引导（单次演示）与自主在线强化学习相结合，能够成为实现机器人自主改进的切实可行路径，为摆脱大规模人工数据收集开辟了新方向。

Abstract: A central goal in robot learning is to move beyond task-specific human data collection toward robots that improve through autonomous interaction. Yet fully autonomous learning remains difficult with current policies: sparse rewards and weak zero-shot exploration make it unlikely that a robot will discover successful behavior from scratch. We study minimal-data adaptation, a regime in which a pre-trained robot policy must learn a new task from as little as one demonstration followed by autonomous online interaction. This setting serves as the closest tractable proxy for fully autonomous improvement, allowing us to study whether minimal human guidance can bootstrap autonomous learning and what algorithmic ingredients make it feasible. We build MiDAS, a simple offline-to-online RL recipe that first anchors a pre-trained VLA to the target task with behavior cloning on single/few demonstrations, then improves it through value-based online RL on a residual policy parameterization. Across LIBERO and RoboCasa, MiDAS recovers strong task performance from as little as one demonstration, substantially outperforming baselines and generalizing beyond demonstrated conditions. We further evaluate MiDAS on a bimanual YAM platform. Starting from a fragile low-success policy obtained from a single demonstration, MiDAS improves its robustness and learns new successful behaviors over ~6 hours of online interaction. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of reliable robot policy adaptation from a single task demonstration.

</details>


### [161] [Top-down Traffic Scenario Generation via Joint Initial-Goal Diffusion and Trajectory Infilling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11407)
*Da Saem Lee,Yash Vardhan Pant,Sebastian Fischmeister*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TrafficDiffuser，一个自上而下的交通场景生成框架，通过联合建模初始状态和目标状态对来生成高层交通场景（即初始与目标状态对），使初始状态更具可解释性，并将轨迹生成简化为填充问题。在Argoverse 2数据集上，TrafficDiffuser在目标状态生成和智能体初始化方面均优于现有方法，速度分布距离降低55.3%，离路率降低2.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的交通仿真器要求智能体提供初始状态才能生成轨迹，这限制了可扩展性和多样性。虽然数据驱动的智能体初始化已有研究，但生成的初始状态缺乏可解释性（无法说明为何智能体在特定位置初始化）。此外，给定初始状态后的轨迹生成也面临目标点可变性和到达方式学习的挑战。因此需要一种能同时解决可解释性、多样性和轨迹生成难题的方法。

Method: TrafficDiffuser采用生成式（扩散模型）框架，联合建模高层交通场景（即初始状态和目标状态对）。流程分为两个层次：(1) 高层场景生成：生成可解释的初始与目标状态对；(2) 轨迹生成：利用生成的场景将轨迹生成转化为填充（infilling）问题，从而简化求解。该方法还支持基于不同轨迹模式的约束，并能与现有轨迹生成模型集成。

Result: 在Argoverse 2运动预测数据集上进行了大量实验。TrafficDiffuser在生成目标状态方面表现优异，同时相比次优的智能体初始化方法，将速度分布距离降低了55.3%，离路率降低了2.8%，生成的输出能更好地捕获真实世界分布。

Conclusion: TrafficDiffuser通过将交通场景生成提升到高层（初始-目标状态对）层面，解决了现有仿真器初始状态可解释性差和轨迹生成困难的问题。实验证明其生成的场景能更好地贴近真实世界分布，且生成的场景可直接与现有轨迹生成模型集成使用，为自动驾驶仿真提供了更高效、更可靠的场景生成方案。

Abstract: Robust traffic simulators are crucial for developing and testing autonomous vehicles to reduce the costly, labor-intensive real-world data collection process and the need for physical presence on the road. However, existing simulators require agents' initial states to generate trajectories, which limits scalability and diversity due to restrictions on the given initial states. While data-driven agent initialization has been widely studied, the generated initial states are not interpretable in terms of why the agents are initialized at those specific locations. Given known initial states, trajectory generation is also a challenging problem, as the model must learn the variability of the destination and how agents should reach it over time. In this paper, we propose TrafficDiffuser, a top-down traffic scenario generation framework that generates high-level traffic scenarios, defined by initial and goal state pairs, by jointly modeling them. The high-level scenario generation makes initial states better interpretable and reduces trajectory generation into as simple as an infilling problem. We demonstrate how the generated high-level traffic scenarios can be used, including constraining based on different trajectory modes and integrating them with existing trajectory generation models. We conduct extensive experiments on the Argoverse 2 motion prediction dataset to evaluate how well the generated outputs capture real-world distributions. In addition to generating goal states, TrafficDiffuser outperforms the next-best approach for agent initialization, reducing speed distribution distance by 55.3% and the off-road rate by 2.8%.

</details>


### [162] [From Self-Normal-Positioning to Omni-Directional Tracking: Real-Time Surface Modeling Enabled Probe Tilt Control for Robotic Ultrasound Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11409)
*Xihan Ma,Haichong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种全方向探头定向控制框架，集成RGB-D感知、局部表面建模与任务空间定向控制，使超声探头能够跟踪相对于表面法线的任意角度，解决了现有机器人超声系统仅支持垂直探头定位、无法满足超声心动图等非垂直成像需求的问题。实验显示平均角度跟踪误差为1.06±0.66°，可恢复相对于表面法线最大44.39±2.59°的非垂直倾斜角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声成像质量严重依赖探头相对患者身体的姿态。现有机器人超声系统大多仅支持探头垂直于局部表面的正常定位，这一约束在超声心动图等需要非垂直探头角度的检查中不适用，因此临床上需要能实时感知局部表面并保持所需探头朝向的机器人系统。

Method: 提出全方向探头定向控制框架，包含三个模块：RGB-D感知（采集多视角点云）、局部表面建模（融合点云并给出局部表面的二次估计）、任务空间定向控制（将期望成像方向相对于法线编码，使探头能够跟踪任意角度）。

Result: 通过平面跟踪、仿真体模目标角度恢复以及专家选定视图的体内跟踪实验进行评估。平均角度跟踪误差为1.06±0.66°，系统可恢复相对于表面法线最大44.39±2.59°的非垂直倾斜角，并在仿真体模和体内实验中成功获取目标心腔视图。

Conclusion: 该框架使机器人超声系统能够灵活跟踪任意探头朝向，克服了以往仅支持垂直定位的限制，在超声心动图等需要非垂直成像角度的临床场景中具有可行性。

Abstract: Ultrasound (US) provides real-time, radiation-free imaging, but the image quality depends strongly on how the probe is oriented against the patient body. Robotic US can reduce operator workload and improve acquisition consistency; however, most existing systems focus on normal positioning, where the probe is maintained perpendicular to the local surface. This constraint is inadequate for examinations like echocardiography, where obtaining a diagnostic view requires a non-normal probe angle. Consequently, a clinically useful robotic system must sense the local surface in real-time and preserve the desired probe orientation. Here, we propose an omni-directional probe-orientation control framework that integrates RGB-D perception, local-surface modeling, and task-space orientation control. The surface model fuses multi-view point clouds and provides a quadratic estimate of the local surface. A desired imaging direction is then encoded relative to the normal, enabling the probe to track arbitrary angles. The framework was evaluated through flat-surface tracking, phantom target-angle recovery, and in-vivo tracking of an expert selected view. Results show that the mean angular tracking error was 1.06 +- 0.66 deg. The system recovered a non-normal tilt angle of up to 44.39 +- 2.59 deg relative to the surface normal, and acquired the desired heart chamber view in the phantom and in-vivo experiments.

</details>


### [163] [Locomotion Variability and User Experience in Smart Wheelchair Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11417)
*Sean Kille,Adina M. Panchea,Balint Varga,Sören Hohmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了共享控制场景中不同辅助策略如何影响人类运动变异性、任务表现和主观交互体验，提出了一种保留用户自然运动结构的自主支持型共享控制策略，并通过用户研究证明其在保持任务性能的同时显著提升感知能动性和感知有用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于模型的辅助策略通常假设人类行为是确定性的，并抑制运动变异性，这可能改变交互体验并降低感知能动性。尽管运动变异性在功能上具有重要意义，但其在辅助交互中的有意保留及其对用户体验的影响尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出一种自主支持型共享控制策略，保留用户自然运动结构。通过用户研究进行实证评估，参与者分别在三种条件下推动智能电动轮椅：(1)无辅助，(2)传统减少变异性的辅助，(3)保留变异性的辅助，比较任务表现和主观交互体验。

Result: 两种辅助模式下的任务相关性能相当，但保留自然运动变异性的辅助模式带来更优的交互体验：参与者报告的感知能动性显著高于传统辅助，感知有用性也最高。

Conclusion: 变异性感知的辅助策略能够在保持任务性能的同时支持用户自主性。更广泛地，结果表明设计辅助机器人系统时应尊重人类运动的具身结构，而非将变异性视为需要被忽略或消除的噪声。

Abstract: Human movement is inherently variable, with variability structured according to task relevance: movements are typically more consistent at task-critical points and more flexible elsewhere. In human-robot interaction (HRI), however, model-based assistance strategies commonly assume deterministic human behavior and suppress such variability, potentially altering how interactions are experienced and lowering sense of agency. While movement variability is increasingly recognized as functionally meaningful, its deliberate preservation in assisted interaction, and its consequences for user experience, remain underexplored. In this paper, we empirically investigate how different assistance strategies shape human movement variability, task performance, and subjective interaction experience in a shared control setting. We introduce an autonomy-supportive shared control strategy that preserves users' natural movement structure. This approach is evaluated in a user study in which participants push an intelligent powered wheelchair under three conditions: no assistance, conventional variability-reducing assistance, and variability-preserving assistance. While task-relevant performance remained comparable across assisted modes, preserving natural movement variability led to more favorable interaction experiences. In particular, participants reported significantly higher perceived agency compared to conventional assistance and highest perceived usefulness. These findings suggest that variability-aware assistance can support both performance and user autonomy in physical human-robot collaboration.
