*Yirui Liu,Ruoling Qi,Longwen Wang,Xuaner Wu,Jian Chen,Yuxin Jin,Jiawei Shao,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LinearKV是一个针对混合LLM的无训练位置无关缓存（PIC）框架。其核心洞见是"解耦初始化"：每个线性层将K个匹配的局部状态映射为单一初始状态，而全注意力层照常拼接KV缓存。研究发现单个缓存状态足以作为线性层初始化器，而并发的HYPIC方案（将K个缓存状态精确组合成全前缀状态）不仅不必要，在某些架构上甚至有害。实验在三个混合模型和三个PIC选择器上进行，结果表明单状态初始化在GDN模型上与精确组合效果相当（恢复最高92%质量），而在Mamba-2模型上精确组合全面崩溃（EPIC下仅恢复46.6% vs 单状态86.8%），且单状态初始化更便宜，TTFT降至全预填充的0.46倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的位置无关缓存（PIC）方法都是为全注意力模型设计的，其核心操作（匹配token块、拼接KV、选择性重算）都依赖token索引的KV缓存。但混合LLM用线性循环替换了大部分注意力层，只暴露固定大小的状态，没有可拼接或局部修复的token索引KV。这引发一个问题：PIC能否惠及混合模型？需要什么条件？

Method: 提出LinearKV，一个无训练的混合PIC框架。关键洞见是"解耦初始化"：每个线性层将K个匹配的局部状态映射为单一初始状态（single cached state），而全注意力层照常拼接KV。该框架与现有PIC方法兼容，直接复用其token选择和重算逻辑。与并发的HYPIC方法（将所有K个缓存状态精确组合成完整前缀状态）形成对比，通过解耦初始化避免精确组合的开销和潜在危害。

Result: 在三个混合模型（两个GDN模型和Mamba-2）和三个PIC选择器上进行对比。GDN模型上两种方法效果持平，都能恢复大部分全质量（最高92%）；Mamba-2上精确组合在所有选择器下崩溃（EPIC下仅恢复46.6%），而单缓存块初始化恢复86.8%。单状态初始化也更便宜，TTFT降至全预填充的0.46倍，而精确组合额外增加5-17%开销。结果在LongBench QA和RULER的8K-32K设置下均成立。

Conclusion: 线性层的状态初始化应走"解耦"路线而非"精确组合"路线。单个缓存状态作为线性层初始化器不仅足够，而且在某些架构（如Mamba-2）上优于精确组合方案，同时更高效。这为混合LLM的PIC设计提供了新的指导原则。

Abstract: LLM serving is increasingly accelerated by position-independent caching (PIC). Existing PIC methods, however, are built for full-attention models, where a token-indexed KV cache underlies its core operations: matching reusable token chunks, concatenating their KV entries, and selectively recomputing a few tokens to restore cross-chunk context. Hybrid LLMs break these primitives---they replace most attention layers with linear recurrences that expose only a fixed-size state, leaving no token-indexed KV to concatenate or to locally repair. This raises a natural question: can PIC benefit hybrid models, and what would it take? We present LinearKV, a training-free hybrid-PIC framework. Its key insight is a \emph{decoupled initialization}: each linear layer maps its $K$ matched local states to a single initial state, while full-attention layers concatenate their KV as before. LinearKV is therefore compatible with existing PIC methods, reusing their token selection and recomputation as-is. Under this framework, we find that a \emph{single cached state} suffices as the linear layer's initializer. The algebraically principled alternative---composing all $K$ cached states into the exact full-prefix state, as concurrent work HYPIC does---is unnecessary and, on some architectures, even harmful. We compare the two across three hybrid models and three PIC selectors. On the two GDN models the two tie, both recovering most of full quality (up to $92\%$); on the Mamba-2 model, exact composition instead collapses under every selector---under EPIC, for instance, it recovers only $46.6\%$ of full quality, versus $86.8\%$ for a single cached block initializer. A single state initializer is also cheaper, cutting time-to-first-token to $0.46\times$ full prefill versus a further $5$--$17\%$ overhead for exact composition; results hold across LongBench QA and RULER at 8K--32K.

</details>


### [198] [InfraBench: Evaluating Infrastructure Agents Across Layers, Lifecycle, and Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11234)
*Yuan Gao,Zeren Yang,Junnan Li,Shawn,Zhong,Ahmed Dajani,Mai Zheng,Andrea Arpaci-Dusseau,Remzi Arpaci-Dusseau*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了InfraBench，一个用于评估AI智能体在真实基础设施管理任务上能力的基准套件，覆盖全系统栈和全运维生命周期，并包含细粒度风险评估。实验表明最强的智能体也无法得到满分，平均有效得分约为40%-88%，且智能体往往只能满足短期目标而留下非持久性更改、破坏的分布式不变量、不安全副作用等隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代计算基础设施管理日益复杂，AI智能体的进步为自动化基础设施管理提供了契机，但现有研究尚不清楚这些智能体能否应对真实世界的基础设施复杂度，缺乏系统性的评估基准。

Method: 构建InfraBench基准套件，覆盖完整系统栈与全运维生命周期，设计细粒度风险评估机制。通过多种智能体模型配置（共15种配置）在基准上进行实验，采用多次重复实验（每个任务重复三次）与逐项检查（per-check）评分相结合的方法分析模型表现及失败模式。

Result: 即使最强的智能体配置也无法在所有任务上获得满分。平均有效得分范围约为40%到88%（每种配置的标准误差为6-12分）。在重复实验下，顶级配置也仅能通过部分尝试。逐项检查揭示了一般性失败模式：智能体常满足短期目标但留下非持久性更改、破坏的分布式不变量、不安全的副作用和未清理的状态。

Conclusion: 现有AI智能体在基础设施管理任务上距离完全可靠仍有显著差距，尤其是在长期稳定性、分布式系统正确性和安全性方面。InfraBench及其即时排行榜、任务和评估框架已公开，可帮助社区持续评估和推动智能体在该领域的能力提升。

Abstract: Managing modern computing infrastructure has become a steadily harder problem due to the ever-increasing complexity. Recent advances in AI agents create a timely opportunity to automate infrastructure management tasks, but it remains unclear how well such agents can handle real-world infrastructure complexity. We present InfraBench, a benchmark suite for evaluating AI agents on realistic infrastructure tasks across the full system stack and full operational lifecycle with fine-grained risk assessment. Experiments with 15 agent-model configurations show that even the strongest agent cannot secure a full score across all tasks. Mean effective scores range from roughly 40% to 88% (with per-configuration standard errors of 6-12 points), repeating every task three times reveals that top configurations still pass only a fraction of their attempts, and per-check scoring exposes a general failure pattern: agents may routinely satisfy short-term objectives while leaving non-durable changes, broken distributed invariants, unsafe side effects, and uncleaned state behind. INFRABENCH, including its live leaderboard, tasks, and evaluation harness, is publicly available at infraben.ch.

</details>


### [199] [CORA-Diff: Confidence-Oriented Residual Acceptance for Efficient Diffusion Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11235)
*Yifan Wu,Yufeng Zhang,Kenli Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CORA-Diff，一种无需训练的扩散语言模型加速方法，利用原生轨迹信号（置信度与持续性）识别并接受已稳定的token，仅对未解析位置进行门控处理，在不修改骨干网络、不学习接受模型的情况下，实现了2.70x-13.14x的加速且任务质量几乎无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型在推理时通常使用固定去噪步数，但许多token在早期就已稳定，逐块解码浪费了密集的前向计算。现有加速方法依赖学习训练过滤器、修改得分、依赖模型或特定缓存机制，增加了复杂性和额外开销。本文探索是否可以利用原生的轨迹信号（而非附加的过滤机制）来识别那些可能已经到达确定性强密端点的残余位置，从而实现无训练、无修改的推理加速。

Method: CORA-Diff采用以下核心设计：(1) 保持原始扩散转移规则不变，仅对规则未解析（未稳定）的位置应用置信度与持续性门控（confidence-and-persistence gating）；(2) 已接受的token作为上下文保持可见；(3) 当一个块中所有位置都解析完成时终止该块；(4) 不需要修改骨干网络、无需学习接受模型、不修改logit；(5) 提供理论解释说明高置信度且持续性强的预测更可能匹配固定水平密集端点，并通过配对干预后的轨迹提供实证支持；(6) 在独立的GSM8K校准子集上选择单一操作点，并在所有评估中冻结使用。

Result: 在匹配Learn2PD-style LLaDA协议下，CORA-Diff在全部8个任务长度设置中具有最低的实测运行时间。5个设置中任务分数达到或超过密集解码，最大性能下降仅为1.22分。相比EOS感知的密集解码，在GSM8K和HumanEval上分别实现2.70x和3.32x加速；在固定水平1024/1024机制隔离协议下达到13.14x；迁移到Dream无需重新调参，实现3.18x-3.53x加速。

Conclusion: 原生置信度与持续性信号能够可靠地支持残余位置接受，有效减少重复去噪计算的同时保持任务质量。CORA-Diff证明了无需额外学习组件或模型修改，仅利用轨迹本身的信号即可实现高效的扩散语言模型推理加速。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) update many tokens in parallel, yet practical decoders often use a fixed denoising horizon. Many predictions stabilize early, but blockwise decoding continues until all positions are resolved, causing repeated dense forward passes. Existing accelerators often rely on learned filters, modified scores, dependency models, or cache-specific mechanisms. We ask whether native trajectory signals can identify residual positions likely to match the deterministic dense endpoint. We propose CORA-Diff, a training-free method that preserves the original transfer rule and applies confidence-and-persistence gating only to positions that rule leaves unresolved. Accepted tokens remain visible as context, and the block terminates once all positions are resolved. This requires no backbone change, learned acceptance model, or logit modification. Our theory explains why high-confidence, persistent predictions are more likely to match the fixed-horizon dense endpoint, and paired post-intervention trajectories provide direct empirical support. We select one operating point on a separate GSM8K calibration subset and freeze it for all evaluations. Under a matched Learn2PD-style LLaDA protocol, CORA-Diff has the lowest measured runtime in all eight task-length settings. Task scores match or exceed dense decoding in five settings, and the largest observed drop is 1.22 points. Its incremental speedups over EOS-aware dense decoding are 2.70x and 3.32x on GSM8K and HumanEval. It also reaches 13.14x under the fixed-horizon 1024/1024 mechanism-isolation protocol and transfers to Dream without retuning at 3.18x-3.53x. These results show that native confidence and persistence enable reliable residual acceptance, reducing repeated denoising computation while preserving task quality.

</details>


### [200] [Geometry-aware Incremental Neural Operator for Long-Horizon PDE prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11237)
*Jiaquan Zhang,Shuxu Chen,Haifan Meng,Yi Lu,Zhihan Lyu,Fan Mo,Wei Dong,Yang Yang,Chaoning Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoIncNO，一种几何感知的增量神经算子，用于稳定的长时程偏微分方程预测。该方法通过预测潜在增量实现残差推进，利用轻量级低秩投影器调节活跃频带内的通道耦合，并引入均值-涨落解耦重构机制以降低物理空间重构误差。在1D/2D/3D六个PDE基准上取得优于基线的一致预测精度、滚动稳定性和频谱保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子方法在长时程自回归预测中面临误差累积问题，表现为谱不一致、相位错位或均值漂移。已有方法多侧重于改进状态表示和算子骨干结构，却未对重复应用的潜在增量转移进行有效结构化约束，导致滚动过程中频谱误差和通道耦合不稳定累积。

Method: GeoIncNO包含三个核心组件：(1) 增量式预测框架，通过预测潜在空间残差实现逐步推进；(2) 轻量级低秩投影器，基于增量频谱能量分布识别活跃频带，并在这些频带内约束通道耦合；(3) 均值-涨落解耦重构机制，将稳定的均值结构与非平稳的动态涨落分别融合，仅对零均值涨落分量施加相位校正。

Result: 在覆盖1D、2D和3D动力系统的六个PDE基准上，GeoIncNO相比竞争性神经算子基线，在预测精度、滚动稳定性（rollout stability）和频谱保真度（spectral fidelity）方面均取得一致改进。

Conclusion: 通过显式结构化潜在增量转移并解耦均值与涨落分量，GeoIncNO有效缓解了长时程自回归预测中的误差累积问题，验证了几何感知和谱感知设计对神经算子稳定性的关键作用。

Abstract: Neural operators have shown strong potential for learning solution operators of partial differential equations (PDEs). However, long-horizon autoregressive prediction remains challenging: local errors accumulate as spectral inconsistency, phase misalignment, or mean drift. Existing methods mainly improve state representations and operator backbones, while leaving the repeatedly applied latent transition increment weakly structured, allowing spectral errors and unstable channel couplings to accumulate during rollout. To address these issues, we propose a geometry-aware incremental neural operator (GeoIncNO) for stable long-horizon PDE prediction. GeoIncNO predicts latent increments for residual advancement and uses lightweight low-rank projectors to regulate channel coupling within active frequency bands derived from the increment spectral energy distribution. To reduce physical-space reconstruction errors, GeoIncNO further introduces a mean--fluctuation decoupled reconstruction mechanism, where stable mean structures and dynamic fluctuations are fused separately, and phase correction is applied only to the zero-mean fluctuation component. Extensive experiments on six PDE benchmarks, covering 1D, 2D, and 3D dynamical systems, show that GeoIncNO achieves consistently strong prediction accuracy, improved rollout stability, and better spectral fidelity compared with competitive neural-operator baselines.

</details>


### [201] [Towards Query-Agnostic RAG Evaluation via Query Coverage and Claim Verifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11238)
*Jeonghwan Choi,Taewon Yun,Minjeong Ban,Gyeonghun Sun,Jae-Gil Lee,Hwanjun Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Q-CARE是一个查询无关且完全无需参考的RAG评估框架，通过将查询分解为子查询、答案分解为原子声明，在统一的"查询覆盖度"与"声明可验证性"原则上实现细粒度评估，提出了覆盖感知的检索器指标（C-Prec@k, C-nDCG@k）和声明级别的生成器指标（Completeness, Conciseness, Verifiableness），在覆盖8个数据集的人工标注基准上超越了RAGEval和RAGChecker等四种现有评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RAG评估框架难以在从封闭式事实查询到开放式解释请求的多样化用户查询上提供一致且细粒度的诊断，缺乏统一且无需参考的评估手段。

Method: 将查询分解为子查询、答案分解为原子声明，基于查询覆盖度（query coverage）和声明可验证性（claim verifiability）建立统一评估原则，提出覆盖感知的检索器指标（C-Prec@k, C-nDCG@k）和声明级生成器指标（Completeness, Conciseness, Verifiableness）。

Result: 在覆盖8个数据集的人工标注基准上，Q-CARE与人类评判的相关性高于包括RAGEval和RAGChecker在内的四种现有RAG评估指标，验证了其作为可靠自动化评估框架的有效性。

Conclusion: Q-CARE是一种查询无关、完全参考自由的RAG评估框架，能够在多样化的查询类型上提供细粒度且与人类判断高度一致的诊断，并可公开获取。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation improves the factuality of large language models by grounding responses in retrieved evidence, yet existing evaluation frameworks struggle to provide consistent, fine-grained diagnostics across the diverse spectrum of user queries, ranging from close-ended fact-seeking to open-ended explanatory requests. We propose Q-CARE, a query-agnostic and fully reference-free framework that enables fine-grained assessment by decomposing queries into sub-queries and answers into atomic claims. Q-CARE establishes a unified evaluation principle based on query coverage and claim verifiability, yielding coverage-aware retriever metrics (C-Prec@k, C-nDCG@k) and claim-level generator metrics (Completeness, Conciseness, and Verifiableness). On a human-annotated benchmark spanning eight datasets, Q-CARE achieves higher correlation with human judgments than four existing RAG evaluation metrics, including RAGEval and RAGChecker, proving its effectiveness as a reliable, automated evaluation framework. Code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/DISL-Lab/Q-CaRE-COLM-26.

</details>


### [202] [VQ-bench: A Composable Vector Quantization Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11240)
*Ashwin Padaki,Amir Ingber,Edo Liberty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了统一的向量量化框架，将量化过程分解为7种基本原语，并展示了如何任意组合这些原语来构建各种量化算法。作者将25种常见量化器重新表达为这些原语的流水线组合，并开源了VQ-bench基准测试工具以推动量化算法的发展和可复现评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量量化虽然是一个经典问题，但近年来因成为AI基础设施的核心组件而再次受到广泛关注，产生了大量工程和研究活动。然而，缺乏统一的框架来开发和评估新的量化算法，导致各方法之间难以进行比较和复用。

Method: 作者提出了7种常见的概念化量化原语（conceptual quantization primitives），建立了一个统一框架，支持将这些原语任意组合成完整的量化算法流水线。然后将25种已有量化器分解并重新表达为这些原语的组合，验证了框架的表达能力。最后发布了开源的VQ-bench基准测试平台。

Result: 完成了25种常见量化器的统一框架表达，验证了7种原语的组合表达能力，并发布了开源的VQ-bench基准测试工具用于可复现的量化算法评估。

Conclusion: 该工作为向量量化领域提供了一个统一的抽象框架和公开可扩展的基准测试平台，有助于标准化量化算法的开发流程、促进算法之间的公平比较，并推动整个AI基础设施中向量量化技术的进一步发展。

Abstract: Vector quantization is an old problem but has recently become central to AI infrastructure. It is therefore experiencing a surge of renewed engineering and research activity. This paper provides a unified framework for developing and benchmarking new quantization algorithms. We describe 7 common conceptual quantization primitives and show how to compose them arbitrarily. We then re-express 25 common quantizers as pipelines of these primitives. Finally, we publish VQ-bench as open-source to be extended further and make reproducible benchmarks publicly available.

</details>


### [203] [RecSys Factory: Bounding LLM Agent Autonomy to Decision Points in the Industrial Recommender Lifecycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11241)
*Dongyang Ao,Kaixiang Fang,Shijie Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RecSys Factory，一个用于工业推荐系统运维的LLM智能体平台，解决了自主性-确定性-效率三难困境。平台在腾讯三个异构推荐业务线部署78天，通过将运行时解构为三种宿主事件源（零CPU等待）、能力解构为29个技能文件与400条目PitfallStore、并保留人在决策环中，实现了1,624次CLI调度78.6%的聚合成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将LLM智能体部署到工业推荐系统运维中面临三向张力：通用自主性（解释操作者意图、零样本生成胶水代码）、工业确定性（符合模式的特性提取、非崩溃A/B测试、零合规幻觉）与端到端效率之间的三难困境，三者同时最大化不可行，需要明确的设计解构方案。

Method: 提出三个解构维度：(1) 运行时解构为三个宿主发出的事件源（Claude Code Stop钩子、企业IM webhook、调度器API），等待阶段无常驻守护进程、零CPU消耗；(2) 能力解构为29文件技能生态（8,971行SKILL.md），技能陷阱表机械编译为400条目PitfallStore，将自主性限制在预提交流水线内的有界类型化决策面；(3) 通过人类在环卡片协议保留诊断与执行边界的人为决策，作为模式验证、幂等、可重放的审计轨迹原语。

Result: 平台在腾讯三个标签语义、A/B层拓扑和操作者画像均不相同的业务线部署78天，记录1,624次CLI工具调度，聚合成功率78.6%；两线观察到入职时间压缩（作为案例观察而非泛化主张）；进行了8天16次运行的人机协作试点。

Conclusion: 设计原则是"在决策点而非流水线上赋予自主性"，通过运行时、能力、人机边界的系统化解构，可以在工业级确定性要求下平衡效率与自主性；同时作者严谨地将部署观察限定为案例研究而非受控泛化结论。

Abstract: Deploying LLM agents into industrial recommender operations exposes a three-way tension we frame as the autonomy-determinism-efficiency trilemma: general autonomy (interpreting operator intent, generating glue code zero-shot), industrial determinism (schema-conforming feature extraction, non-crashing A/B, zero compliance-path hallucination), and end-to-end efficiency. Any two can be maximized against the third. We present RecSys Factory, an LLM-agent platform deployed for 78 days across three heterogeneous Tencent recommender business lines. The design principle is autonomy at decision points, not over pipelines, made concrete through three deconstructions that each discharge one vertex of the trilemma. Runtime is deconstructed into three host-emitted event sources (Claude Code Stop hooks, corporate-IM webhooks, workflow scheduler APIs): the platform carries no long-running daemon during the wait phase and consumes zero CPU during the 94% of wall-clock spent waiting on Spark or GPU jobs. Capability is deconstructed into a 29-file skill ecosystem (8,971 lines of SKILL.md) whose per-skill pitfall tables mechanically compile into a 400-entry PitfallStore, confining autonomy to bounded typed decision surfaces inside pre-committed pipelines. Deployment spans three business lines with disjoint label semantics, A/B layer topologies, and operator personas; an onboarding-time compression is observed on two of the three and is reported as a case-study observation, not a generalization claim, and not measured against a controlled pre-platform baseline. The human is retained at the diagnostic-versus-execution boundary via a human-in-the-loop card protocol, deployed as an audit-trail primitive (schema-validated, idempotent, replayable) and reported from an 8-day 16-run pilot. Across the 78-day window the platform recorded 1,624 CLI-tool dispatches at a 78.6% aggregate success rate.

</details>


### [204] [The Off-Support Barrier: Why Semantic Safety Constraints Are Not Learning-Problem Invariants, and What Follows for Prior Design, Containment, and Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11243)
*Yoshinori Watanabe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出安全约束（如"智能体不逃出沙箱"）在形式上属于"离支撑对象"（off-support object），即安全谓词 B 对于模型与数据分布生成的 σ-代数不可测，而奇异学习理论（SLT）中的实对数正则阈值（RLCT）是可测的。由此推导出一系列关于AI安全的结论，包括奖励黑客、沙箱逃逸、贝叶斯先验与软惩罚的局限性、硬件不变量与软性倾向的划分、形式验证的局部可证性，以及残差困难与执行预测和自我指涉功能动力学的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图为当代AI安全中的众多现象提供一个统一的结构性解释框架，而非零散的经验观察。通过将安全约束形式化为"离支撑对象"，利用奇异学习理论的数学工具，揭示安全问题的深层本质，并为工程实践（如形式验证与离线推理）提供理论依据。

Method: 该论文采用形式化/理论分析方法：首先定义安全谓词 B 不可关于 σ(模型, 数据分布) 可测，而 RLCT 可测，建立"非不变性"核心论点；然后从该论点作为推论而非独立观察推导出五类安全现象；最后以2026年7月OpenAI-Hugging Face评测事故作为具体案例验证框架，并提供Lean中的证明与数值实验代码。

Result: （i）从非不变性推导出奖励黑客与沙箱逃逸在基于结果的优化下必然出现；（ii）贝叶斯先验和软惩罚在奇异模型中杠杆作用差；（iii）硬性不变量应放在harness中，软性倾向放入模型；（iv）同一安全谓词可由形式验证在局部可靠地认证，类比LLC局部固定RLCT，但存在两个精确的不类比点；（v）残余困难（识别哪个离支撑区域重要）对应执行预测与自我指涉功能动力学，此时SLT分析机制失效。

Conclusion: 安全约束的离支撑本质决定了它无法通过模型内部的软性机制得到可靠保证，而必须借助外部harness与形式验证。SLT在静态分析上有力，但在面临自指涉/执行预测等动态问题时失效，这界定了AI安全理论工具的适用范围。

Abstract: We argue that a single structural fact organizes a wide range of phenomena in contemporary AI safety: a semantic safety constraint (e.g., the agent does not escape its sandbox) is an off-support object. Formally, if q is the data distribution and \(p(\cdot\mid w)\) the model, the safety predicate B is not measurable with respect to \(σ(\text{model}, q)\), whereas the real log-canonical threshold (RLCT) of singular learning theory (SLT) is. From this non-invariance we derive, as corollaries rather than independent observations: (i) why reward hacking and sandbox escape arise under outcome-based optimization; (ii) why encoding such constraints through Bayesian prior design or soft penalty weighting has poor leverage in singular models; (iii) why hard invariants belong in the harness and soft dispositions in the model; (iv) why the same B is nonetheless soundly and locally certifiable by formal verification, exactly as the local learning coefficient (LLC) locally pins the same RLCT --- with two precise points of disanalogy; and (v) why the residual difficulty, identifying which off-support region matters, coincides with performative prediction and self-referential functional dynamics, where SLT's analytic machinery breaks down. We use the July 2026 OpenAI--Hugging Face evaluation incident as the motivating case. Numerical experiments code and related proofs in lean are available at https://github.com/xiangze/Preventing_Jailbreak_as_regularization

</details>


### [205] [Towards the Harness of Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11246)
*Qi Wang,Tianyi Wang,Chengyang Li,Shikun Ban,Yurun Chen,Yizhong Ge,Jason Qin,Chengtai Li,Wentao Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Thea，一个将编码代理的"harness"范式扩展到物理世界具身代理的系统框架。Thea 通过"场景图作为上下文"和"评估作为退出代码"两个核心设计，弥合了软件世界与物理世界之间的关键差距（读取世界状态与判断行动结果），从而支持机器人在真实环境中通过工具组合完成长时程任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理的成功表明，代理的成就不仅取决于模型本身，还取决于围绕它的基础设施（harness）。作者质疑这一范式是否能扩展到物理世界的具身代理，并试图将编码代理的核心组件改造后应用于真实机器人系统。

Method: Thea 是一个 harness 系统，其代理循环将机器人能力封装为可调用的工具。它继承了编码代理的核心组件，并根据物理世界需求进行修改。针对物理世界无法免费提供两种能力（读取世界状态、判断行动结果）的问题，Thea 引入两个核心机制：1) Scene Graph as Context，一个持久化、符号化的世界表示；2) Evaluation as Exit Codes，用于检测行动终止条件、判定行动是否成功，并在失败时诊断原因，从而闭合代理与物理世界之间的循环。

Result: 在真实环境中，丰富的机器人行为从工具组合中涌现，而闭合循环使长时程任务得以完成。

Conclusion: Thea 证明编码代理的 harness 范式可以成功扩展到物理世界的具身代理，通过"场景图作为上下文"和"评估作为退出代码"两个机制弥补了物理世界与软件世界的根本差异，使复杂长时程行为可以在真实环境中涌现。

Abstract: The success of coding agents has established the harness as a paradigm: what an agent achieves depends not on the model alone, but on the infrastructure around it. We ask whether the same paradigm extends to embodied agents in the physical world. We present Thea, a harness in which an agentic loop orchestrates robot capabilities, each wrapped as a callable tool. It inherits the core components of coding agents, modified as the physical world requires. The world, however, withholds two abilities that software grants for free: reading the state of the world, and judging the outcome of an action. To bridge these gaps, Thea introduces Scene Graph as Context, a persistent, symbolic representation of the world, and Evaluation as Exit Codes, which detects when an action should terminate, judges whether it succeeded, and on failure diagnoses the cause. Together they close the loop between the agent and the physical world. Rich behaviors then emerge from the composition of tools, and the closed loop carries long-horizon tasks to completion in real environments.

</details>


### [206] [BEST-KAG: Enhancing Question Answering of Building Engineering Standards with Multimodal Knowledge Graph Modeling and Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11244)
*Jia-Rui Lin,Junxi Guo,Keyin Chen,Peng Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了BEST-KAG，一个面向建筑工程标准知识问答的多模态知识增强生成框架。该框架通过多模态知识图谱（MKG）统一表示文档层次结构和异构标准知识，采用规则与大语言模型混合的知识构建流水线实现可扩展的多模态知识提取，并基于图谱检索的知识增强生成架构实现条款级可追溯问答。实验表明其在专家评估、BLEU和ROUGE等指标上优于多个主流大语言模型，最高提升达74.01%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有建筑标准应用流程依赖于基于关键词的文档检索和人工跨条款解读，无法可靠支持多条款推理、多模态知识利用以及可追溯的条款级证据关联，亟需一种更强大的知识驱动框架来满足建筑标准问答的复杂需求。

Method: 框架包含三个核心组件：1）多模态知识图谱（MKG），用于统一表示文档层次结构与异质标准知识及其各种关联；2）规则与大语言模型混合的知识构建流水线，实现可扩展的多模态知识提取，构建了包含251部建筑工程标准、171,652个节点和310,914条边的知识图谱；3）基于图谱检索的知识增强生成架构，支持条款锚定和可追溯的问答。

Result: 实验表明BEST-KAG在专家评估、BLEU和ROUGE等指标上持续优于多个主流大语言模型，相比基线模型最高改进达74.01%。

Conclusion: BEST-KAG通过多模态知识图谱和知识增强生成架构，有效解决了建筑标准问答中的多条款推理、多模态知识利用和可追溯证据关联问题，显著提升了问答性能。

Abstract: Construction standards are critical for building safety and sustainability. Existing standard application workflows rely on keyword-based document retrieval and manual cross-clause interpretation, which cannot reliably support multi-clause reasoning, multimodal knowledge utilization, or traceable clause-level evidence linkage. To address these limitations, this study develops a multimodal knowledge-driven framework that supports question answering on standard knowledge named BEST-KAG (Knowledge-Augmented Generation for Building Engineering STandards). The framework introduces 1) a multimodal knowledge graph (MKG) for unified representation of document hierarchy and heterogeneous standard knowledge with various connections, 2) a rule-LLM hybrid knowledge construction pipeline for scalable multimodal knowledge extraction, creating a large MAG with 251 building engineering standards, 171,652 nodes and 310,914 edges, and 3) a graph-retrieval-based knowledge-augmented generation architecture for clause-grounded and traceable question answering. Experiments demonstrate that BEST-KAG consistently outperforms multiple mainstream LLMs in terms of Expert evaluation, and metrics including BLEU, and ROUGE, with the best improvement up to 74.01% compared to the baselines.

</details>


### [207] [Towards Sustainable Learning in Online Education: A Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11245)
*Chaofan Zhai,Yicheng Song,Ravi Bapna,Junyao Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI-Tutor，一种基于强化学习的在线教育智能辅导模型，通过同时优化短期和长期学习成果来促进可持续学习。短期利用认知理论平衡新知识获取与旧知识巩固，长期通过建模学习者参与度来维持动力、减少辍学。在33,700名学习者的2300万条学习记录上进行实证评估，在参与度、知识保留和最终学习成果方面均优于最先进基线，并通过学习路径分析展示其对不同学习者的自适应策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线教育虽具备高度可扩展性和可及性，但普遍面临学习参与度低、长期学习效果不佳的问题，亟需一个能同时兼顾短期知识获取与长期学习动机维持的智能辅导系统。

Method: 采用强化学习框架构建AI-Tutor：短期层面基于认知理论引导学习者平衡新知识学习与旧知识强化；长期层面建模学习者参与度，据此制定策略以维持学习动机、降低辍学率。模型能够提供个性化学习指导。

Result: 在包含33,700名学习者和2300万条学习记录的大规模数据集上进行实证评估，AI-Tutor在参与度、知识保留和最终学习成果三个方面均持续优于当前最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: AI-Tutor能够根据不同学习者画像自适应调整辅导策略，提供以人为本的个性化支持，有效兼顾学习效果与持续性参与，为在线教育的可持续发展提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: Online education offers unprecedented scalability and accessibility to global learners from diverse backgrounds, but it often suffers from low engagement and poor long term learning effectiveness. To address these challenges, we introduce AI Tutor, a reinforcement learning based model designed to promote sustainable learning by optimizing both short and longterm learning outcomes. In the short term, AI-Tutor draws on cognitive theory to guide learners through a balance of acquiring new knowledge and reinforcing prior learning. In the long term, it models learner engagement to inform strategies that sustain motivation and reduce dropout. These enhancements enable AI-Tutor to provide personalized guidance that fosters both effective learning and sustained participation. Empirical evaluations on 23 million learning records from 33,700 learners show that AI Tutor consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across engagement, knowledge retention, and final learning outcomes. Learning path analyses further reveal how AI-Tutor adapts its strategies to learners with diverse profiles, offering adaptive and human-centered support.

</details>


### [208] [Conformity Mitigations in Large Language Models Lie on a Single Resistance-Receptivity Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11247)
*Zafar Hussain,Kristoffer Nielbo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了多语言模型协作场景中同行意见对模型自身参数化知识的替代效应。通过对23个开源模型、19种条件和3个数据集的超过一百万次评分响应，发现一致的错误多数会使模型原本正确的答案出现显著反转（MMLU上22.8%，GPQA上54.8%，SimpleQA上71.0%）。现有缓解方法仅关注"阻抗性"（保持正确答案的能力），而忽略了协作代理同样需要的"接受性"（采纳正确同行答案的能力）。研究揭示大多数方法在提升阻抗性的同时牺牲接受性，形成一条R²在0.80-0.90之间的权衡曲线，唯有"推理"方法能同时提升两者，是唯一能使两项指标共同改善的干预手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多语言模型协作（多代理）设置的日益普及，模型会看到其他代理的答案后才作答，同行意见与模型自身的参数化知识形成竞争。错误的多数意见可能使模型推翻自己本可答对的答案，这一"意见位移"现象严重威胁协作系统的可靠性。现有研究几乎只关注提升模型的"阻抗性"（抵抗错误同行压力的能力），但这只是协作代理所需能力的一半——代理同样需要"接受性"（接受正确同行答案的能力）。作者旨在系统量化这一权衡，并寻找能同时改善两个维度的干预方法。

Method: 作者在23个开源权重模型、19种条件和3个数据集（MMLU、GPQA、SimpleQA）上进行了大规模实验，获得超过一百万条评分响应。他们提出双轴评估框架：Resistance（阻抗性，模型在错误多数压力下保留正确回答的比率）和Receptivity（接受性，模型在初始答错后采纳正确同行答案的比率）。他们评估了六种缓解方法（四种来自既有文献，两种为自创），分别测量其在两个轴上的表现，并拟合了Resistance-Receptivity的前沿权衡曲线。

Result: 一致的错误多数能反转模型22.8%的正确MMLU答案、54.8%的GPQA答案和71.0%的SimpleQA答案，其中84-89%的被反转答案与同行答案一致。六种方法在阻抗性和接受性之间呈现明显的权衡关系，所有方法均值落在R²为0.80-0.90的单一前沿线上。最强的已发表方法"反思"（Reflection）在MMLU上提升7.9点阻抗性但损失15.3点接受性。"推理"（Reasoning）是唯一例外：在GPQA和SimpleQA上与其他方法一样存在权衡，但在MMLU中模型能自行推导答案的科目上，它同时将阻抗性提升7.2点、接受性提升9.6点，是唯一能同时改善两个维度的干预手段。

Conclusion: 多代理LLM协作中，同行意见对模型正确答案的破坏性位移现象被严重低估，现有缓解方法普遍面临阻抗性与接受性之间的根本性权衡。这些方法在教模型"坚持己见"的同时也使其变得"对正确意见充耳不闻"。唯一的突破是推理干预——在模型具备自主推导能力的问题域中，它能同时提升模型抵抗错误压力和接受正确意见的能力。这提示未来提升协作模型可靠性的方向应聚焦于增强模型的推理与推导能力，而非简单的"坚持"或"顺从"策略。

Abstract: Recent advances in language models have enabled collaborative settings in which multiple models leverage one another's capabilities, iteratively improving, transforming, and extending each other's outputs. Each agent sees what the others assert before it answers, so peer opinion competes with the model's own parametric knowledge, and a wrong majority can overturn an answer the model would otherwise get right. We measure that displacement in 23 open-weight models, 19 conditions, and three datasets, yielding more than a million graded responses. A unanimous wrong majority reverses 22.8% of a model's correct MMLU answers, 54.8% on GPQA and 71.0% on SimpleQA, and 84-89% of the reversed answers match the peers' answers. Existing mitigations aim to increase Resistance, the rate at which a model keeps its correct answer under this pressure, which is only half of what a collaborating agent needs. We pair it with Receptivity, the rate at which a model adopts a correct peer answer after initially answering incorrectly. We score six methods on both axes, four drawn from prior work and two of our own. Each gains Resistance only by losing Receptivity, and their means fall on a single Resistance-Receptivity frontier with $R^2$ between 0.80 and 0.90. Reflection, the strongest published method, gains 7.9 points of MMLU Resistance and gives up 15.3 of Receptivity. Reasoning is the one exception. On GPQA and SimpleQA it trades like the rest, but on the MMLU subjects whose answers a model can derive for itself it raises Resistance by 7.2 points and Receptivity by 9.6 at once, the only intervention we find that improves both.

</details>


### [209] [EvoGraph-Mem: Failure-Aware Editable Graph Memory for Long-Term Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11248)
*Yuxi Qian,Yuxiang Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于可编辑洞察图的失败感知记忆维护框架，用于解决长期语言智能体记忆中洞察质量随时间退化（过时、过度泛化或有害）导致的记忆污染问题，通过在节点上追踪正负证据和激活状态，并引入效用感知检索和图表控制器进行记忆更新，实验证明该方法优于现有记忆增强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强智能体主要关注存储和检索过去的经验，但存储的记忆质量会随时间退化——先前提炼的洞察可能在新任务上下文中变得过时、过度泛化或有害，反复使用会造成记忆污染。这一问题在长期交互和任务演化场景中尤为突出，而现有方法缺乏对洞察级记忆质量的系统维护机制。

Method: 提出基于可编辑洞察图的失败感知记忆维护框架：(1) 每个洞察节点记录正面证据、负面证据和激活状态，以区分可复用洞察与冲突或无效洞察；(2) 引入效用感知检索机制，优先选择高效用记忆；(3) 图表控制器在任务执行后更新记忆图，包括保留可靠洞察、归档无效洞察、修订过时洞察和添加新发现的可复用洞察。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在不同骨干模型上均一致优于代表性记忆增强智能体基线。消融实验进一步证明：仅追加（append-only）记忆不足以应对长期任务，而证据感知检索和图表级编辑能提升记忆可靠性和下游任务性能。

Conclusion: 洞察级记忆维护对长期语言智能体至关重要，通过追踪证据和动态编辑记忆图可以有效缓解记忆污染问题，提升长期任务中的可靠性。简单的追加式记忆方案无法满足长期任务需求，需要更精细的记忆维护机制。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for language agents operating across extended interactions and evolving tasks. Existing memory-augmented agents mainly focus on storing and retrieving past experience, but the quality of stored memories may degrade over time. In particular, previously distilled insights can become outdated, over-generalized, or harmful under new task contexts, causing memory pollution when repeatedly reused. To address this issue, we study insight-level memory maintenance for long-term language agents and propose a failure-aware memory maintenance framework based on an editable insight graph. Each insight node tracks positive evidence, negative evidence, and an activation state, enabling the agent to distinguish reusable insights from conflicting or invalid ones. We further introduce a utility-aware retrieval mechanism and a graph controller that updates the memory graph after task execution by keeping reliable insights, archiving invalid ones, revising outdated ones, and adding newly discovered reusable insights. Extensive experiments show that our method consistently outperforms representative memory-based agent baselines across different backbone models. Ablation studies further demonstrate that append-only memory is insufficient for long-horizon tasks, while evidence-aware retrieval and graph-level editing improve memory reliability and downstream task performance.

</details>


### [210] [AgonAlpha: Autonomous Alpha Discovery via Prompt Economy and Scalable Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11250)
*Weicheng Ye,Youran Sun,Xingyu Ren,Shunyao Yu,Chugang Yi,Haizhao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgonAlpha是一个自主alpha挖掘系统，它搜索经过验证的研究工件（假设、可执行表达式、平台证据、理由和审查状态）而非仅搜索公式。该系统首次结合了经过验证的工件搜索、具有重新执行和否决权的新鲜上下文对抗评审器，以及感知待处理任务的并行预算分配，并保留完整的公共证据链。在WorldQuant BRAIN上的独立部署中，五个用户和六个模型后端产生了SPECTACULAR级别的alpha，Fitness达9.50，Sharpe达3.48，并为每次提交保留了从提示到表达式的完整溯源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型可以提出许多合理的交易因子，但自主研究系统还必须分配评估预算、验证自身证据，并保留每个候选因子的产生过程。现有系统仅搜索公式本身，缺乏对研究工件全生命周期（从假设到审查状态）的系统性管理和验证。

Method: AgonAlpha采用三层核心机制：(1) 对冻结的研究工件（假设、可执行表达式、平台证据、理由、审查状态）进行搜索，而非仅搜索公式；(2) 引入具有重新执行和否决权的新鲜上下文对抗评审器，用于验证候选因子的证据质量；(3) 采用感知待处理任务的并行预算分配策略，优化评估资源的使用效率。同时保留每个提交从提示到表达式的完整溯源信息。

Result: 在WorldQuant BRAIN平台上的独立部署中，AgonAlpha在五个用户和六个模型后端上产生了SPECTACULAR级别的alpha，其中Fitness达到9.50，Sharpe达到3.48，且每次提交均保留了完整的提示到表达式溯源。

Conclusion: AgonAlpha证明了在alpha挖掘系统中整合工件搜索、对抗性验证和预算感知分配的有效性，为自主量化研究系统提供了一种可验证、可溯源的新架构范式。

Abstract: Language models can propose many plausible trading factors, but an autonomous research system must also allocate its evaluation budget, verify its own evidence, and preserve how each candidate was produced. We present AgonAlpha, an architecture that searches over frozen research artifacts---hypotheses, executable expressions, platform evidence, rationales, and review status---rather than formulas alone. To our knowledge, AgonAlpha is the first alpha-mining system to combine verified artifact search, a fresh-context adversarial reviewer with re-execution and veto authority, and pending-aware parallel budget allocation, together with a complete public evidence trail. Independent deployments on WorldQuant BRAIN produced SPECTACULAR-grade alphas across five users and six model backends, with Fitness reaching 9.50 and Sharpe reaching 3.48, while retaining prompt-to-expression provenance for every submission.

</details>


### [211] [Local verification cannot detect non-transportability: a cohomological theory of context preservation in agentic reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11252)
*Suyash Mishra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明基于局部验证的智能体安全机制在结构上是不完备的。利用Cech上同调建模证据链，指出路径独立推理等价于上链恰为精确上同调类，路径分歧对应第一Cech上同调类的和乐。Hodge分解将证据冲突分为梯度、旋度和调和三部分，关键定理是任何单纯形支持的局部一致性检查无法区分精确上链与调和扰动，检测需基于循环基的统计量。作者提出Ksetra流程，通过上边界投影估计并用调和分量门控弃权，并给出调和分量的机制解释（效应修饰与重叠特定人群构成）。证据网络自由度划分为校准、相干与传输三部分，给出全局主张存在性的精确F检验，并提供精度白化修正形式。外汇无套利基准上检验正确有界、能检测环形套利而忽略三角套利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI系统在生物、临床和金融等跨领域间传递结论，现有主流安全保障是"局部验证"——在每一步检查实体可表示性、参数兼容性和输出与计划的一致性。作者想探究这类局部验证是否足以确保全局推理正确性，发现其在结构上具有根本性缺陷。

Method: 以拓扑与代数方法建模：用覆盖的神经（nerve）表示上下文空间，用实值1-上链表示证据。智能体的证据链化过程被建模为路径积分，结论的路经独立性与上链的恰当性（exactness）等价。用Cech上同调刻画路径分歧（和乐），用Hodge分解将证据冲突分解为梯度（校准）、旋度（局部不一致、三重交叠可见）与调和三部分。构造Ksetra流程：通过上边界投影估计证据，用调和分量触发弃权；利用循环基上的统计量检测局部检查无法发现的调和扰动。在验证层面构造基于自由度分解的精确F检验，并推导精度白化版本恢复精确性。

Result: 核心结果：没有任何单纯形支撑的局部一致性检查族能够区分上链ω与ω+h（h为调和上链），上述调和分量仍会产生有效路径间的非零分歧，因此需要循环基上的统计量。Ksetra通过上边界投影估计并经调和分量门控弃权；调和分量被发现源自效应修饰与重叠特定人群构成的交互，在效应修饰缺失时降至机器精度为零。证据网络自由度分解为校准/相干/传输三部分，给出精确F检验；在不等精度下有失真，白化形式恢复精确性。在外汇基准中，无套利假设使上链恰为共边界：Ksetra检验尺寸正确、能检测环形套利、忽略三角套利。

Conclusion: 局部验证作为安全手段具有结构性的不完备性；调和上同调分量构成了局部检查无法触及的盲区。需借助全局的循环基统计量（如Ksetra）来弥补，并以外汇无套利场景为校准基准验证方法的正确性。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems routinely transport conclusions across biological, clinical and financial contexts, and the emerging safeguard is local verification: checking at each step that the entity is representable in the chosen tool, that parameters are compatible, and that outputs cohere with the plan. We prove this class of safeguard is structurally incomplete. Modelling a covering of context space by its nerve and evidence by a real-valued 1-cochain, an agent chaining evidence performs path integration: its conclusion is path-independent if and only if the cochain is exact, and disagreement between valid reasoning paths is exactly the holonomy of a first Cech cohomology class. Hodge decomposition partitions evidence conflict into a gradient part (calibration), a curl part (local inconsistency, visible at triple overlaps) and a harmonic part. Our central result is that no family of simplex-supported consistency checks can distinguish omega from omega+h for harmonic h, which nonetheless generates non-zero disagreement between valid paths; detection requires a statistic on a cycle basis. The resulting procedure, Ksetra, estimates by coboundary projection and gates abstention on the harmonic component, which we give a mechanism: it arises from effect modification combined with overlap-specific population composition, and vanishes to machine precision when effect modification is absent. The degrees of freedom of an evidence network partition into calibration, coherence and transport, yielding an exact F-test for the existence of a global claim; we quantify its distortion under unequal precision and supply the precision-whitened form that restores exactness. Foreign exchange, where the arbitrage-free null makes the cochain exactly a coboundary, serves as a calibration bench: the test is correctly sized, fires on loop arbitrage, and ignores triangular arbitrage.

</details>


### [212] [Symbolic Machine Learning for Vapor-Liquid Equilibrium Prediction in Cx-N2 Binary Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11255)
*Bongseok Kim,Suman Chakraborty,Gary Huang,Mehek Mathur,Guang Lin,Li Qiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种符号机器学习方法，从实验数据中发现可解释的符号修正项，用于改进Peng-Robinson状态方程（PR-EOS）对烃-氮体系气液相平衡（VLE）的预测精度，并采用两级策略（先对单个烃体系识别符号表达式，再将系数表示为碳数的函数）实现跨体系预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 立方状态方程（如PR-EOS）对烃-氮混合物在宽组成范围和烃链长范围内VLE预测精度不足；深度学习方法虽准确但缺乏可解释性和显式解析表达式。

Method: 提出两级符号机器学习策略：第一级为单个烃体系识别符号修正表达式；第二级将修正项系数表示为碳数的函数，从而推广到不同烃类体系。该方法从实验数据出发，发现可解释的符号修正项以校正PR-EOS预测。

Result: 在所有烃-氮体系中，该方法的预测精度较原始PR-EOS均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一个可解释的符号修正框架，能够有效改进PR-EOS对烃-氮体系VLE的预测能力，兼顾精度与解析表达式的可解释性。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of vapor--liquid equilibrium (VLE) for hydrocarbon-nitrogen mixtures remains challenging for cubic equations of state, particularly across broad ranges of composition and hydrocarbon chain length. While deep learning models can provide accurate predictions, they often lack interpretability and explicit analytical expressions. In this work, we propose a symbolic machine learning approach to discover interpretable symbolic corrections to Peng-Robinson equation-of-state (PR-EOS) predictions from experimental data. The proposed approach adopts a two-level strategy: symbolic expressions are first identified for individual hydrocarbon systems, after which their coefficients are represented as functions of carbon number to enable accurate prediction across different hydrocarbon systems. The results demonstrate significantly improved prediction accuracy over the original PR-EOS across all hydrocarbon-nitrogen systems. Overall, the proposed approach provides an interpretable symbolic correction framework for improving PR-EOS predictions of hydrocarbon-nitrogen VLE.

</details>


### [213] [Adaptive Hybrid Particle Swarm Optimization with Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11258)
*Aryan Gurudeo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应混合粒子群优化（AHPSO），通过sigmoid函数依据种群多样性自动调制梯度注入强度（探索期接近零、开发期接近最大），无需人工切换阶段。实验表明：在等预算比较下，PSO在52.5%配置中胜出而AHPSO仅20%（p=7.0e-5）；但在具有光滑局部盆地的问题上AHPSO优势明显。在迭代匹配比较中（29个函数、42种配置、14,700次运行），AHPSO-Adadelta在9种方法（含CMA-ES）中排名第一（p=9.75e-4）。论文的核心贡献是对梯度注入在群智能搜索中何时有价值的原理性刻画，而非声称普适优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将梯度注入与PSO结合时往往隐含假设其普遍有益，但作者观察到梯度注入仅在种群已识别出具有光滑局部结构的盆地时才有效，并非全局适用。因此需要一种自适应机制来根据搜索状态动态调控梯度影响，从而避免在探索期被不当梯度干扰。

Method: 提出AHPSO，核心是使用sigmoid函数将种群多样性映射为梯度注入权重：多样性高（探索阶段）时梯度影响趋近于零，多样性低（开发阶段）时梯度影响趋近于最大，全程无需人工阶段切换。实验采用两种比较协议：1) 预算归一化比较（PSO获得等量总函数评估次数）；2) 迭代匹配比较，跨29个函数、42种配置、14,700次运行，与CMA-ES等9种方法对比。

Result: 预算归一化比较中，PSO在40种配置里获胜52.5%，AHPSO仅20%（Friedman检验p=7.0e-5）；AHPSO仅在具有光滑局部盆地的问题（F8, F24-F27）上保持优势。迭代匹配比较中，AHPSO-Adadelta在9种方法中排名第一（p=9.75e-4）。

Conclusion: 梯度注入的价值是有条件的：它仅在种群已锁定光滑局部盆地时提供超越无向采样的优势。AHPSO的自适应机制能在不预设阶段切换的情况下自动平衡梯度影响，但研究旨在提供对梯度注入何时有用这一问题的原理性理解，而非声称其普适最优。

Abstract: Gradient injection helps Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO) only when the swarm has identified a basin with smooth local structure, not universally. We propose Adaptive Hybrid PSO (AHPSO), which uses a sigmoid function on swarm diversity to automatically modulate gradient influence: near-zero during exploration, near-maximum during exploitation, with no manual phase-switching. Under budget-normalized comparison (PSO given equivalent total function evaluations), PSO wins 52.5% of 40 configurations versus AHPSO's 20% (p = 7.0e-5, Friedman). AHPSO retains advantage specifically on problems with smooth local basins (F8, F24-F27) where directed descent outperforms undirected sampling even at equal cost. Under iteration-matched comparison across 29 functions (42 configurations, 14,700 runs), AHPSO-Adadelta ranks first of 9 methods including CMA-ES (p = 9.75e-4). The contribution is a principled characterization of when gradient injection provides value in swarm-based search, not a claim of universal superiority.

</details>


### [214] [Glance, Scrutinize, and Think: Advancing Video Anomaly Detection from Training-Free to Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11260)
*Shibo Gao,Peipei Yang,Xu-Yao Zhang,Linlin Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出视频异常检测(VAD)中"何时-何事"分离问题的统一解决方案：一是GtS(Glance then Scrutinize)无训练框架，通过静态与动态文本引导实现由粗到细的异常定位与理解；二是基于工具增强的智能体VAD方法，利用多模态大语言模型学会调用视频裁剪工具、检查密集重采样帧并自我纠正错误定位。同时扩展VAGU基准为VAGU-T(含7,567个真实视频、21类异常)，并引入联合评估语义可解释性与时间精度的JeAUG指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VAD方法存在"何时-何事"分离问题：传统DNN方法能定位异常何时发生但缺乏语义理解，而LLM方法能解释发生了什么却忽略了精确的时间定位。作者将此归因于缺乏统一的推理范式，并受人类检查监控视频方式(全局扫视形成时间假设、仔细检查可疑片段、迭代思考纠正错误)的启发，研究全局到局部的推理范式。

Method: (1) GtS框架：无训练方法，利用静态和动态文本引导进行由粗到细的异常定位与理解，平衡精度与速度；(2) 工具增强的智能体VAD方法：多模态大语言模型学习调用视频裁剪工具、检查密集重采样帧、自我纠正错误假设，采用冷启动监督微调加联合答案-定位奖励的强化学习；(3) 提出VAGU-T基准：从VAGU扩展，含7,567个真实视频、21类异常，有人工验证的定位、解释、QA对和思维链工具调用轨迹；(4) 提出JeAUG指标：联合评估语义可解释性和时间精度。

Result: 实验表明：GtS显著超越无训练的基线方法；智能体模型在精度和推理速度两方面均优于现有方法，实现了更高准确率和更快推断。

Conclusion: 全局到局部(global-to-local)的统一推理范式能有效弥合VAD中的"何时-何事"鸿沟。无训练的GtS提供了高效基线，而工具增强的智能体模型通过学习主动调用工具和自我纠正，突破了冻结外部模块的性能上限，兼顾了语义理解与时间定位能力。

Abstract: Video Anomaly Detection (VAD) aims to identify anomalous events and localize their temporal intervals. Existing approaches exhibit a "when-what" dissociation: traditional DNN-based methods localize when anomalies occur but lack semantic understanding, whereas LLM-based methods explain what happens but neglect precise temporal grounding. We attribute this to the absence of a unified reasoning paradigm. Inspired by how humans inspect surveillance videos - glancing globally to form temporal hypotheses, scrutinizing suspicious segments, and thinking iteratively to correct errors - we study this global-to-local paradigm from two perspectives. We first propose Glance then Scrutinize (GtS), a training-free framework using static and dynamic textual guidance for coarse-to-fine anomaly grounding and understanding, balancing accuracy and speed. To break the ceiling imposed by frozen external modules, we further propose a tool-augmented agentic VAD method, where a multimodal large language model learns to invoke a video cropping tool, inspect densely resampled frames, and self-correct mislocalized hypotheses, via cold-start supervised fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning with a joint answer-grounding reward. For training and evaluation, we extend our prior VAGU benchmark into VAGU-T (Video Anomaly Grounding, Understanding, and Thinking), comprising 7,567 real-world videos over 21 anomaly categories with human-validated grounding, explanations, QA pairs, and chain-of-thought tool-calling traces. We further introduce JeAUG, a metric jointly evaluating semantic interpretability and temporal precision. Experiments show that GtS substantially surpasses training-free baselines, while the agentic model delivers both higher accuracy and faster inference.

</details>


### [215] [Deployment Decision Reliability: A Generalizability-Theory Framework for Sizing Long-Horizon Agent Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11323)
*Vasundra Srinivasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过Generalizability Theory四面方差分解，证明现有agent排行榜（TheAgentCompany、τ²-bench、AppWorld）排名的是"专长"而非"通用能力"：agent主效应方差占比不足3%，而agent-by-task交互项占7-23%。据此提出Deployment Decision Reliability (DDR)报告规范，并开源全部代码与数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业从业者将agent排行榜误读为agent能力的直接排序，但实际上排行榜可能掩盖了任务交互等关键因素。作者旨在揭露排行榜真正衡量的内容，并为企业买方提供可辩护的部署决策依据。

Method: 采用四面Generalizability Theory方差分解，使用三种估计器（Henderson Method-I、lme4 REML、贝叶斯二项GLMM），在三个开放agent-trace基准（TheAgentCompany、τ²-bench、AppWorld）上分析，并结合MAST故障分类体系进行trace级与cell级的剖面分析。

Result: ① agent主效应占方差<3%，agent-by-task交互占7-23%；② 最难任务四分位上聚合信度崩溃（Eρ²从0.752降至0.000）；③ 训练cell信度与留出信度负相关（r=-0.90）；④ 群体指标可迁移（能力差距比稳定在0.35-0.40）但按家族agent排名反转；⑤ trace级故障模式剖面特异（MAE=0.261），cell级剖面可概化（MAE=0.056，r=0.83）。三种估计器结果一致到小数点后三位。

Conclusion: 排行榜衡量的是专长而非通用能力；聚合信度在困难任务上不可靠；最可信的训练设计反而复制最差。作者提出DDR（Deployment Decision Reliability）报告纪律，将方差成分表转化为企业买方可辩护的五项决策，并开源全部工件。

Abstract: Enterprise practitioners read agent leaderboards as if they ranked agent capability. We show, across three open agent-trace benchmarks (TheAgentCompany, $τ^2$-bench, and AppWorld), that the agent main effect accounts for less than 3% of total variance in every dataset and check type, while the agent-by-task interaction accounts for 7-23%. Leaderboards rank specialization, not capability. We arrive at this through a four-facet Generalizability Theory variance decomposition, fit with three estimators (Henderson Method-I, REML via lme4, and a Bayesian binomial GLMM) that agree to three decimal places. Four further findings sharpen what the leaderboard is hiding. First, aggregate reliability collapses on the hardest task quartile: $Eρ^2$ on $τ^2$ action_checks falls from 0.752 to 0.000. Second, training-cell reliability negatively correlates with held-out reliability ($r = -0.90$ on $τ^2$), meaning the designs that look most reliable replicate worst. Third, population-level diagnostics transfer across enterprise benchmarks (capability-gap ratio stable at 0.35-0.40) but per-family agent rankings invert. Fourth, on the MAST failure taxonomy, trace-level mode profiles are idiosyncratic (MAE = 0.261) while cell-level profiles generalise (MAE = 0.056, $r = 0.83$). We package these into Deployment Decision Reliability (DDR), a one-page reporting discipline that turns the variance-component table into five decisions an enterprise buyer can defend. All code, data loaders, and fit artifacts are released under an open-source license.

</details>


### [216] [Apodex Discovery: Reality Benchmarks and Environments for Evaluating and Building Discoverative Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11341)
*Brian Wang,Bin Feng,Xiaoman Pan,Chenyang An,Felix Liu,Tangqi Fang,Gongbo Sun,Lingfeng Shen,Ning Wang,Handuo Zhang,Feng Chen,Fuchao Yang,Xiang Wang,Jiacheng Lin,Siting Li,Zixuan Liu,Chi Han,Zhenhailong Wang,Kunlun Zhu,Lawrence Zhao,Yueqi Guo,Kailong Wen,Feng Xing,Yiling Guo,Lidong Bing,David Tan,Bo An,Heng Ji,Sheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Apodex Discovery是一个用于构建和评估"发现性AI"的框架，核心是重型求解器（heavy-duty solver），包含问题侦察、环境-任务-情节抽象和六维度评估三大组件。在AAV衣壳设计中超越现有最优水平7%，在药物重定位与改方中分别将GPT-5.5和GPT-5.6-sol的预测得分提升2.5和7.6分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型擅长解决已明确指定问题、工具和成功标准的任务，但真实世界中的重大挑战往往不以便于执行或可验证的形式呈现。借鉴阿波罗登月计划从愿景转向"目标-仿真-验证-修正"的任务架构经验，AI需要类似的转型框架来应对不可执行、不可验证的现实问题。

Method: 引入Apodex Discovery框架，包含三部分：(1) 问题侦察过程：调查561个行业、16个部门，整理423个高价值现实问题，选出20个作为初始版本；(2) 通用的环境-任务-情节抽象，提供数据、工具、约束、反馈、轨迹记录以及中间产物和最终提交的验证；(3) HDS6评估体系，独立评估工具使用（Tools）、修复（Repair）、替代方案（Alternatives）、连贯性（Coherence）、证据（Evidence）和范围（Scope），不依赖最终任务是否成功。

Result: 在AAV衣壳设计任务中，Apodex在可行性、趋向性、结构预测和生成设计方面超越已发表的最先进水平7%。在药物重定位和改方任务中，任务特定的生物医学环境将GPT-5.5和GPT-5.6-sol的平均归一化预测得分分别提升2.5和7.6点（相比同一封闭知识模型基线）。受控消融实验表明，固定的TRACES情节接口能将性能差异归因到具体求解器组件。

Conclusion: Apodex Discovery将AI评估从预定义基准转向可验证的真实发现性调查，其可归因的评估接口和分维度评估体系为构建能够应对现实世界未定义问题的AI系统提供了方法论框架，标志着AI向"发现科学"方向的范式演进。

Abstract: Apollo did not reach the Moon merely because its engineers could solve difficult equations. It succeeded by turning a distant ambition into a mission architecture of explicit objectives, simulation, verification, and repeated correction. AI now faces a similar transition: frontier models can solve difficult tasks once the problem, tools, and success criteria are specified, yet consequential real-world challenges rarely arrive in an executable or verifiable form.
  We introduce Apodex Discovery, a framework for building and evaluating discoverative AI through the heavy-duty solver, a system comprising a foundation model, harness, tools, and control policies that pursues extended, stateful, verifiable investigations. It has three core components. First, a problem-scouting process surveyed 561 industries across 16 sectors, assembled 423 high-value real-world problems, and selected 20 for the initial release. Second, a common environment-task-episode abstraction provides data, tools, constraints, feedback, trajectory recording, and verification of intermediate artifacts and final submissions. Third, HDS6 evaluates Tools, Repair, Alternatives, Coherence, Evidence, and Scope independently of final-task success.
  In AAV capsid design, Apodex surpassed the published state of the art by 7% across viability, tropism, structure prediction, and generative design. In drug repurposing and reformulation, a task-specific biomedical environment improved the mean normalized prediction score of GPT-5.5 and GPT-5.6-sol by 2.5 and 7.6 points over the same closed-book backbone. Controlled ablations show that the fixed TRACES episode interface enables attribution of performance differences to specific solver components. Apodex Discovery moves AI evaluation beyond predefined benchmarks toward verifiable investigations aimed at genuine discovery.

</details>


### [217] [Can Frontier LLMs Match Natively Multimodal Embeddings? A Comparison on Hard-Negative Text-to-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11343)
*Archan Dutta,Vyanktesh Kanungo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次在Flickr30k数据集上直接对比了原生多模态嵌入模型（Gemini Embedding 2）与基于LLM的视觉排序方法。研究发现GPT-4.1和Claude Sonnet 4.6在多模态检索任务上与Gemini Embedding 2表现相当，但预计算嵌入后的多模态嵌入模型在低延迟场景下更具优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Gemini Embedding 2等原生多模态嵌入模型的发布，以及前沿LLM展现出强大的视觉理解能力，多模态检索领域需要明确这两种不同技术路线的优劣。此前缺乏对原生多模态嵌入与LLM零样本排序能力的直接系统比较。

Method: 在Flickr30k数据集上，将Gemini Embedding 2（原生多模态嵌入模型）与GPT-4.1和Claude Sonnet 4.6（作为零样本视觉排序器）进行直接的端到端性能对比评估，涵盖文本、图像等多种媒体类型的检索与分类任务。

Result: GPT-4.1和Claude Sonnet 4.6的检索性能与Gemini Embedding 2相当（on par）；但在嵌入预计算后，多模态嵌入模型在低延迟应用场景中更为适用。

Conclusion: 多模态检索存在两条可行路径：LLM驱动的零样本排序在性能上可与专用多模态嵌入模型媲美，而原生多模态嵌入在延迟敏感场景下更具部署优势。未来工作需关注两者的权衡与互补。

Abstract: Multimodal retrieval and classification across different types of media, spanning text, images,video and audio, has traditionally relied on dual-encoder models that align visual and textual representations through contrastive learning. The March 2026 release of Gemini Embedding 2, Google's first natively multimodal embedding model to map text, images, video, audio, and documents into a single shared space, raises competition among multimodal retrieval systems. Simultaneously, frontier Large language models (LLMs) have also demonstrated strong visual understanding, raising the question of whether they can serve as effective zero-shot rankers. Our study provides the first direct comparison of native multimodal embeddings against LLM-based visual ranking on Flickr30k. We observe that GPT-4.1 and Claude Sonnet 4.6 perform on par with Gemini Embedding 2. Additionally, once embeddings are precomputed, multimodal embeddings are better suited for low-latency applications.

</details>


### [218] [Inverse Theory of Mind Modeling for Content Recommendation: From Web Browsing to Dynamic Intelligent Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11354)
*Mengyu Chen,Feiyu Lu,Chun-Fu Chen,Lucas Vinh Tran,Jay Katukuri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出反向心智理论（Inverse Theory of Mind, IToM）管道，从用户行为观测中反向推理其信念、偏好和决策特质，生成结构化用户画像。在OPeRA数据集上验证，结果显示推理出的画像在多个任务上匹配或超越真实画像，多假设推理对人格预测至关重要，并在VisionOS上实现了跨模态的空间银行应用验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推荐系统将用户行为视为偏好的可靠代理，但实际上交互往往反映探索或比较行为而非稳定的偏好表达。随着界面从静态布局向生成式UI和沉浸式XR演变，系统需要更深层、跨模态的用户理解——不仅要决定呈现什么，还要决定在哪里、何时、以何种显著性呈现，以及最重要的是用户为何行动。

Method: 提出IToM管道：（1）重构每位用户的决策上下文，包括所选项和可用替代项；（2）应用LLM驱动的反事实推理生成基于证据的自然语言信念陈述；（3）通过多假设溯因推理将这些信念综合为结构化用户画像。采用从观测交互反向推理以推断解释行为的信念、偏好和决策特质。

Result: 在OPeRA数据集上针对四项任务（下一动作预测、购物态度对齐、大五人格推理、保留类目预测）进行评估，结果显示：推理出的画像匹配或超越ground-truth画像；多假设推理对人格预测的准确性至关重要；并跨模态展示了基于画像驱动的空间银行应用在VisionOS上的可行性。

Conclusion: 反向心智理论管道能够从行为观测中生成与人工评估画像相当甚至更优的用户画像，多假设溯因推理是准确推断人格特质的关键，且该方法具有良好的跨模态迁移能力，适用于生成式UI和XR等自适应环境。

Abstract: Modern recommender systems treat observed actions as reliable proxies for user preferences, yet interactions often reflect exploration or comparison rather than stable preference expression. As interfaces evolve from static layouts toward generative UIs and immersive extended reality (XR), the need for deeper, modality-agnostic user understanding grows: these adaptive environments must decide not only what to present but where, when, how prominently, and most importantly why a user acts. We propose an Inverse Theory of Mind (IToM) pipeline that reasons backward from observed interactions to infer the beliefs, preferences, and decision-making traits that explain behavior. The pipeline reconstructs each user's decision context, including what was chosen and what alternatives were available, applies LLM-driven counterfactual reasoning to produce evidence-grounded natural-language belief statements, and synthesizes these beliefs through multi-hypothesis abductive inference into a structured user persona. We evaluate on the OPeRA dataset against ground-truth personality assessments, attitudinal surveys, and interview-based personas across four tasks: next action prediction, shopping attitude alignment, Big Five personality inference, and held-out category prediction. Results show that inferred personas match or exceed ground-truth personas and that multi-hypothesis reasoning is essential for accurate personality prediction. We further demonstrate cross-modal transferability with a persona-driven spatial banking application on VisionOS.

</details>


### [219] [From Numbers to Judgment: Specialist LLM Agents and Reinforcement Learning for European Listed Real Estate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11381)
*Pardis Taghavi,Santosh Bhavani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究金融分析中的本地化数值操作与整合性判断是否受益于相同的LLM专业化形式。Larix将16镜头欧洲上市房地产分析框架映射为8个镜头对齐的专家模型；实验表明，提示词层面的专家分解使数值任务提升15.8个百分点但在判断任务上无效或有害；而使用GRPO对Qwen3.5-9B进行任务对齐的结构化奖励后训练，则在判断任务上提升14.2个百分点，且对未见公司和监管框架均有正迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究金融分析中两类本质不同的任务（局部数值运算 vs 整合性判断）是否需要不同形式的LLM专业化——提示词层面的分解（prompt-level decomposition）还是参数层面的适应（parameter adaptation）。现有的LLM专业化研究通常将专业化视为整体方案，缺乏对任务类型差异的精细刻画。

Method: 1) Larix框架：将16镜头欧洲上市房地产分析框架映射为8个镜头对齐的专家；2) 对照实验：固定模型、源证据、任务指令、输出schema和评分，比较前沿LLM的单一提示 vs 专家分解提示；3) 覆盖19家公司、7种监管包装、4次冻结模板派遣；4) 单智能体对照（完整框架单一提示）；5) 后训练：用GRPO和任务对齐的结构化奖励微调Qwen3.5-9B；6) 在开发集及4个反记忆化分割（未见公司、未见监管包装等）上评估。

Result: 分解提示在数值任务聚合上提升15.8个百分点，但在判断任务上不可靠甚至降低性能；单智能体对照无法复现数值增益。GRPO + 结构化奖励后训练使开发集数值评分提升12.0点、判断聚合提升14.2点，并在全部4个次天花板任务上均有增益；迁移到未见公司（整体+15.2点；契约压力测试+40.4点）和未见监管包装（+4.3点），在全部3个反记忆化分割上呈正迁移。

Conclusion: 提示词层面的专家分解有利于模块化数值执行，而针对性的参数适应有利于整合性金融判断。两种专业化形态适用于不同类型任务，为LLM在金融分析领域的任务感知式专业化设计提供了实证依据。

Abstract: We study whether the localized numerical operations and integrative judgments of financial analysis benefit from the same form of LLM specialization. Larix maps a 16-lens European listed-real-estate analysis framework to eight lens-aligned specialists; we compare a frontier LLM under monolithic versus specialist-decomposed prompting while holding the model, source evidence, task instructions, output schema, and scoring fixed. Across 19 firms spanning seven regulatory wrappers, decomposition improves the numerical-task aggregate by 15.8 percentage points but does not reliably improve, and can reduce, performance on judgment tasks, a pattern stable across four frozen-template dispatches; a single-agent control given the complete framework does not reproduce the numerical gain. Post-training Qwen3.5-9B with GRPO using task-aligned structured rewards then raises the development-split score by 12.0 points and the judgment aggregate by 14.2 points, with gains on all four sub-ceiling tasks; the gains transfer to unseen firms (+15.2 points overall; +40.4 on covenant stress) and to unseen regulatory wrappers (+4.3), with positive transfer on all three anti-memorization splits. Prompt-level decomposition thus improves modular numerical execution, whereas targeted parameter adaptation improves integrative financial judgment.

</details>


### [220] [When Self-Consistency Backfires: Majority Vote Hurts the Majority of Hard Science Problems for Small LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11403)
*Utkarsh Bahuguna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在GPQA Diamond基准上，多数投票（Self-consistency）对两个指令微调模型（Qwen2.5-7B和Llama-3-8B）在大多数问题（56.6%和65.7%）上反而降低了准确率。即使理论上有网格oracle可提升最多14-17个准确率点，但所有无验证器门控方法（plurality-agreement门控和token-entropy门控）都无法实现该提升。根本机制是置信度在这些问题上不追踪正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Self-consistency通过多数投票是广泛使用的推理时计算分配方法，但作者质疑其在真实困难基准上的有效性。此前研究多在较简单任务上验证SC的优势，作者希望在GPQA Diamond这一研究生级别科学问题上严格检验，并探究置信度与正确性的关系。

Method: 在198题的GPQA Diamond基准上对Qwen2.5-7B和Llama-3-8B进行系统实验：先于47个探索性问题观察效应，再在151个确认性分裂问题预注册四项假设并全部通过。测试多个N值（1,2,4,8,16,32,64），对比多数投票、网格oracle上界路线、plurality-agreement门控和token-entropy门控。

Result: 多数投票在大多数问题上降低而非提高准确率（Qwen为56.6%问题下降，Llama为65.7%）；网格oracle理论上限比N=1高14点（Qwen）和17点（Llama），但两种无验证器门控均无法达到该上界，准确率变化不超过0.002。在最高一致性bin中，Qwen的多数答案正确率仅约一半，Llama最高一致性bin准确率甚至低于最低一致性bin。

Conclusion: 在有难度的基准上，self-consistency的多数投票并不保证优于单次采样；置信度（链间一致性）与正确性在这些问题上不相关甚至负相关。该发现仅限于小规模指令微调模型，未测试推理原生（reasoning-native）模型，这是关键的开放问题。

Abstract: Self-consistency (SC) via majority vote is a widely used way to spend inference-time compute: sample N chains of thought, return the plurality answer. On the full GPQA Diamond benchmark (198 graduate-level science questions), majority voting reduces per-problem accuracy on a majority of problems for two instruction-tuned models from different families: 56.6% of problems for Qwen2.5-7B and 65.7% for Llama-3-8B, with Qwen the primary demonstration and Llama corroborating the direction from a near-chance baseline. The effect was pre-registered on a 151-problem confirmatory split after being observed on 47 exploratory problems, and all four confirmatory hypotheses passed. A grid oracle that routes each problem to the best N across {1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64} marks a theoretical upper bound 14 accuracy points above N = 1 for Qwen and 17 for Llama, an oracle bound requiring ground truth rather than a deployable method. No verifier-free gate reaches it: neither a plurality-agreement gate nor a token-entropy gate moves accuracy more than 0.002 from fixed-budget voting at N = 64. The mechanism is direct: confidence does not track correctness on these problems. In the highest-agreement bin the plurality answer is correct about half the time for Qwen, and for Llama that bin is less accurate than its lowest-agreement bin. We pre-register and confirm these findings on small instruction-tuned models; we do not test reasoning-native models, which we flag as the central open question.

</details>


### [221] [Social Chain of Thought: A Multi-Agent Architecture Grounded in Medical Differential Diagnosis Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11420)
*Del Coburn,Scott Sanner,Dan Silver*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Social Chain of Thought (SCoT)，一种用于医学鉴别诊断的多轮多智能体协作推理框架，通过结构化多智能体交互实现了优于单智能体推理的召回率，尤其在最具挑战性的诊断案例中表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学诊断推理是LLM的高影响力应用场景，OpenAI报道全球超过5%的ChatGPT消息涉及医疗健康，因此系统透明性成为重要设计考量。现有研究提出多智能体医学诊断方法，但缺乏对"何时需要此类系统、为何有效、在何处优于单次推理"的清晰理解。

Method: SCoT将多智能体互动构建为协商式推理框架，通过多轮对话模拟专家协作进行鉴别诊断。研究将SCoT与单智能体基线、单智能体流水线消融以及best-of-n扩展方案进行系统对比评估。

Result: SCoT的召回率优势无法通过单一的整体推理（monolithic inference）复现。SCoT在最困难的诊断案例中表现最佳，多轮专家对话有助于恢复真实诊断并收敛到更高召回率的鉴别诊断列表。

Conclusion: 多智能体协作框架在复杂医学鉴别诊断中具有独特价值，尤其是面对高难度病例时，结构化多轮专家对话能够超越单体推理的性能上限，为LLM在医疗诊断中的透明性和可靠性提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Medical diagnostic reasoning is a high-impact use case for LLMs that carries significant implications for the health and wellbeing of users. When OpenAI (2026) reports that more than 5% of ChatGPT messages globally are healthcare-related, the transparency of these systems becomes a serious design concern. This is especially true for complex cases, where differential diagnosis often requires integrating multiple forms of specialist reasoning. Existing work has proposed multi-agent approaches to medical diagnosis, but it remains unclear when such systems are needed, why they help, and where they outperform monolithic inference. We introduce Social Chain of Thought (SCoT),a multi-round pipeline for medical differential diagnosis that structures multi-agent interaction as a deliberative framework for collabora. tive LLM reasoning. Evaluating SCoT against single-agent baselines, one-agent pipeline ablations, and best-of-n scaling, we show that its recall advantage is not reproduced by monolithic inference alone. SCoT is most successful in the hardest diagnostic cases, where multiple rounds of specialist conversation help recover ground-truth diagnoses and converge on a higher-recall differential.

</details>


### [222] [Benchmarking LLM Judges for Mobile Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11434)
*Ziqiang Wan,Li Gu,Zhixiang Chi,Zhi Liu,Seyed Mehdi Ayyoubzadeh,Yuanhao Yu,Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MobileJudgeBench是一个系统评估LLM作为评判器在移动智能体轨迹上可靠性的基准，包含931条人工标注轨迹和6种评判方法。实验发现：简单基线评判器与专门定制方法相当甚至更优，LLM主干是主要驱动力；基准质量指标能可靠预测真实场景中的评判器效用；不同LLM后端呈现性质相反的失败模式（保守型与宽容型）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动智能体基准越来越依赖LLM评判器来评估任务完成度，但这些评判器在移动智能体轨迹上的可靠性尚未被系统检验，缺乏一个专门的基准来衡量评判器质量。

Method: 构建MobileJudgeBench：涵盖931条人工标注轨迹，覆盖6个移动智能体基准、4个智能体模型和68个应用；评估6种评判方法（从SPA-Bench、A3、AndroidArena、AgentRewardBench改编的5种方法加上一个简单的采样子截图基线）在多个LLM后端上的表现。

Result: 三个关键发现：(1)简单采样子截图基线具有竞争力且常超越专门构建的方法，说明更复杂的评判流程并非持续提升质量，LLM骨干才是主要驱动因素；(2)基准质量指标能可靠预测实际效用，与智能体排名保真度和作为奖励信号用于强化学习的下游性能均相关；(3)两个LLM后端的失败分析揭示性质相反的失败画像——保守型和宽容型，与主干网络的精确率-召回率特性相关。

Conclusion: 该工作表明移动智能体评判器研究中应更多关注LLM主干选择而非复杂流程设计，同时基准质量指标可作为可靠的评判器选择依据，且不同后端的失败模式差异需要在实践中加以考量。

Abstract: Mobile agent benchmarks increasingly rely on LLM-based judges to evaluate task completion, yet the reliability of these judges on mobile agent trajectories remains largely unexamined. We introduce MobileJudgeBench, a benchmark for systematically evaluating LLM-as-judge methods on mobile agent trajectories. Our benchmark comprises 931 human-annotated trajectories spanning 6 mobile agent benchmarks, 4 agent models, and 68 apps. We evaluate 6 judge methods (five adapted from SPA-Bench, A3 with two modes, AndroidArena, and AgentRewardBench, plus a simple baseline we design) across multiple LLM backends. Our experiments reveal three key findings. First, a simple baseline judge with sampled screenshots is competitive with, and often exceeds, purpose-built methods, indicating that more elaborate judge pipelines do not consistently improve judge quality; among competitive methods, the LLM backbone is the primary driver. Second, benchmark quality metrics reliably predict real-world judge utility: they correlate with both agent ranking fidelity for evaluation and downstream performance when judges serve as reward signals for on-policy reinforcement learning. Third, failure analysis across two LLM backends uncovers qualitatively opposite failure profiles, one conservative and the other permissive, linked to the backbone's precision-recall characteristics.

</details>


### [223] [A Modular Agentic Framework for Synthetically Constrained Multi-Objective Hit-to-Lead Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11483)
*Kelvin P. Idanwekhai,Enes Kelestemur,Benjamin Strickland,Matthew Hart,Steini Davidsson,Angelos Angelopoulos,Ron Alterovitz,Marcello DeLuca,Alexander Tropsha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SABLE是一个开源框架，利用自然语言编排（LLM）驱动化学结构优化，整合反应模板类物枚举、物化和ADMET预测、基于结构的亲和力打分以及贝叶斯优化，模拟设计-制造-测试-分析循环的分析与优先级划分阶段，提供可追溯的输出，并支持通过配置文件灵活替换工具模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 命中化合物到先导化合物的优化需要在新一轮设计中平衡效力、选择性、理化性质、药代动力学、安全性和合成可及性等多重竞争性约束，传统方法难以高效地在庞大的化学空间中迭代筛选，缺乏一个能够统一编排多种计算工具、并提供可追溯决策支持的自动化框架。

Method: SABLE采用LLM（大语言模型）作为自然语言编排核心，解释用户目标并路由任务；配备多个专用工具模块：反应模板类物枚举生成合成可行的类似物、物化与ADMET性质预测、基于结构的亲和力打分以及贝叶斯优化。整体架构模块化，通过配置文件即可替换工具和表征后端，无需修改操作逻辑。

Result: 在单目标和多目标优化研究中，SABLE仅评估枚举搜索空间的一个子集即可为用户定义的计算目标富集候选集，同时为每个数值输出提供来源追踪（provenance），验证了其作为决策支持框架的有效性与计算效率。

Conclusion: SABLE为早期药物发现中合成约束类似物的优先级排序提供了一个可扩展的决策支持框架，其模块化架构和自然语言驱动的编排模式使其具备良好的通用性、透明性和可维护性。

Abstract: Hit-to-lead optimization requires iterative design of hit analogs across competing potency, selectivity, physicochemical, pharmacokinetic, safety, and synthetic constraints. We present SABLE (Synthetically-accessible Agentic Bayesian Ligand Exploration), an open-source framework that employs natural-language orchestration to guide chemical structure optimization. SABLE uses an LLM to interpret user-defined goals and route tasks, while specialized tools perform reaction-templated analog enumeration, physicochemical and ADMET property prediction, structure-based affinity scoring, and Bayesian optimization. The resulting workflow is a computational twin of the analytical and prioritization stages of the design-make-test-analyze cycle, providing provenance of each numerical output. Across single, and multi-objective optimization studies, SABLE enriches candidate sets for user-defined computational objectives while evaluating only a subset of the enumerated search space. Its modular architecture allows tools and characterization backends to be replaced by editing a simple config file, without modifying operational logic. SABLE provides an extensible decision-support framework for prioritizing synthetically constrained analogs in early-stage drug discovery.

</details>


### [224] [From Prompting to Behavioral Alignment: Personalized LLM Judges for Recommendation Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11493)
*Alireza S. Ziabari,Kat Ellis,Colleen Chan,Ding Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现零样本LLM在离线推荐评估中存在"双向合理化"失效模式（对同一物品同一证据可同时论证正负用户参与结果），为此提出顺序行为对齐框架，结合微调与偏好优化（在配对的正确反事实理由上进行）。在真实首页交互日志上，该方法较零样本基线提升32.19%的Macro-F1，并达到生产级特征工程基线的水平，同时产出人类可解释的推理轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统离线推荐评估依赖复杂、人工维护且难以扩展的特征管道。虽然LLM从原始文本日志直接预测用户参与是一个有前景的替代方案，但研究发现其存在"双向合理化"的关键失效模式——零样本LLM在同一物品与相同证据下能同时令人信服地论证正负两种参与结果，说明开箱即用的LLM在用户参与预测上不可靠。

Method: 开发并应用顺序行为对齐框架：将微调（fine-tuning）与偏好优化（preference optimization）相结合，在配对的正确理由与反事实理由上进行训练，以对齐LLM的行为预测。

Result: 在真实世界首页交互日志上进行评估，对齐后的推理方法相比零样本基线在Macro-F1上获得32.19%的提升，并匹配了生产级特征工程基线的表现。

Conclusion: 行为对齐能够缓解LLM在离线推荐评估中的双向合理化问题，同时无需人工管道开销即可提供人类可解释的推理轨迹，验证了LLM作为替代传统特征工程评估方案的可行性。

Abstract: Traditional offline recommendation evaluation relies heavily on complex, manually maintained feature pipelines that are difficult to scale. While Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a promising alternative by predicting user engagement directly from raw text logs, empirical analysis in this study identifies a critical failure mode termed bidirectional rationalization. In a zero-shot setting, LLMs are found to convincingly argue for both positive and negative user engagement outcomes on the exact same item with identical evidence, highlighting the unreliability of off-the-shelf LLMs in predicting user engagement. To resolve this, we develop and apply a sequential behavioral alignment framework pairing fine-tuning with preference optimization over paired correct and counterfactual rationales. Evaluated on real-world homepage interaction logs, this aligned reasoning approach achieves a 32.19\% lift in Macro-F1 score over the zero-shot baseline and matches the production feature-engineered baseline. The results demonstrate that behavioral alignment mitigates bidirectional rationalization while delivering human-interpretable reasoning traces without manual pipeline overhead.

</details>


### [225] [Localizing Safety Alignment: MLP Layers and Mid-Network Blocks Encode Refusal Behavior in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11583)
*Mingyu Zong,Sampad Mohanty,Bhaskar Krishnamachari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过对齐模型到未对齐基础模型的权重移植实验，发现大语言模型的安全对齐（拒答行为）并非均匀分布在整个网络中，而是主要集中在MLP权重中，且具有中段网络（层8-11）的聚集特性；同时安全相关组件的组合存在非加性效应，即选择性块子集可优于完整MLP移植。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全对齐通常被视为整个网络的分布式属性，但实际中安全行为表现出脆弱性和易被绕过，这暗示拒答行为可能集中在较少的参数子集中。理解安全对齐在参数层面的具体定位，有助于解释其脆弱性并设计更有针对性的安全干预措施。

Method: 使用两个开放权重模型对（对齐模型与其匹配的未对齐基础模型），在四个安全基准上系统性地将对齐模型的权重移植到基础模型中，在不同粒度（注意力权重、MLP权重、连续层区域、MLP块）下进行比较实验，并通过贪心搜索策略分析拒答行为在MLP堆栈中的分布。

Result: 拒答行为的迁移主要由MLP权重主导：替换MLP参数比替换注意力参数能恢复至少2.7倍的恶意提示拒答效果。安全相关参数在MLP堆栈中呈现中段网络集中趋势（层8-11的块在全部六组模型-数据集贪心搜索中被首选）。安全组件组合是非加性的：六条贪心轨迹中五条显示添加更多对齐块反而会降低拒答性能，选择性块子集在恶意拒答、良性过度拒答或两者上均可优于完整MLP移植。转移到OR-Bench的贪心顺序随源基准不同而变化，存在基准依赖的精度-覆盖权衡。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型的安全对齐既是局部化的（集中于MLP权重及中段网络）又是交互敏感的（组件组合存在非加性效应），这些发现为理解对齐脆弱性提供了新视角，并为设计更有针对性的安全干预策略提供了可能路径。

Abstract: Safety alignment in large language models is often treated as a distributed property of the entire network, yet its practical brittleness suggests that refusal behavior may be concentrated in a smaller set of parameters. This work addresses where safety-aligned refusal is encoded by transplanting weights from aligned models into matched unaligned base models at multiple levels of granularity. Using two open-weight model pairs and four safety benchmarks, we conducted experiments to compare the effects of replacing attention weights, MLP weights, contiguous layer regions, and MLP blocks. Across both model families, refusal transfer is dominated by MLP weights: replacing MLP parameters recovers substantially more malicious-prompt refusal than replacing attention parameters, with gains of at least 2.7 times more across benchmarks. Within the MLP stack, refusal-relevant parameters exhibit a consistent mid-network concentration, as the block spanning layers 8-11 is selected first in all six greedy searches over model-dataset pairs. The results also show that the composition of safety-relevant components is non-additive: in five of six greedy trajectories, adding more aligned blocks can reduce refusal performance, and selective block subsets can outperform full MLP transplantation on malicious refusal, benign over-refusal, or both. Finally, greedy orders transferred to OR-Bench vary with the source benchmark used to derive them, indicating a benchmark-dependent precision-coverage trade-off. These results suggest that safety alignment in current LLMs is both localized and interaction-sensitive, offering insight into alignment brittleness and potential avenues for targeted safety interventions.

</details>


### [226] [EnterpriseRAG: Benchmarking LLM Instruction Adherence and Robustness under Non-Ideal Enterprise Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11584)
*Huiqi Miao,Xinbao Sun,Bo Wang,Fanyu Meng,Lijun Mei,Na Wu,Di Jin,Chao Deng,Junlan Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EnterpriseRAG基准，包含983个经专家验证的跨六个领域样本，系统模拟检索噪声、知识缺口和事实冲突三种生产环境故障模式。对13个SOTA LLM的评估揭示了指令遵循崩塌现象——高单约束满足率掩盖低整体合规率。研究发现知识缺口和事实冲突场景下存在深层障碍，即使采用推理增强推理也无法解决，表明生产级RAG需要显式上下文感知协议和校准判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业级RAG部署面临关键可靠性缺口：LLM能满足80%的单个约束，但仅26.8%的响应同时满足所有要求，存在57个百分点的编排缺口。现有基准假设干净的检索环境和简单查询，无法捕捉生产条件下噪声文档与多维约束并存的实际场景。

Method: 构建EnterpriseRAG基准，包含983个专家验证样本，覆盖六个领域，系统模拟三类现有工作缺失的故障模式：检索噪声、知识缺口和事实冲突，并与复杂指令相结合。对13个SOTA LLM进行系统评估。

Result: 揭示严重的指令遵循崩塌现象：高逐约束满足率掩盖低整体合规率。关键发现表明知识缺口和事实冲突场景下存在深层障碍，即使采用推理增强推断也无法克服，显示生产级RAG需要显式上下文感知协议和校准判断。基准和评估框架将在发表后发布。

Conclusion: EnterpriseRAG为衡量和缩小生产级RAG系统的可靠性缺口提供了可复现的基础，能直接为企业级RAG部署决策提供信息支撑。

Abstract: Enterprise RAG deployments face a critical reliability gap: while LLMs satisfy 80% of individual constraints, only 26.8% of responses meet all requirements simultaneously, revealing a 57-point orchestration gap. Existing benchmarks assume clean retrieval with simple queries, failing to capture production conditions where noisy documents and multi-dimensional constraints coexist. We introduce EnterpriseRAG, a benchmark of 983 expert-validated samples across six domains that systematically simulates three failure modes absent from prior work: retrieval noise, knowledge gaps, and factual conflicts, coupled with complex instructions. Evaluation of 13 state-of-the-art LLMs reveals a severe instruction adherence collapse, where high per-constraint satisfaction masks low holistic compliance. Critical findings expose deep barriers under knowledge gaps and factual conflicts, even with reasoning-enhanced inference, indicating production RAG requires explicit context-aware protocols and calibrated judgment. EnterpriseRAG provides a reproducible foundation for measuring and closing these gaps, directly informing deployment decisions for enterprise-scale RAG systems. We will release the benchmark and evaluation framework upon publication.

</details>


### [227] [CoAdapt-GUI: Joint Workflow Context and Policy Adaptation for Unseen GUI Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11588)
*Linqiang Guo,Li Gu,Zihuan Jiang,Zhixiang Chi,Siobhan Reid,Ziqiang Wang,Yuanhao Yu,Wei Liu,Yang Wang,Tse-Hsun,Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CoAdapt-GUI，一个面向移动GUI智能体的测试时自适应（TTA）框架，在无目标应用演示且交互预算有限的条件下，通过联合自适应工作流上下文与策略，使智能体在未见过的应用上实现稳健泛化，在AndroidWorld两个基准上分别达到45.0%和52.9%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动GUI智能体在部署到训练时未见的应用时表现脆弱。现有方法依赖源域演示或大量目标数据，难以在有限交互预算且无目标演示的条件下实现有效泛化，需要一种能够从智能体自身rollout中学习的自适应机制。

Method: CoAdapt-GUI采用测试时自适应的双通道框架：1) 工作流上下文通道——从智能体自身目标应用rollout中提取可迁移的程序性知识（流程、失败模式、验证规则），剔除应用绑定的源端界面状态；2) 策略通道——采用任务上下文匹配的组相对优化（Group-relative optimization）更新冻结的视觉语言模型上的LoRA适配器，实现策略的联合自适应。

Result: 在两个未见应用评估中，CoAdapt-GUI在AndroidWorld-Generalization上达到45.0%（对比Policy-Only TTA基线的37.5%），并将AndroidWorld Plus从38.6%提升至52.9%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，受迁移约束的工作流上下文提供了显著的性能增益，且联合策略自适应进一步提升了零样本（无目标演示）场景下的泛化性能，验证了分离可复用工作流知识与源端界面状态的有效性。

Abstract: Mobile GUI agents remain brittle when deployed to applications absent from source training. We study novel-app generalization under a limited target interaction budget and without target demonstrations. We introduce CoAdapt-GUI, a test-time adaptation (TTA) framework that jointly adapts structured workflow context and policy from the agent's own target-app rollouts and rewards. The workflow context retains transferable procedures, failure modes, and verification rules while excluding app-bound source details. This separation allows reusable workflow knowledge to guide adaptation without transferring source-interface state. For policy adaptation, task-context-matched group-relative optimization updates a LoRA adapter on a frozen vision-language model. Across two unseen-app evaluations, CoAdapt-GUI reaches 45.0% on AndroidWorld-Generalization, compared with 37.5% for the reported Policy-Only TTA baseline, and raises AndroidWorld Plus performance from 38.6% to 52.9%. These results show that transfer-constrained workflow context provides substantial gains and that joint policy adaptation further improves held-out performance.

</details>


### [228] [Learning from Online User Feedback for Shopping Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11604)
*Haobo Zhang,Kelong Mao,Sulong Xu,Simiu Gu,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LOFA框架，使购物智能体无需人工标注即可直接从真实在线交互日志中学习，通过结合基于可验证购买结果的强化学习与感知反馈的在线策略蒸馏，将用户对话中的指令转化为密集的token级监督信号，在真实电商日志上显著提升推荐质量、回复有用性和用户满意度对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有购物智能体的训练主要依赖离线信号（如用户-物品交互或合成偏好数据），忽略了用户自然对话反馈中蕴含的丰富监督信息；同时在线反馈存在异构、稀疏和噪声大的问题，难以自动转化为可靠的学习信号。

Method: LOFA框架包含两个互补的训练目标：1) 基于可验证购买结果的强化学习，捕捉协作性的行为模式；2) 感知反馈的在线策略蒸馏，识别用户对话中的指令并将其转化为密集的token级监督信号，捕捉用户特定偏好。

Result: 在真实电商日志上的大量实验表明，LOFA在推荐质量、回复有用性和用户满意度对齐方面持续优于强基线方法，验证了从真实在线用户反馈中学习购物智能体的有效性。

Conclusion: 实验证明直接从真实在线交互日志中学习（无需人工标注）是可行且有效的，LOFA通过融合协作行为模式与用户个性化偏好，提供了一种不需依赖离线或合成信号的全新训练范式。

Abstract: Large language model-based shopping agents are increasingly deployed in real-world e-commerce platforms, generating massive amounts of user interaction logs that provide valuable supervision for improving these agents. However, existing approaches primarily rely on offline training signals, such as user-item interactions or synthetic preference data, while largely overlooking the rich supervision contained in users' natural conversational feedback. Moreover, the available online feedback is heterogeneous, sparse, and noisy, making it difficult to transform into reliable learning signals automatically. To address these challenges, we propose LOFA, a framework that enables shopping agents to learn directly from real online interaction logs without human annotation. LOFA combines reinforcement learning over verifiable purchase outcomes with feedback-aware on-policy distillation, which identifies users'in-dialogue directives and converts them into dense token-level supervision. These complementary objectives capture both collaborative behavioral patterns and user-specific preferences. Extensive experiments on real-world e-commerce logs demonstrate that LOFA consistently improves recommendation quality, response helpfulness, and user-satisfaction alignment over strong baselines, highlighting the effectiveness of learning shopping agents from real online user feedback.

</details>


### [229] [Foresight Without Seeing: Latent Futures for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11605)
*Jiakai Huang,Zhongbo Wu,Zheng Zhang,Zihan Wang,Shan You,Tao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ForeWAM，一种动力学条件化的直接策略世界动作模型（WAM）。它通过Future-KV机制在单个Video DiT预填充中生成未来隐状态，并复用各层键值状态用于动作去噪，无需显式解码未来视频即可为动作生成提供预测上下文。配合动力学寄存器（dynamics registers）和冻结的潜在动作教师模型进行监督，在不进行机器人数据预训练的条件下，分别在LIBERO和LIBERO-Plus上达到96.7%和61.6%的平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAM）在如何将预测动力学暴露给动作通路方面存在两难：显式未来WAM能直接访问预测的场景演化，但迭代视频去噪带来高昂推理成本；直接策略WAM虽能高效地从当前观测预测动作，却缺乏显式推理时接口来将预测动力学暴露给Action DiT。作者旨在弥合这一差距，在保持直接策略高效性的同时提供预测上下文。

Method: 提出ForeWAM，核心组件包括：（1）Future-KV机制：对当前视觉潜在表示和随机未来槽位执行单次Video DiT预填充，并在动作去噪过程中复用生成的逐层键值（KV）状态，从而避免未来视频解码；（2）动力学寄存器（dynamics registers）：通过冻结的潜在动作教师模型进行监督，促使隐式未来状态捕获交互诱导的转变，如物体运动、接触变化和任务进度。训练阶段使用真实未来观测和教师模型，部署时两者均不需要，也不生成未来视频。

Result: 在无机器人数据预训练条件下，ForeWAM的标准变体和加速变体在LIBERO上分别达到96.7%和96.9%的平均成功率；标准变体在LIBERO-Plus上达到61.6%的成功率。这些结果证明直接策略WAM能够在保持高效动作预测的同时，将预测动力学暴露给动作通路，而无需显式生成未来观测。

Conclusion: 直接策略世界动作模型可以通过动力学条件化的方式（如ForeWAM的Future-KV和动力学寄存器设计）在推理时高效地暴露预测动力学信息给动作生成通路，消除显式未来视频生成的计算开销，同时保持甚至提升任务成功率。该工作为WAM设计提供了一种兼顾效率与预测能力的新范式。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) couple future visual prediction with robot action generation, enabling policies to model how the physical world evolves during interaction. Existing WAMs differ in how predictive dynamics are exposed to the action pathway. Explicit-future WAMs provide direct access to predicted scene evolution, but incur substantial inference costs from iterative video denoising. In contrast, direct-policy WAMs efficiently predict actions from the current observation but lack an explicit inference-time interface for exposing predictive dynamics to the Action DiT. To bridge this gap, we propose ForeWAM, a dynamics-conditioned direct-policy WAM that provides predictive context for action generation without decoding future videos. At its core, Future-KV performs a single Video DiT prefill over the current visual latent and stochastic future slots, and reuses the resulting layer-wise key-value states throughout action denoising. We further introduce dynamics registers supervised by a frozen latent action teacher, encouraging the implicit future states to capture interaction-induced transitions such as object motion, contact changes, and task progress. Ground-truth future observations and the teacher are used only during training; deployment requires neither and performs no future video generation. Without embodied robot data pretraining, the standard and accelerated variants of ForeWAM achieve average success rates of 96.7% and 96.9% on LIBERO, respectively. The standard variant further achieves 61.6% success on LIBERO-Plus. These results demonstrate that direct-policy WAMs can retain efficient action prediction while exposing predictive dynamics to the action pathway without explicitly generating future observations.

</details>


### [230] [MBA: Multimodal Benchmark and Agents for Real-World Business Ideation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11616)
*Hojun Choi,Jaeyo Shin,Suin Lee,Hyunjung Shim*

Main category: cs.AI
