TL;DR: 本文提出一个操作化的森林地上生物量（AGB）遥感估算框架，核心是单个全局训练的卷积神经网络（CNN），结合GEDI Level-4A生物量参考数据和多源遥感数据（Sentinel-1/2、ALOS-2 PALSAR-2、DEM）进行训练，再通过轻量级野外样地标定（尺度-偏差校正和随机森林微调）实现区域自适应，无需针对每个景观重新训练。全局模型R²约0.78、RMSE约22 Mg/ha；经过野外标定后，局部验证R²提升至约0.82、RMSE降至约15 Mg/ha，优于未标定全局模型和ESA CCI Biomass产品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 森林AGB的空间连续量化对碳核算和减缓策略至关重要，但野外调查精度高而空间稀疏，GEDI星载LiDAR提供广覆盖样本却缺乏空间连续性，且对高生物量森林存在系统性低估。作者旨在弥合这两个数据源之间的差距，构建一个既能在全球范围泛化又能适应局部景观的操作化框架。

Method: 方法包括：1) 单个全局训练的CNN，融合Sentinel-2光学、Sentinel-1 C波段SAR、ALOS-2 PALSAR-2 L波段SAR和DEM地形数据；2) 以GEDI Level-4A生物量数据为训练参考，覆盖多区域、湿季和干季，使模型学习持久木质结构特征而非单一日期表现；3) 统一10m网格的数据预处理流程，包含植被指数和极化比计算、逐波段归一化统计；4) 使用混合log域SmoothL1与RMSE损失函数处理偏斜的生物量分布；5) 局部野外标定：少量样地拟合尺度-偏差校正，配合随机森林微调与10折交叉验证。

Result: 全局GEDI模型在留出验证集上R²约0.78、RMSE约22 Mg/ha。结合10折交叉验证的野外标定（随机森林微调）后，局部验证性能提升至R²约0.82、RMSE约15 Mg/ha，在野外样地上同时优于未标定的全局模型和ESA CCI Biomass产品。

Conclusion: 该框架验证了"全局预训练+局部轻量标定"范式的可行性：一个全局训练的CNN即可作为通用生物量估算基座，通过少量野外样地的低成本标定即可消除区域偏差，在保持操作效率的同时获得优于现有产品的精度，为大规模碳核算提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Spatially continuous quantification of forest above-ground biomass (AGB) is what makes carbon accounting credible and mitigation strategies actionable. While field inventories provide high localized accuracy, they are spatially sparse; conversely, spaceborne LiDAR from the Global Ecosystem Dynamics Investigation (GEDI) offers broad biomass samples but lacks spatial continuity and systematic underestimation of high-biomass forests. This paper presents an operational framework centered on a single globally trained convolutional neural network (CNN) that is seamlessly adapted to each new landscape through a lightweight empirical field-calibration workflow. The global model combines optical (Sentinel-2), C-band SAR (Sentinel-1), L-band SAR (ALOS-2 PALSAR-2), and terrain (DEM) data. It is trained once against GEDI Level-4A biomass reference data spanning multiple regions and both wet and dry seasons so that it learns the persistent woody-structure rather than a single-date appearance. To avoid retraining for every landscape, the framework applies a small number of local field plots to fit a scale-and-bias correction that aligns the global prediction with ground truth in each region. The pipeline harmonizes sensor data onto a shared 10 m grid, derives vegetation indices and polarimetric ratios, computes per-band normalization stats, and trains the CNN with a hybrid log-domain SmoothL1 with RMSE loss for skewed biomass distribution. On held-out validation the global GEDI-based model achieved R^2 approximately 0.78 and RMSE approximately 22 Mg/ha. A subsequent field calibration combining Random Forest fine-tuning under a 10-fold cross-validation eliminates localized regional biases. This improves local validation performance to R^2 approximately 0.82 and reduces RMSE to approximately 15 Mg/ha, outperforming both the uncalibrated global model and the ESA CCI Biomass product against field plots.

</details>


### [299] [Towards a Formal Definition of Agent Memory: Basis, Span, Optimality, and the Sequential Memory Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11654)
*Hongyao Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为大型模型代理中的"记忆"提供了统一的形式化框架：将记忆定义为基（basis），知识定义为其张成空间（span），可回答性定义为覆盖问题（coverage problem）。最优记忆是容量约束下期望覆盖的最大化，其价值沿效用-容量前沿（utility-capacity frontier）刻画，为不同记忆系统提供了可量化的比较基准。论文进一步引入噪声下的覆盖-精度权衡、以及将持续记忆形式化为顺序MDP，最后在《奥德赛》上实例化并在现有系统中定位该框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型模型代理已广泛部署记忆系统，但缺乏统一的形式化描述来回答"什么是记忆"以及"何时记忆是最优的"这两个根本问题，阻碍了记忆系统的理论分析和公平比较。

Method: 核心方法：将记忆定义为基（basis），知识定义为其张成空间（span），通过生成算子将事件集合转化为蕴含知识；可回答性被建模为覆盖问题（single item in span covers the query）。在此基础上定义容量约束下的期望覆盖最大化问题，并引入效用-容量前沿作为统一比较标准。处理噪声时讨论覆盖与精度的权衡，将持续记忆问题形式化为覆盖多层级的顺序MDP（记忆为状态、写为动作、查询时的效用为延迟奖励）。最后在《奥德赛》文本上实例化计算前沿、压缩区和覆盖-精度偏差。

Result: 论文在《奥德赛》上给出了效用-容量前沿、压缩区和覆盖-精度发散的具体数值结果，证明该框架可产生可量化的记忆比较基准；同时将现有记忆系统定位到框架内，使"记忆有多好"成为可测量的问题。

Conclusion: 该框架为代理记忆提供了统一形式化基础，使记忆的构造与学习开放问题被重铸为具体的研究问题；通过类比生物记忆，为持续学习的代理记忆提供了MDP形式化路径，但框架的实用性和在不同领域（非叙事文本）上的泛化仍有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Despite the wide deployment of memory in large-model agents, there is no unified formal account of what a memory is or when it is optimal. This paper takes a first step toward this account. The central idea is that memory is a basis, knowledge is its span, and answerability is a coverage problem: an agent stores events extracted from a material; a generation operator turns any event set into the knowledge it entails; and a query is answerable exactly when some single item in the span covers it. The optimal memory is then the capacity-constrained maximizer of expected coverage, and its value traces a utility--capacity frontier, the common yardstick on which memory systems can be compared. Next, we consider noise in the memory and discuss coverage versus precision under it: a memory may store false claims, so the write policy must infer the truth of what it stores. Drawing an analogy with biological memory, which is formed continuously through ongoing experience, we formalize the continual agent-memory problem in a sequential MDP that covers multiple levels, where memory is the state, writing is the action, and the utility settled at query time is the delayed reward that drives learning. To make the framework concrete, we instantiate it on Homer's \emph{Odyssey}, turning the frontier, the compression zone, and the divergence of coverage from precision into concrete numbers. Finally, we position existing systems within the framework, making ``how good is a memory'' measurable and recasting the open problems of constructing and learning agent memory as concrete research questions.

</details>


### [300] [Continuous-Latent Predictive Modeling with Semantic Alignment for EEG-Language Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11656)
*Myeong-Ju Cho,Hye-Bin Shin,Seo-Hyun Lee,Seong-Whan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出脑潜在预测模型（BLPM），这是首个将连续EEG表征与自然语言语义对齐的EEG-语言基础模型。通过引入连续EEG潜在预测（CELP）编码器和多查询语义分解（MQSD）模块，BLPM将异构EEG解码任务统一为连续语义嵌入预测问题，在多个基准上展现了跨任务的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG基础模型的预训练范式存在关键局限：掩码自编码倾向于优先重建低层信号而非任务相关语义，自回归建模则在连续神经动态与离散词元空间之间存在失配。因此需要新的策略来有效对齐连续EEG表征与自然语言语义，并支持与大语言模型的集成。

Method: BLPM包含两个核心组件：1）连续EEG潜在预测（CELP）编码器，通过潜在目标预测学习可迁移的表征；2）多查询语义分解（MQSD）模块，提取任务相关信息并在共享潜在空间中依据语义关系将连续EEG表征与文本语义对齐。整个框架将异构的EEG解码任务重新构造成连续的语义嵌入预测问题。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，BLPM在不同任务上展现了一致的泛化性能，证明了连续潜在语义预测作为EEG-语言基础模型的有效范式。

Conclusion: 连续潜在语义预测被确立为一种有效的EEG-语言基础模型范式，BLPM通过弥合连续EEG信号与离散语言语义之间的鸿沟，为可泛化的神经解码提供了新路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in EEG foundation models have demonstrated the potential of large-scale pretraining to enable generalizable neural decoding across subjects, recording environments, and datasets. However, dominant pretraining paradigms face key challenges: masked autoencoding tends to prioritize low-level signal reconstruction over task-relevant semantics, while autoregressive modeling creates a mismatch between continuous neural dynamics and discrete token spaces. To address these challenges, new strategies are needed to effectively align continuous EEG representations with natural-language semantics and enable their integration with large language models. Accordingly, we propose Brain Latent Predictive Model (BLPM), an EEG-language foundation model that reformulates heterogeneous EEG decoding tasks as a continuous semantic embedding prediction problem. BLPM introduces a Continuous EEG Latent Predictive (CELP) encoder that learns transferable representations through latent target prediction. Building on these representations, a Multi-Query Semantic Decomposition (MQSD) module extracts task-relevant information and aligns continuous EEG representations with textual semantics within a shared latent space according to their semantic relationships. Experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate consistent generalization performance across diverse tasks, establishing continuous latent semantic prediction as an effective paradigm for EEG-language foundation models.

</details>


### [301] [Is Per-Agent Policy Composition Safe? Rethinking Successor-Feature Transfer in Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11658)
*Zijian Zhao,Sen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多智能体强化学习中的目标动态变化问题。作者证明，在多智能体场景下，各智能体独立重组合自的策略库（独立组合）可能产生比库中所有策略都更差的联合行为，因为队友策略改变会改变每个智能体面临的环境，导致其价值估计失效。唯一无条件安全的固定规则是同步组合（整个团队切换到同一联合训练策略），但这无法服务对不同智能体分配不同目标的需求。为此提出MA-USFA方法：下层用通用后继特征逼近器在队友目标条件下预测每个智能体的后继特征，上层合成器跨智能体选择库条目并提供跨智能体修正。该方法在目标分布上训练一次，部署时无需逐任务适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多强化学习系统（从车队管理到交通信号控制）在部署后需要服务动态变化的目标，为每个新目标重新训练策略成本过高。单智能体场景下，后继特征与广义策略提升（及其通用扩展）已被很好地解决，但多智能体迁移受到的关注很少。常见的做法是让每个智能体独立重组合自的策略库，这种方式继承了单智能体的"配方"却缺乏其保证。作者旨在揭示这一缺口并提供解决方案。

Method: 作者首先从理论上证明独立组合可能产生比库中所有策略都差的联合行为（由于队友策略重组合改变每个智能体面对的环境，失效其依赖的价值函数），并证明唯一无条件安全的固定规则是同步组合。为兼顾安全性与灵活性，提出MA-USFA（多智能体通用后继特征近似器）：采用两层层级结构，下层为通用后继特征逼近器，在队友目标条件下预测各智能体的后继特征；上层为合成器，跨智能体选择每个智能体应遵循的库条目，并提供单智能体价值无法表示的跨智能体修正项。整个系统在目标分布上训练一次，部署时无需逐任务适配。

Result: 理论层面：证明了多智能体独立组合策略库会产生严格的性能退化（比库中所有策略都差），这是单智能体场景不存在的失效模式；证明了唯一无条件安全的固定组合规则是同步组合，但其代价是无法服务异构目标。方法层面：提出了MA-USFA层级框架，在保持安全性的同时支持不同智能体拥有不同目标的灵活场景。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统性地分析了多智能体目标迁移的失效机理，填补了该领域的理论空白。所提出的MA-USFA兼顾了同步组合的安全性（通过训练期目标分布覆盖和跨智能体修正）与独立组合的灵活性（支持异构目标），为多智能体强化学习在动态目标场景下的实际部署提供了理论与方法基础。

Abstract: Many reinforcement learning systems, from fleet management to traffic signal control, must serve an objective that changes dynamically after deployment, and retraining a policy for each new objective is prohibitively expensive. For a single agent, this problem is well understood: successor features with generalized policy improvement, together with their universal extension, recombine a library of learned policies into a policy for any new objective, with a guarantee that the result is never worse than any policy in the library. However, multi-agent transfer has received far less attention, and the common practice of letting each agent recombine its own library independently inherits the recipe but not the guarantee. We prove that this independent composition can produce joint behavior strictly worse than every policy in the library, because recombining teammates changes the environment each agent faces and invalidates the values it relies on, a failure with no single-agent counterpart. We further show that the only unconditionally safe fixed rule is synchronized composition, which moves the whole team to one jointly trained policy but cannot serve objectives that assign different goals to different agents. To attain safety and flexibility at once, we propose MA-USFA, a hierarchical method with two layers: a lower layer of universal successor feature approximators that predicts each agent's successor features while conditioned on its teammates' objectives, and an upper composer that selects, across agents, which library entry each agent should follow and supplies the cross-agent correction a per-agent value cannot represent. Trained once over the distribution of objectives, it is applied at deployment with no per-task adaptation.

</details>


### [302] [Low-Interaction-Rank Learning: Unifying Multiplicative Dual-Encoder Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11661)
*Zijian Zhao,Sen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为乘法双编码器网络（输出为两个输入各自编码的内积）这一被多个领域独立开发但缺乏统一理论的架构提供了数学基础。作者引入"低交互秩函数"类及交互谱作为内在复杂度度量，证明近似误差可分解为谱截断项与编码器实现项，样本复杂度由两项编码器复杂度之和而非乘积决定，并基于谱衰减提出可用性准则。论文还揭示了编码器仅定义到线性规范对称性这一可识别性问题，证明归一化即规范固定、白化可将交互模式固定到置换和符号，从而解释对比学习维度不可解释性的根源并提供构造性解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乘法双编码器网络（计算两输入的编码内积）已在算子学习、二分匹配、对比视觉-语言模型、检索等多个领域被独立开发，但缺乏统一理论来指导基本设计决策：应表示多少交互模式、如何归一化编码器、何时应避免该架构。本文旨在为这些问题提供理论基础。

Method: 引入低交互秩函数类，以交互谱度量内在复杂度；将近似误差分解为谱截断项和编码器实现项；推导由编码器复杂度之和（而非乘积）控制的样本复杂度；提出基于谱衰减的可用性准则。针对编码器仅定义到线性规范对称性的可识别性问题，证明归一化是规范固定，白化可将交互模式固定到置换和符号。

Result: 在合成核、算子学习和CLIP模型上验证了理论预测：谱衰减速率与预测标度一致；白化恢复真实模式；独立训练的CLIP模型之间仅相差一个旋转，经白化去除后暴露了可解释的概念轴。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了乘法双编码器网络的设计原则，提供了关于交互模式数量选择、归一化策略的理论指导，并通过规范对称性的洞察揭示了对比学习维度不可解释性的根源，提出了白化这一构造性修复方法。

Abstract: A multiplicative dual-encoder network computes a real-valued output for a pair of inputs as the inner product of their separate encodings. This architecture has been developed independently in operator learning, bipartite matching, contrastive vision-language models, retrieval, and other areas, yet no unified theory guides the basic design decisions: how many interaction modes to represent, how to normalize the encoders, and when the architecture should be avoided. We provide such a foundation by introducing the class of functions of low interaction rank, a class whose intrinsic complexity is measured by its interaction spectrum. Within this framework, approximation error decomposes into a spectral truncation term and an encoder-realization term; sample complexity is governed by the sum of the two encoder complexities rather than their product; and a usability criterion based on spectral decay determines when the architecture can succeed. The same framework exposes a central identifiability problem: the encoders are defined only up to a linear gauge symmetry that leaves the learned coordinates arbitrary. We show that normalization is gauge fixing and that whitening pins the interaction modes up to permutation and sign, thereby explaining the uninterpretability of contrastive dimensions and providing a constructive remedy. Experiments on synthetic kernels, operator learning, and CLIP models validate the theoretical predictions: spectral decay rates match the predicted scaling, whitening recovers the true modes, and independently trained CLIP models are related by a single rotation which, after removal by whitening, exposes interpretable concept axes. The code of this paper is provided at https://github.com/RS2002/Mul-Net .

</details>


### [303] [Rubric Dropout: A Simple Way to Mitigate Reward Hacking in Rubric-as-Reward RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11669)
*Minglai Yang,Xinyu Guo,Utkarsh Tyagi,Mian Zhang,Razvan Dumitru,Sunjie Hou,Yunzhong He,Daniel Yue Zhang,Ying Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM通过rubric（评分标准列表）进行强化学习后训练时产生的奖励欺骗（reward hacking）问题。作者训练Qwen3-8B使用GRPO在医疗和科学rubrics上，发现训练评委分数持续上升但更强的黄金评委分数先升后降，证明这是奖励欺骗而非评委噪声。作者提出Rubric Dropout——一种类似神经元dropout的简单方法，每步随机丢弃部分rubric标准后再计算奖励，防止策略重复优化同一rubric，在OOD基准上提升了黄金分数且不影响领域内性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM评委的rubric评分是缺乏确定性答案的任务进行后训练的标准方法，但rubric只是质量的固定代理，并不完整描述质量。策略经过长时间训练后会学习利用rubric与真实质量之间的差异（即奖励欺骗）。作者希望直接测量这种现象并提出缓解方案。

Method: 作者使用Qwen3-8B配合GRPO在医疗和科学rubric上训练，用训练评委和更强黄金评委分别评估OOD基准，测量两分数分歧。提出Rubric Dropout方法：每步随机丢弃部分rubric标准后再计算奖励，丢弃子集在每轮rollout组内共享以保证GRPO组间相对优势可比，评估时始终使用完整rubric。比较无dropout和30%/50% dropout，并扫描dropout比例，同时对比基于标准有用性重加权的方法。

Result: 训练过程中训练评委分数持续上升但黄金评委分数先升后降（HealthBench-Hard下降3分，ResearchQA下降22分），证明是奖励欺骗。Rubric Dropout在30%和50%配置下在所有匹配检查点提升OOD黄金分数（HealthBench-Hard +1到+2分，ResearchQA +6到+7分），降低两种欺骗测量指标，领域内无损。30-50% dropout是广泛的最优区间，而基于有用性重加权标准的方法比无干预更差。

Conclusion: Rubric Dropout是一种简单有效、几乎零成本（代码一行的修改）的缓解LLM强化学习中rubric奖励欺骗的方法，优于更复杂的重加权方案。该发现表明随机性可以打破策略对固定评分代理的过度优化，为安全对齐LLM提供了实用的正则化手段。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning against rubrics, lists of criteria graded by an LLM judge, has become a standard way to post-train language models on tasks with no deterministic answer. The rubric, however, is a fixed proxy for quality, never a complete description of it, and a policy trained against it long enough will learn to exploit the difference. We measure this directly. Training Qwen3-8B with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) on medical and science rubrics and grading out-of-distribution (OOD) benchmarks with both the training judge and a stronger gold judge, we find that the two scores diverge during training. The training judge's score keeps climbing while the gold judge's score peaks and then falls, by 3 points on HealthBench-Hard and by 22 points on ResearchQA. A judge with a fixed bias would shift the gold curve by a constant, not send it down while the training score rises, so the divergence is reward hacking, not judge noise. We propose Rubric Dropout, a one-line fix borrowed from neuron dropout. At every step, we randomly drop a subset of the rubric's criteria before computing the reward, so the policy never optimizes the same rubric twice. The dropped subset is shared across each rollout group, so GRPO's group-relative advantages stay comparable, and evaluation always uses the full rubric. Comparing no dropout against dropout at 30% and 50% on both benchmark pairs, dropout raises the OOD gold score at every matched checkpoint (+1 to +2 points on HealthBench-Hard, +6 to +7 points on ResearchQA), lowers the two hacking measures we track, and costs nothing in domain. Sweeping the dropout fraction shows a broad 30-50% sweet spot, while the natural alternative, reweighting criteria by how useful they are to training, performs worse than no intervention at all in our setting.

</details>


### [304] [GCPO: Diagnosing and Constraining Subspace Geometry in Rollout RL for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11674)
*Kai Yang,Jingwei Xu,Wanyu Wang,Kai-Yuan Guo,Zhenbo Yu,Yi Wang,Yu Qiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出主子空间重叠度（PSO）作为诊断指标，用于测量策略梯度更新与预训练权重主奇异子空间的重叠程度，发现瞬时峰值前兆可预测性能退化；并据此提出GCPO方法，通过硬双边正交投影将更新约束到互补子空间，在数学推理、代码生成和工具使用任务上显著优于GRPO及DAPO、GSPO等变体，同时消除响应长度膨胀并稳定策略熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO等on-policy rollout方法在大语言模型后训练中广泛使用，但存在训练不稳定、跨任务能力退化及响应长度膨胀等问题。已有工作虽然刻画了聚合更新的子空间几何，但单步更新的几何变化及其与模型性能的关系尚不清晰，缺乏有效的诊断工具和预防性设计原则。

Method: 提出主-子空间重叠度（PSO），一个维度校正的度量，用于衡量单次rollout更新相对于预训练权重主导奇异子空间的投影程度。在诊断基础上，提出GCPO（几何约束策略优化），通过硬双边正交投影将策略更新强制约束到互补子空间，从结构上防止更新偏离安全区域。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B和GLM4-9B上，涉及数学推理、代码生成和工具使用任务，GCPO一致优于GRPO及DAPO、GSPO等近期变体：相对基础模型最高提升27.69分，相对最强基线最高提升2.37分。此外，GCPO保持了通用能力，消除了响应长度膨胀，并稳定了策略熵。

Conclusion: PSO为诊断RLHF后训练不稳定性提供了新视角（瞬时重叠尖峰可预警性能退化），GCPO则为稳定强化学习后训练提供了基于几何原理的设计范式，证明了将更新约束在预训练权重互补子空间中是一种有效且稳健的优化策略。

Abstract: On-policy rollout methods such as GRPO are central to post-training of large language models, yet they frequently suffer from training instabilities, cross-task capability degradation, and response-length inflation. Although prior work has characterized the subspace geometry of aggregate updates, the stepwise variation of this geometry and its relationship to model performance remain unclear. We introduce Principal-Subspace Overlap, a dimension-corrected measure of individual rollout updates relative to the dominant singular subspaces of pretrained weights. Despite low average overlap, transient spikes often precede performance degradation. To address this, we propose GCPO (Geometrically Constrained Policy Optimization), which applies hard bilateral orthogonal projections to constrain updates to the complementary subspaces, preventing such excursions by construction. Across mathematical reasoning, code generation, and tool-use tasks on Qwen3-8B and GLM4-9B, GCPO consistently outperforms GRPO and recent variants, including DAPO and GSPO, improving over the base models and the strongest baseline by up to 27.69 and 2.37 points, respectively. Furthermore, GCPO preserves general capabilities, eliminates response-length inflation, and stabilizes policy entropy. Our findings provide a new diagnostic lens and a principled design perspective for stable reinforcement learning post-training.

</details>


### [305] [FunnelCausalNet: Funnel-aware Joint Conversion-Revenue Uplift for Multi-tier Coupon Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11675)
*Yu Zhang,Zhihan Wang,Guanlin Chen,Min Jiang,Shuai Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FunnelCausalNet，一种将转化二分类头与条件价值回归头耦合的增量（uplift）估计器，通过GMV漏斗分解（转化率×条件订单价值）来建模零膨胀、重尾的GMV分布，并配套split-conformal CATE审计带与拉格朗日预算分配器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优惠券营销的目标同时提升转化率和收入，但GMV遵循从转化到条件订单价值的确定性漏斗，且呈现零膨胀和重尾分布，直接建模GMV难以兼顾这两种统计特性，需要专门的uplift估计框架。

Method: FunnelCausalNet将估计器分解为二元转化头μ_conv与非负条件价值头μ_val，通过μ_gmv=μ_conv×μ_val耦合。在显式RCT、支撑集、率差与跨头协方差控制等假设下，通过leading-order MSE比较导出漏斗组合可降低点方差的机制（作为启发式）。配套边际split-conformal CATE汇总，经Bonferroni并集组合为审计带，并使用拉格朗日预算分配器进行补贴感知的ROI核算。

Result: 在Criteo-MT7半合成数据上，FunnelCausalNet的均值AUUC_GMV在11个基线中位于领先特征交互基线的一个种子标准差以内；消融实验相对直接GMV回归将GMV效应误差降低18-48%。在约490万保留曝光记录的工业酒店优惠券RCT日志上，在7个10%-60%相关锚点处取得最优种子平均DeltaROI。在稀疏二值支出公共基准上，收入导向排序器可能超过uplift曲线代理指标。

Conclusion: 漏斗分解式uplift建模在零膨胀重尾GMV场景下具有方差降低与实际ROI优势，但结果需以描述性一致性的视野看待（非独立显著性），且存在明确的适用边界——当支出数据稀疏时，收入导向排序器可能更优。

Abstract: Coupon campaigns seek to lift both conversion and revenue, but gross merchandise value (GMV) follows a deterministic funnel from conversion to conditional order value and is zero-inflated and heavy-tailed. We propose FunnelCausalNet, an uplift estimator coupling a binary conversion head with a nonnegative conditional-value head through $μ_{\mathrm{gmv}}=μ_{\mathrm{conv}}μ_{\mathrm{val}}$. Under explicit RCT, support, rate-gap, and cross-head covariance-control assumptions, an idealized leading-order MSE comparison identifies a regime in which funnel composition can reduce pointwise variance; this is a heuristic, not a guarantee for the shared-representation neural model. The estimator is paired with marginal split-conformal CATE summaries, combined through a Bonferroni union as audit bands, and a Lagrangian budgeted allocator using RCT-anchored estimates for subsidy-aware ROI accounting. On semi-synthetic multi-tier Criteo-MT7, FunnelCausalNet's mean AUUC_GMV is within one seed standard deviation of the leading feature-interaction baseline among eleven baselines, while a controlled ablation reduces GMV effect error versus direct GMV regression by 18--48% across tested zero-inflation regimes. On de-identified industrial Hotel-Coupon RCT logs with about 4.9 million hold-out exposure records per seed, expected-outcome evaluation sweeps full LP frontiers; FunnelCausalNet has the best seed-averaged mean DeltaROI at all seven correlated anchors from 10% to 60%, which we treat as descriptive frontier consistency rather than independent significance. On sparse binary-spend public benchmarks, revenue-focused rankers can dominate uplift-curve proxies, defining an explicit regime boundary.

</details>


### [306] [Drift and Dependence: Layer-wise Information-Theoretic Bounds for Replay-Based Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11690)
*Tieliang Gong,Zhongbo Zhang,Wen Wen,Yong-Jin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个逐层信息论框架，将持续学习中重放策略的泛化差距分解为表示漂移与优化依赖两部分，后者进一步解析为稳定性、可塑性、交互与残差耦合组件。通过Wasserstein松弛与SGLD实例化，得到深度相关的漂移-敏感性权衡及曲率感知的梯度对齐统计量，用于遗忘诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习中重放策略是缓解灾难性遗忘的有效方法，但其泛化行为受两个耦合效应影响：有限记忆的经验代理效应与共享优化轨迹导致的缓冲-数据-假设耦合。现有分析将这两个效应折叠为单一假设层面量，缺乏逐层解析，无法分离二者并定位需要稳定化的内部层。

Method: 构建逐层信息论框架，将泛化差距分解为重放诱导的表示漂移项与优化依赖项。优化项进一步分解为稳定性、可塑性、交互和残差耦合四个分量。采用两种精化手段使框架可操作：(1) 对漂移项施加Wasserstein松弛以处理支持失配，得到深度依赖的漂移-敏感性权衡；(2) 将优化项用SGLD实例化，化简为轨迹级对数行列式预算，得到曲率感知的梯度对齐统计量。

Result: 受控实验与基准实验验证了预期的记忆扩展规律、内部漏斗现象以及梯度对齐信号与遗忘之间的关联，确认了理论预测的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架成功分离了重放的记忆代理效应与优化轨迹耦合效应，提供了识别内部层稳定化目标的可操作准则，以及可在线监测任务级遗忘的统计诊断工具。

Abstract: Continual learning must absorb new tasks without erasing old ones, and replay---mixing a small buffer of past examples into current training---is among the most effective remedies for catastrophic forgetting. Yet its generalization behavior is shaped by two coupled effects that existing analyses fold into a single hypothesis-level quantity: finite memory replaces each past distribution with an empirical proxy, and repeated reuse couples the buffer, the current data, and the final hypothesis through a shared optimization trajectory. We develop a layer-wise information-theoretic framework that separates these effects at every depth. Our main result decomposes the expected generalization gap into a replay-induced representation drift and an optimization-dependence term, the latter further resolved into stability, plasticity, interaction, and residual-coupling components. Two refinements make the framework operational. A Wasserstein relaxation of the drift term, valid under support mismatch, yields a depth-dependent drift--sensitivity trade-off whose minimizer identifies which interior layer to stabilize. An SGLD instantiation of the optimization term reduces it to a trajectory-level log-determinant budget, exposing a curvature-aware gradient-alignment statistic that serves as an online diagnostic of task-wise forgetting. Controlled and benchmark experiments confirm the predicted memory scaling, the interior funnel, and the alignment signal's link to forgetting.

</details>


### [307] [LEMUR: Latent Entropy-aware Multimodal Unlearning via Visual-anchored Reasoning Redirection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11691)
*Xinhao Zhong,Yuxia Qiao,Junhao Li,Hao Fang,Yi Sun,Bin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现强化学习(RL)后训练赋予多模态大推理模型(MLRMs)的思维链(CoT)能力引入了独特的隐私漏洞：即使敏感事实已从最终答案中成功遗忘，模型仍可能在推理轨迹中复现该信息。基于RL诱导的探索在敏感内容上留下独特的token级熵特征这一观察，提出LEMUR——一种完全无需训练、推理时的遗忘框架，通过熵动态作为控制信号识别敏感推理的起始与终止，并在此区间内通过熵调制的视觉锚点潜变量注入引导推理轨迹转向消毒后的、基于输入图像重新扎根的概率加权嵌入。实验表明LEMUR在抑制推理轨迹和答案泄漏方面优于现有遗忘方法，同时更好地保留非敏感效用和输出流畅性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器遗忘(unlearning)方法仅关注最终答案层面的信息移除，未考虑RL训练的多模态推理模型在思维链中的隐私泄漏。RL诱导的探索行为使模型在理性推理过程中可能复现已被"遗忘"的敏感事实，且这一泄漏在原生RL训练的MLRMs中比基准模型更为显著，构成现有方法无法覆盖的新隐私风险。

Method: 提出LEMUR框架：(1) 利用RL训练带来的token级熵动态作为检测信号，识别敏感推理开始的时刻及消毒应停止的边界；(2) 在检测到的敏感区间内，通过熵调制的视觉锚点潜变量注入(entropy-modulated visual-anchor latent injection)重定向推理轨迹；(3) 用消毒后的、基于输入图像重新扎根的概率加权嵌入取代已承诺的敏感token。整个过程完全训练自由(inference-time)。

Result: 在多种MLRMs上，LEMUR在抑制推理轨迹和最终答案泄漏方面均一致优于现有遗忘方法，同时在保留非敏感效用和输出流畅性方面表现更好。

Conclusion: RL诱导的熵动态为隐私泄漏提供了独特而可靠的信号，利用该信号可以实现面向推理能力多模态模型的有效、无需训练的训练时遗忘，弥补了现有遗忘方法在推理轨迹隐私保护上的空白。

Abstract: Reinforcement-learning (RL) post-training equips multimodal large reasoning models (MLRMs) with exploratory chains of thought (CoT), substantially improving visual reasoning. However, we find that this capability introduces a distinct privacy vulnerability: even when a sensitive fact is successfully unlearned from the final answer, the model may still reproduce it in its reasoning trace. This leakage is substantially more pronounced in natively RL-trained MLRMs than in their non -reasoning base models, revealing a privacy risk that existing unlearning methods are not designed to address. We show that RL-induced exploration leaves sensitive content with a distinctive token-level entropy signature that is largely absent from base models. Based on this observation, we propose LEMUR, a fully training-free, inference-time unlearning framework for natively RL-trained multimodal models. LEMUR uses entropy dynamics as a control signal to identify when sensitive reasoning begins and when sanitization should stop. During this interval, it redirects the reasoning trajectory through entropy-modulated visual-anchor latent injection, replacing committed tokens with sanitized, probability-weighted embeddings re-grounded in the input image. Across diverse MLRMs, LEMUR consistently outperforms existing unlearning met hods in suppressing both reasoning-trace and answer leakage, while better preserving non-sensitive utility and output fluency. These results demonstrate that RL-induced entropy dynamics provide a distinctive signal for privacy leakage and that exploiting this signal enables effective training-free unlearning for reasoning-capable multimodal models.

</details>


### [308] [REOPD: Reliability-Adaptive Reward Extrapolation for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11698)
*Yang Sun,Lichao Ma,Houyuan Qin,Yuxin Liu,Hanyang Lu,Yao Zhu,Pinlong Cai,Guohang Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出REOPD框架，通过token级兼容性权重与batch级自适应预算结合，为每个token分配可变的奖励外推系数，避免全局单一λ导致的奖励黑客问题，在数学和代码任务上优于或匹配现有最强方法G-OPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奖励外推方法（如ExOPD）对所有token使用单一全局系数λ，这会迫使student去拟合隐式奖励中的极端峰值，导致奖励黑客（reward hacking）和训练不稳定；且最优λ因领域而异，需要昂贵的超参数搜索。

Method: REOPD将token级兼容性权重与batch级自适应预算相结合，得到token级系数λ_{b,t}=1+γ_b q_t。该系数在保持与teacher对齐的同时，仅沿可靠的teacher-reference方向进行选择性外推。系统不需要额外验证器、奖励模型、价值模型或额外rollout。

Result: REOPD在单teacher数学任务和多teacher的两个领域上均优于G-OPD，在单teacher代码任务上与G-OPD持平，展示了跨领域和teacher配置的细粒度可靠性适应能力。

Conclusion: REOPD通过细粒度的token级自适应外推有效解决了全局λ的问题，在保持训练稳定性的同时提升了OPD性能，且不增加系统复杂度。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own trajectories under dense token-level supervision from a teacher. Reward-extrapolation methods such as ExOPD amplify the teacher-reference log-likelihood ratio to move beyond direct imitation, but apply a single global coefficient $λ$ to every token. This can drive the student to fit extreme peaks in the implicit reward, causing reward hacking and unstable training, and the optimal $λ$ varies across domains, requiring costly sweeps. We propose REOPD, a reliability-adaptive reward extrapolation framework for OPD. REOPD combines a token-level compatibility weight with a batch-level adaptive budget, yielding a token-wise coefficient $λ_{b,t}=1+γ_b q_t$ that preserves teacher alignment while selectively extrapolating along reliable teacher-reference directions. It requires no verifier, reward model, value model, or extra rollout beyond standard OPD. REOPD outperforms G-OPD on single-teacher mathematics and on both domains in the multi-teacher setting, while matching G-OPD on single-teacher code, demonstrating effective fine-grained reliability adaptation across domains and teacher configurations.

</details>


### [309] [Consolidator: Learning Persistent Routed Memory Across Context Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11701)
*Sungwoo Goo,Hwi-yeol Yun,Sangkeun Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在Phasor Memory Network (PMNet)中引入共享的slot-local操作符Consolidator，将短期记忆(STM)变换后累积到长期记忆(LTM)中，而无需重放源token。实验表明，这种学习式的整合机制不仅保留了LTM内容，还通过层次化路由器影响后续的显式记忆槽位选择，在双段模10映射任务上将更新映射的召回率从44.38%提升至87.02%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅仅将短期记忆拷贝到较慢的存储中可以跨上下文边界保存状态，但持久化本身并不能保证保留的状态能影响后续的记忆访问。作者旨在区分"内容保留"与"状态影响访问"这两个概念，并检验是否可以通过学习式整合让LTM既作为可检索内容，又作为决定后续槽位选择的访问状态。

Method: 采用PMNet架构，引入Consolidator——一个共享的slot-local操作符，将路由后的STM变换后累积进LTM，不重放源token。每次整合后将KV cache和STM清空，保留的LTM既可直接被读取，也反馈给层次化路由器以调节后续输入的显式记忆槽位访问。整个模型仅12.35K参数（Consolidator）可训练，占30M模型的0.041%。实验在双段模10映射任务上进行：第一段建立映射，第二段在同一地址更新映射，经过第二次整合和重置后，用held-out查询从LTM恢复更新后的映射。

Result: 在五次从相同STM预训练检查点配对的运行中：直接LTM路由将更新映射召回率从44.38±1.94%提升到87.02±1.76%（+42.64个pp）；立即STM召回率两种条件下均为89.90%；学习式整合比强制恒等累积在无路由时高21.40±1.91个pp，有路由时高68.70±1.76个pp。

Conclusion: 在该任务上，学习式整合（consolidation）后的LTM同时具备双重功能：既是可检索的内容存储，又是塑造后续槽位选择的访问状态。这说明经过学习的整合过程能够使持久化记忆真正影响未来的记忆访问行为，而不仅仅是保存状态。

Abstract: Copying short-term memory (STM) into a slower store can preserve state across a context boundary, but persistence alone does not ensure that the retained state influences subsequent memory access. We test this distinction in a Phasor Memory Network (PMNet) using Consolidator, a shared slot-local operator that transforms routed STM before accumulating it into long-term memory (LTM), without replaying the source tokens. After each consolidation, the KV cache and STM are cleared. The retained LTM can still be read and is also fed into the hierarchical router, thereby conditioning which explicit-memory slots subsequent inputs access. We evaluate this mechanism on a two-segment modulo-10 mapping task in which the second segment updates the mapping at the same memory address. Following a second consolidation and reset, a held-out query must recover the updated mapping from LTM. The backbone and memory interface are frozen, leaving only 12.35K Consolidator parameters trainable (0.041\% of a 29.95M model). Across five paired runs from the same STM-pretraining checkpoint, direct LTM routing raises updated-mapping recall from $44.38\pm1.94\%$ to $87.02\pm1.76\%$ ($+42.64\pm1.10$ percentage points), while immediate STM recall remains 89.90\% in both conditions; both train separate Consolidators and retain the same LTM read paths. Learned consolidation outperforms forced identity accumulation by $21.40\pm1.91$ percentage points without routing and $68.70\pm1.76$ with routing. Thus, on this task, consolidated LTM serves as both retrievable content and an access state that shapes subsequent slot selection.

</details>


### [310] [Robust and Efficient Noisy-Label Time-Series Classification via Dynamic Time Warping Based Granular Ball Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11704)
*Ziqiang Li,Yun Liu,Gouhei Tanaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于动态时间规整的粒球计算方法（DTW-GBC），将时间上相似的训练样本组织成粒球并在粒球层级进行分类，以提升基于DTW的最近邻分类器对标签噪声的鲁棒性，同时显著减少推理时的DTW计算次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于DTW的最近邻分类器在时间序列分类中表现良好，但对训练样本的误标注（标签噪声）较为敏感，且推理时需要大量DTW计算，效率低下。现有方法缺乏同时解决鲁棒性和推理效率问题的方案。

Method: 提出DTW-GBC框架，将时间上相似的训练样本组织成粒球，并在粒球层面进行分类。文中进一步设计了两种粒球构建策略来生成粒球。通过在粒球粒度上进行分类，减少对单个噪声样本的依赖，并降低推理时的比较次数。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上、采用对称标签噪声的实验表明，DTW-GBC的两种变体普遍能够缓解标签噪声导致的性能下降，同时在推理时所需的比较次数远少于DTW-based 1-NN。

Conclusion: DTW-GBC在分类鲁棒性和推理效率之间取得了良好的平衡，是一种有效的替代DTW-based 1-NN的时间序列分类方法。

Abstract: Dynamic Time Warping (DTW)-based Nearest-Neighbor (NN) classifiers are effective for time-series classification but are vulnerable to mislabeled training samples and require numerous DTW computations during inference. We propose DTW-based Granular Ball Computing (DTW-GBC), which organizes temporally similar training samples into granular balls and performs classification at the granule level. We further develop two granular-ball construction strategies for DTW-GBC. Experiments on four benchmark datasets with symmetric label noise show that the two DTW-GBC variants generally mitigate the performance degradation caused by label noise while requiring substantially fewer comparisons than DTW-based 1-NN during inference. These findings suggest that DTW-GBC provides a favorable balance between classification robustness and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [311] [High-dimensional Multi-objective Bayesian Optimization with Learned Variable Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11713)
*Hongyan Wang,Jiayu Huang,Haotian Zheng,Xin Gao,Chi Ding,Ying Liu,Xia Wang,Qing Xu,Keqiang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ViaMOBO框架，利用决策变量交互分析将高维决策空间完全或部分划分，并在子空间内进行局部多目标贝叶斯优化，从而解决传统MOBO方法在高维空间中指数级采样复杂度过高的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多目标贝叶斯优化方法在处理高维决策空间时面临指数级采样复杂度，严重限制了其在实际昂贵黑箱问题中的应用。

Method: 通过变量交互分析模型判断决策空间是否可完全或部分划分，识别目标的可分性（可分、部分可分或不可分），进而在划分的子空间内执行局部贝叶斯优化。

Result: 在合成与真实基准上，ViaMOBO在逼近高维昂贵多目标问题的Pareto前沿方面优于现有MOBO基线方法。

Conclusion: ViaMOBO是一个通用的高维多目标贝叶斯优化框架，通过变量交互分析有效降低采样复杂度，无需强假设即可处理可分及不可分问题。

Abstract: Multi-objective Bayesian optimization (MOBO) is effective in identifying the Pareto fronts for expensive black-box problems. However, most current MOBO approaches are limited to low-dimensional decision space due to its exponential sampling complexity. This paper presents decision variable interaction analysis-based MOBO, ViaMOBO, a generic framework for expensive multi-objective problems with high-dimensional decision space. The key idea of ViaMOBO is that it utilizes a variable interaction analysis model to determine whether the decision space can be completely or partially divided, and then performs local Bayesian optimization in the divided decision subspaces. Through the variable analysis model, it can be derived whether the objectives in black-box problems are separable, partially separable, or non-separable based on the potential independent or interdependent relationships among decision variables without any strong assumptions. We compare ViaMOBO with the state-of-the-art MOBO methods on both synthetic and real-world benchmarks. The experimental results demonstrate that ViaMOBO outperforms other related MOBO baselines in approximating the Pareto front of high-dimensional expensive multi-objective problems.

</details>


### [312] [Chain-of-Thought Shows the Path to a Tree: Realizing Branching Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11716)
*Debanjan Dutta,Anish Chakrabarty,Swagatam Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文填补了有界深度Transformer中CoT表达能力的空白，设计了基于硬注意力、最多两层解码器的显式DFS和Dijkstra算法，并以此为共享计算基板，实现树Strahler数（4层、2n-1步）和树宽度（3层、n-1步）的线性步数计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有理论将CoT步数与电路复杂度类联系起来，但缺乏具体的深度有界构造和遍历过程的实例化。本文旨在为分支复杂度提供显式的、深度有界的具体构造。

Method: 利用唯一硬注意力解码器构建至多两层的有界深度DFS和Dijkstra算法；以DFS和Dijkstra作为共享计算基板，重用其解码器来实现Strahler数和树宽度的计算；并利用DFS生成Dyck路径的构造，给出基于路径表示的两个度量独立构造。

Result: Strahler数在2n-1步、4层内计算；树宽度在n-1步、3层内计算。构造支持任意n叉树，无需层归一化或位置编码。由于该项是NC¹完全问题，这为CoT层次的线性步数机制提供了非平凡证据。

Conclusion: 本文为CoT层级中线性步数机制的NC¹完全问题提供了深度有界的显式构造证据，并通过树的Strahler数与Dyck路径的经典双射进一步巩固了该计算框架的普适性。

Abstract: Chain of Thought (CoT) lifts the expressive ceiling of bounded-depth Transformers, with characterizations tying the number of CoT steps to circuit complexity classes. What remains largely missing are concrete instantiations with explicit, depth-bounded constructions, and the traversal procedures such characterizations presuppose. We close this gap for branching complexity. We give CoT realizations of depth-first search (DFS) and of Dijkstra algorithm, the latter subsuming breadth-first search, by unique hard-attention decoders of at most two layers, and use them as a shared computational substrate: reusing the DFS decoder yields the Strahler number of an $n$-vertex tree in $2n-1$ steps with four layers, and reusing the Dijkstra decoder yields its width in $n-1$ steps with three. Since computing the Strahler number of a binary tree given as a term is \textsf{NC\textsuperscript{1}}-complete, and our constructions handle arbitrary $n$-ary trees without layer normalization or positional encodings, this is a non-trivial witness for the linear-step regime of the CoT hierarchy. Exploiting the classical bijection between ordered trees and Dyck paths, itself realized by our DFS construction, which emits the path as it traverses, we give independent constructions for both measures on the path representation.

</details>


### [313] [Epiplexity Guided Data Selection and Generation for Out-of-Distribution Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11746)
*Ellen Su,Andres Potapczynski,Shikai Qiu,Edward Hughes,Andrew Gordon Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将"结构信息量"（epiplexity）这一度量作为在线训练信号，用于数据选择和合成数据生成。通过拟合缩放律预测跨域数据的epiplexity增益来动态调整采样权重，并用REINFORCE策略梯度引导生成器朝最大化epiplexity的方向优化。实验表明更高的epiplexity能提升零样本和微调任务的跨域迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代系统需要迁移到训练时未见过的任务，但何种数据有利于这种跨任务泛化尚不明确。作者假设结构信息更丰富的数据可能包含可复用的共享电路和子程序，从而在更广泛的下游场景中发挥作用，需要机制来量化和利用这种关系。

Method: 1) 数据选择：为自然数据域的训练损失曲线拟合缩放律，预测epiplexity随训练token数的增益，据此自适应调整各域的采样权重；2) 合成数据生成：将生成器的奖励定义为在先前生成数据缓冲区上学习者epiplexity的变化，使用REINFORCE策略梯度引导生成器朝向最大化epiplexity的分布。

Result: 在两种方案中，更高的epiplexity均预测了更优的零样本和微调任务下游性能，支持了"富含结构信息的数据能产生跨域迁移的表征"这一假设。

Conclusion: 数据中蕴含的结构信息（以epiplexity度量）是跨域迁移泛化的关键因素，将epiplexity作为在线训练信号可以有效地用于数据选择与合成，从而提升模型在未预见任务上的表现。

Abstract: Modern systems are increasingly expected to transfer across tasks not specified during training. What data facilitates generalization in these new, unanticipated settings? One hypothesis is that data with more structural information could contain shared circuits and subprograms that could be recycled in a wider array of downstream settings. Epiplexity, a recently proposed measure of the structural information a compute-bounded learner can extract from data, provides a mechanism to reason about this relationship. In this paper, we show how to operationalize epiplexity as an online training signal for data selection and synthetic data generation. For selection, we fit scaling laws to the training loss curves of natural data domains to predict the expected epiplexity gain as a function of training tokens, and use this signal to adaptively determine the sampling weights over domains during training. For synthetic data generation, we define a generator's reward as the change in learner epiplexity over a buffer of previously generated data and use REINFORCE policy gradients to guide the generator toward an epiplexity-maximizing distribution. In both cases, higher epiplexity predicts improved downstream performance on zero-shot and fine-tuning based tasks, supporting the hypothesis that data rich in structural information yield representations that transfer across domains.

</details>


### [314] [MOON: Multi-Objective OrthoNormalized Updates for Multitask Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11749)
*Shiji Zhou,Kunlin Lyu,Lei Zhang,Ruodong Wang,Yifan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MOON方法，在谱范数-核范数几何下进行多目标优化的梯度操作，替代传统欧几里得空间中的梯度操作，从而在保留参数矩阵结构的同时获得更陡峭的下降方向，提升多任务学习的优化效率和最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MOO方法将模型参数展平为向量并在欧氏几何下进行梯度操作，忽略了现代架构（如Transformer）中参数的矩阵结构。作者指出欧氏空间中的梯度操作在矩阵几何下并不一定产生最陡下降方向，从而可能限制优化效率。

Method: 提出MOON（Multi-Objective OrthoNormalized Updates）：基于矩阵值参数的最陡下降理论，在谱范数-核范数几何下执行梯度操作，并利用正交归一化后的操作梯度进行参数更新。同时在理论层面，针对光滑非凸目标，分别给出确定性和随机梯度设定下的收敛性分析。

Result: 理论上，在光滑非凸目标下，确定性设定中平均Pareto驻点度量的收敛率为O(T^{-1/2})，随机梯度设定下为O(T^{-1/4})。实验上，在多个基准任务中，MOON一致地提升了优化效率和最终多任务性能。

Conclusion: MOON通过在矩阵几何下进行梯度操作，有效弥补了欧氏空间梯度操作的不足，为多任务学习中的多目标优化提供了更高效且理论上可保证收敛的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-objective optimization (MOO) has demonstrated significant success in multi-task learning by mitigating task conflicts through gradient manipulation. However, most existing methods flatten model parameters into vectors and perform gradient manipulation under Euclidean geometry, thereby overlooking the matrix structure prevalent in modern architectures such as Transformers. In this paper, we show that gradient manipulation in Euclidean space does not generally yield the steepest descent direction under matrix geometry, potentially limiting optimization efficiency. Drawing from the theory of steepest descent for matrix-valued parameters, we propose MOON (Multi-Objective OrthoNormalized Updates), which performs gradient manipulation under spectral--nuclear norm geometry and uses the orthonormalized manipulated gradient for parameter updates. Theoretically, for smooth non-convex objectives, we establish convergence of the averaged Pareto-stationarity measure at rates of $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/2})$ in the deterministic setting and $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/4})$ under stochastic gradients. Empirical results across various benchmarks show that MOON consistently improves both optimization efficiency and final multi-task performance. Our code is available at https://github.com/KunlinLyu/MOON.

</details>


### [315] [TradingMoE: Routing the Right Experts in Evolving Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11785)
*Chang Zhou,Xingtong Yu,Minbin Huang,Zhennan Wu,Yuan Fang,Hong Cheng,Xinming Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TradingMoE，一种面向交易场景的稀疏混合专家（MoE）系统，通过查询-键路由器将token所需专业知识表示为低维查询并与可学习专家键匹配，同时引入稀疏专家选择更新机制，使得路由器能够随市场条件变化动态更新专家选择，在股票和加密货币市场上相比22个基线分别提升累计收益30.89%和30.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在金融分析与交易中展现潜力，但直接交易面临挑战，因为预测能力需随资产、决策领域和市场条件而变化。现有LLM交易系统要么协调人类定义的外部专家，要么采用传统内部MoE路由器，但后者不能直接评估单个专家对交易决策的贡献，且无法获得指示"不活跃专家何时变得更为合适"的直接信号。研究发现原生路由器分数不能很好反映专家对交易决策的实际改善程度，并揭示token级专家效用具有紧凑的低维结构，由此启发了新方法。

Method: 提出TradingMoE，一种面向交易的稀疏MoE系统：1）在冻结的密集LLM上增加轻量残差专家；2）引入Query-Key路由器，将当前市场情境下每个token所需的专业知识表示为低维查询，并与可学习的专家键匹配；3）提出稀疏专家选择更新机制，在训练期间抽样少数不活跃专家，估计其是否应替换当前Top-k路由中最弱的专家，从而在市场条件变化时更新专家选择，同时保持稀疏计算。

Result: 在22个基线的对照实验中，TradingMoE在股票市场将累计收益较最佳基线提升30.89%，在加密货币市场提升30.7%；滚动纸面交易（paper-trading）实验进一步表明其优势在forward-only部署下依然保持。

Conclusion: TradingMoE通过将专家选择与交易决策质量直接关联，并利用专家效用的低维结构实现轻量化路由，有效解决了LLM在直接交易任务中动态专家分配的问题，在多个市场条件下均取得显著优于现有方法的收益表现，且具备良好的泛化与部署能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong potential for financial analysis and trading, but direct trading remains challenging because the predictive capabilities required can vary across assets, decision fields, and market conditions. Existing LLM-based trading systems either coordinate human-defined external experts or adopt conventional internal Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) routers that do not directly evaluate how individual experts contribute to trading decisions. Moreover, these routers receive no direct signal indicating when an inactive expert has become more suitable as market conditions change. We find that native router scores poorly reflect how much individual experts improve trading decisions, frequently leaving better alternatives unselected. We further reveal that token-specific expert usefulness exhibits a compact low-dimensional structure. Based on these findings, we propose TradingMoE, a trading-oriented sparse MoE that augments a frozen dense LLM with lightweight residual experts. We introduce a Query-Key router that represents the expertise required by each token under the current market context as a low-dimensional query and matches it with learnable expert keys. We further propose a sparse expert selection update mechanism that samples a few inactive experts during training and estimates whether they should replace the weakest expert in the current Top-k route. This mechanism enables the router to update expert selection as market conditions change while preserving sparse computation. Experiments against 22 baselines on stock and cryptocurrency markets show that TradingMoE improves cumulative return over the best-performing baselines by 30.89% and 30.7%, respectively. Rolling paper-trading experiments further demonstrate that its advantage persists under forward-only deployment.

</details>


### [316] [High-Order Liquid Evidence Encoding for Gradual GNSS Spoofing Detection in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11790)
*Muhammad Ayub Sabir,Junbiao Pang,Fatima Ashraf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于因果高阶液态证据框架的GNSS欺骗检测方法，通过构造物理引导的GNSS-运动不一致残差及其一阶、二阶离散变化作为多流证据，利用自适应液态编码器进行层级耦合，实现对连续且微妙GNSS欺骗攻击的检测。在AV-GPS数据集三个子集上的实验表明，该方法在数据集1和数据集3上取得最优F1分数（0.9535和0.9777）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续且微妙的GNSS欺骗攻击难以检测，因为单个GNSS观测可能看似合理，而GNSS隐含位移与车载运动之间的不一致性是逐渐累积的。现有方法多依赖静态车辆行为特征或单一残差信号，未显式建模这种演化过程，导致检测能力有限。

Method: 首先通过比较GNSS隐含位移与车载运动推导位移，构造物理引导的GNSS-运动不一致残差；然后针对残差水平及其一阶和二阶离散变化分别构建证据流，并依据证据阶数选择相关上下文线索；每条流由独立的自适应液态编码器处理，产生的时序状态通过层级耦合，仅利用当前和过往观测来预测窗口端点的欺骗事件。

Result: 在真实世界AV-GPS数据集的三个子集上进行实验，该方法在数据集1和数据集3上取得最高的F1分数，分别达到0.9535和0.9777；在数据集3上，能在四个采样步内检测到两个标注的正常到攻击的转换。

Conclusion: 所提出的因果高阶液态证据框架通过显式建模GNSS与车载运动不一致性的演化过程，有效提升了连续微妙GNSS欺骗攻击的检测性能，代码和数据集已公开以促进可复现研究。

Abstract: Accurate Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS)-based localization is essential for safe and reliable autonomous driving. However, spoofing attacks can manipulate vehicle position estimates. Continuous and subtle attacks are particularly difficult to detect because individual GNSS observations may remain plausible while the inconsistency between GNSS-implied displacement and onboard vehicle motion gradually increases. Existing methods often rely on static vehicle-behavior features or a single residual signal and do not explicitly model this evolution. To address this problem, we propose a causal high-order liquid evidence framework for GNSS spoofing detection. The method first constructs a physics-guided GNSS--motion inconsistency residual by comparing GNSS-implied displacement with onboard-motion-derived displacement. It then forms separate evidence streams for the residual level and its first- and second-order discrete variations, with relevant contextual cues selected according to the evidence order. Each stream is processed by a separate adaptive liquid encoder, and the resulting temporal states are hierarchically coupled to predict spoofing at the window endpoint using only current and past observations. Experiments on three subsets of the real-world AV-GPS dataset show that the proposed method achieves the highest F1-scores among the evaluated temporal models on Dataset~1 and Dataset~3, reaching 0.9535 and 0.9777, respectively. On Dataset~3, it detects both labeled normal-to-attack transitions within four sampling steps. Code and datasets are publicly available at: https://github.com/pangjunbiao/GNSS_Spoofing.git.

</details>


### [317] [Orientation, not magnitude: the causal structure of task-vector interference in merged language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11797)
*Chencheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过精确的因子化核算和直接干预，发现模型合并（任务算术）中"量级"（magnitude）作为诊断轴并不充分且跨模型家族不一致。跨项（cross-term）的逐层通量主要由现有跨项的被放大传输主导（约65-70%），擦除后会被前向传播重建至99%范数。被携带的方向是前向传播的吸引子，且具有因果承载性：沿该方向擦除能剂量依赖地移除表达干扰并在精确擦除处饱和，而范数匹配的错误方向对照失败甚至适得其反。指令包装（instruction wrapper）会门控该效应。所有46个预测均在数据收集前预注册并冻结，包括对自身假设的反证也如实报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并（task arithmetic）在实际应用中常常失效，现有文献用"量级"来解释失败原因（如逐层表示偏差、偏离跨任务线性、参数重叠等），但这些解释缺乏精确验证。作者试图弄清量级是否是可靠的诊断轴，并通过精确分解和直接干预来揭示跨项（cross-term）的真实行为机制。

Method: 作者采用因子化账本（factorial ledger）精确追踪合并LLMs的逐层跨项，并直接干预该跨项。具体包括：精确分解逐层通量、擦除跨项并观察传播重建、对六种起始位移进行盆地测试、沿携带方向进行因果擦除实验并与范数匹配的错误方向对照比较，还引入指令包装器测试效应的门控作用，并进行长度匹配对照验证。

Result: ①跨项通量由现有跨项的放大传输主导（两家族均约65-70%，后期块增益>1）；②擦除操作会被前向传播重建（重建至99%范数、余弦0.99），除非在靠近输出处应用；③携带方向是前向传播的吸引子，且因果承载性强：沿该方向擦除呈剂量依赖地移除干扰并在精确擦除处饱和；④范数匹配的错误方向对照失败或适得其反；⑤指令包装器会门控效应——在内部放大跨项的包装器下，相同擦除移除的相对干扰少13倍，因为包装器将交互淹没在模板固定的主效应中，而非缩小它；⑥"±15%的bfloat16通用性"实为量化粗糙度；⑦局部跨项生成差异仅1.9倍的任务对，其可因果移除的干扰差异可达14x-337x。

Conclusion: 量级至多是粗略的相关量，而非可靠的因果诊断轴；跨项的方向性（carried direction）及其在前向传播中的吸引子动力学才是模型合并失效的核心机制。该结论跨模型家族基本一致，但量级指标本身并不一致。所有46个预测均为预注册并在数据收集前冻结，包括对自身头条预期和行为恢复（在验证过的连续端点下）的反证也如实报告，体现了严格的方法学承诺。

Abstract: Model merging by task arithmetic works until it doesn't, and the field diagnoses why with magnitudes: layerwise representation bias, deviations from cross-task linearity, parameter overlap. Tracking the exact layerwise cross-term of merged LLMs through a factorial ledger and intervening on it directly, we find magnitude insufficient - and inconsistent across model families - as a diagnostic axis. An exact decomposition of the layerwise flux shows it is dominated by amplifying transport of the existing cross-term (~65-70% in both families, gain >1 per late block), and erasing the term is undone by propagation - rebuilt to 99% of its norm at cosine 0.99 - unless applied near the output; a basin test with six starting displacements establishes the carried direction as an attractor of the forward pass. That direction is causally load-bearing: erasure along it removes expressed interference dose-dependently and saturates at exact erasure, while norm-matched wrong-direction controls fail or backfire. Instruction wrappers gate the effect: the same erasure finds 13x less relative interference to remove under a wrapper that internally amplifies the cross-term, because the wrapper drowns the interaction in a template-pinned main effect rather than shrinking it - a structure that replicates across further instruction templates but not under a length-matched control. Magnitude, by contrast, is at best a coarse correlate, and the striking +-15% "universality" of naive bfloat16 generation turns out to be quantization roughness. Task pairs whose local cross-term generation differs by at most 1.9x differ by 14x-337x in causally removable interference. All 46 predictions were preregistered and frozen before their data; falsifications, including of our own headline expectations and of behavioral recovery under a validated continuous endpoint, are reported as such.

</details>


### [318] [JAPE: Joint Anomaly Prediction and Intrinsic Explanation in Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11801)
*Yian Wei,Yuanyuan Yao,Lu Chen,Xiangmin Zhou,Tianyi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: JAPE是一个联合异常预测与解释框架，将多元时间序列异常预测从数值偏差建模提升到依赖结构建模，通过解耦时空表示、双视角告警机制和原生预测性解释，在五个真实基准上平均F1提升19.7%、AUC-PR提升41.3%、MRR提升26.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常预测方法主要将异常刻画为未来数值的偏差，忽略了弱异常前兆引发的细微依赖结构变化，且无法在发出警报的同时提供原生的变量级解释。

Method: JAPE包含三大核心组件：(i) 解耦时空表示（DSTR）骨干网络，通过解耦时间与空间建模，并利用可学习的滞后聚合来捕获带滞后的依赖关系，从而在数值偏差出现前感知结构前兆；(ii) 双视角告警机制，融合数值预测与演化依赖图进行逐点异常预测，即使在细微数值偏差下也能捕获结构证据；(iii) 原生预测性解释（NPE），直接复用预测得到的依赖图对变量按结构偏差排序，无需额外模型或训练。

Result: 在五个真实世界基准、三个预测时域上的实验表明，JAPE平均F1提升19.7%，AUC-PR提升41.3%，解释质量MRR提升26.6%。

Conclusion: JAPE是首个显式建模演化依赖结构以同时实现逐点告警和原生变量级解释的异常预测框架，验证了从数值偏差建模转向依赖结构建模的有效性。

Abstract: Multivariate time-series anomaly prediction aims to identify whether and when anomalies will occur over a future horizon from historical observations. Existing methods primarily characterize anomalies as deviations in future numerical values, which may overlook subtle dependency changes induced by weak anomaly precursors and provide no native variable-level explanation together with the alert. To bridge these gaps, we propose JAPE, a Joint Anomaly Prediction and Explanation framework that lifts anomaly prediction from numerical-deviation modeling to dependency-structure modeling. JAPE is the first anomaly prediction framework to explicitly model evolving dependency structures for both point-wise alerting and native variable-level explanation. Specifically, JAPE (i) proposes a Decoupled Spatio-Temporal Representation (DSTR) backbone that decouples temporal and spatial modeling and captures lag-aware dependencies via learnable lag aggregation, thereby perceiving structural precursors before numerical deviations emerge; (ii) designs a dual-view alerting mechanism that fuses numerical forecasts with evolving dependency graphs for point-wise anomaly prediction, capturing structural evidence even under subtle numerical deviations; and (iii) presents Native Predictive Explanation (NPE), which directly reuses the predicted dependency graphs to rank variables by structural deviations without additional models or training. Extensive experiments on five real-world benchmarks across three prediction horizons demonstrate that JAPE improves average F1 and AUC-PR by 19.7% and 41.3%, respectively, while improving explainability with 26.6% gain in MRR.

</details>


### [319] [Learning with Bilevel-Minimax Optimization for Efficient and Reliable Transfer Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11815)
*Yaohua Liu,Yifan Guo,Jiaxin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BMAT（Bilevel-Minimax Adversarial Transfer）框架，从双层-最小最大博弈视角重新审视基于迁移的黑盒对抗攻击。通过双层公式捕捉初始化与扰动间的依赖关系，内层极小极大问题提升代理模型的跨架构泛化鲁棒性。算法上采用集成自底向上求解器，结合Soft Weight Modulator与Implicit Gradient Approximator，实现初始化、代理适应与扰动优化的三元耦合。在分类和分割基准上，BMAT在超过30个受害者模型上超越10余个强基线，跨架构迁移效果显著，最高可将mIoU降低2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统迁移对抗攻击主要聚焦于扰动生成本身，而忽略了可迁移性本质上由初始化、代理模型适应和梯度动力学的耦合所决定。现有方法未能系统建模这三者之间的依赖关系，导致跨架构迁移性能受限。

Method: 1) 提出双层-最小最大（bilevel-minimax）形式化框架：外层双层公式刻画初始化与扰动的依赖关系，内层极小极大问题增强代理模型的跨架构鲁棒性。2) 设计自底向上集成求解器，包含Soft Weight Modulator（软权重调制器）和Implicit Gradient Approximator（隐式梯度逼近器），实现初始化、代理适应与扰动优化三者的联合优化。3) 提供双层-最小最大框架的优化动力学理论分析。

Result: 在分类和分割基准上，BMAT在超过30个受害者模型上击败10余个强基线，在架构内和跨架构迁移上均取得显著提升，跨架构迁移中最高可实现mIoU降低2倍的效果。

Conclusion: BMAT通过将初始化、代理适应与扰动优化进行三元耦合，为迁移对抗攻击提供了统一的理论框架和高效的算法实现，显著提升了跨架构对抗迁移性能，并验证了双层-最小最大视角在对抗迁移问题中的有效性。

Abstract: Transfer-based adversarial attacks craft adversarial examples using surrogate models to mislead black-box victim models. Beyond perturbation generation, transferability is fundamentally governed by the coupling of initialization, surrogate adaptation, and gradient dynamics. We revisit this challenge from a bilevel-minimax perspective and propose BMAT (Bilevel-Minimax Adversarial Transfer). The bilevel formulation captures the dependency between initialization and perturbation, while the inner minimax problem promotes surrogate robustness for cross-architecture generalization. Algorithmically, we develop an integrated bottom-up solver that combines a Soft Weight Modulator and an Implicit Gradient Approximator to enable ternary coupling among initialization, surrogate adaptation, and perturbation optimization. We further provide theoretical insights into the optimization dynamics of the proposed bilevel-minimax framework. Extensive experiments on classification and segmentation benchmarks show that BMAT outperforms more than 10 strong baselines across more than 30 victim models, improving both intra- and cross-architecture transfer and yielding up to a 2x reduction in mIoU. Code is available at https://github.com/callous-youth/BMAT.

</details>


### [320] [Towards Understanding On-Policy Distillation through the Lens of Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11829)
*Xinmu Ge,Zizhuo Zhang,Yu Huang,Jianing Zhu,Lin Yuan,Wanli Gu,Weichang Wu,Weiran Huang,Xiaolu Zhang,Bo Han,Jun Zhou,Jiangchao Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过测试时扩展（test-time scaling）视角，以pass@K和avg@K为评估指标，系统性地重新审视了on-policy distillation（OPD）在LLM推理中的作用。研究发现OPD主要提升采样效率而非真正扩展学生的推理能力边界，并提出了"illusory distillation"（幻觉式蒸馏）的概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍认为OPD能使学生模型从更强的教师模型中蒸馏知识，从而扩展其推理能力。但这一假设缺乏系统性的验证，尤其是基于测试时采样预算K的变化来评估OPD的真实增益。

Method: 通过变化采样预算K，使用pass@K和avg@K两个指标评估多个OPD变体；分析OPD训练过程中pass@K的动态变化；基于pass@1024进行问题级别的可解性分析（solvability analysis），比较OPD前后问题的可解性转换。

Result: 1) OPD训练的模型在所有采样预算下保持avg@K优势，但pass@K优势随K增大逐渐转移至基础模型；2) OPD训练过程显示从小K性能增强到大K能力边界损失的概率性转移；3) 基于pass@1024的问题级可解性分析显示，OPD使更多原本可解的问题变得不可解，而非使原本不可解的问题变为可解。

Conclusion: OPD主要提升采样效率而非真正扩展推理能力边界，其表面增益是"幻觉式"的：来自采样效率提升而非从教师模型获取真正的新推理能力。这挑战了传统上对OPD能力蒸馏机制的认知。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has emerged as a promising post-training technique for enhancing LLM reasoning. It is commonly believed to enable the student model to distill knowledge from a stronger teacher model, thereby expanding capabilities beyond the pre-OPD base model. In this study, we examine this view through the lens of test-time scaling by varying the sampling budget K and evaluating performance with pass@K and avg@K. Specifically, across several OPD variants, we observe that OPD-trained models maintain superior avg@K performance across sampling budgets, while the advantage in pass@K gradually shifts to the pre-OPD base models as K increases. These results suggest that OPD primarily improves sampling efficiency rather than consistently expanding the student's reasoning capability boundary. The pass@K dynamics throughout OPD training further reveal a progressive shift toward stronger small-K performance at the expense of the large-K capability boundary. Furthermore, a problem-level solvability analysis using pass@1024 as the criterion reveals an asymmetry: OPD causes more previously solvable problems to become unsolvable than previously unsolvable problems to become solvable. Together, these findings suggest that, from the perspective of capability expansion, OPD behaves more like an "illusory distillation": its apparent gains arise primarily from improved sampling efficiency rather than from acquiring genuinely new reasoning capabilities from the teacher.

</details>


### [321] [Kernel Methods for Learning Operators with Multiple Inputs and Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11831)
*Adrien Weihs,Chunyang Liao,Jingmin Sun,Hayden Schaeffer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于核的通用编码器-解码器框架用于算子学习，将观测、表示、学习和重建分离，支持多输入多输出场景，并引入了KernelMO系列核方法。在五类参数化PDE上达到竞争性或SOTA精度，同时比神经算子架构训练和推理成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在科学机器学习中，学习无限维对象之间的映射（算子学习）是核心挑战。现有的神经算子方法通常计算成本高，且缺乏严谨的逼近理论支撑。作者旨在提出一种兼具数学可处理性和计算效率的通用框架，以弥补该领域理论与实践之间的鸿沟。

Method: 1) 构建通用的核基编码器-解码器算子学习框架，将算子学习流程解耦为观测、表示、学习和重建四个独立环节。2) 发展多输入多输出算子学习的逼近理论，证明收敛率由最具挑战性的构成性逼近子问题决定，而非整体问题维度。3) 提出KernelMO系列方法，包含算子值核方法和乘积空间核方法两种互补的算法实现，均具有闭式训练和推理形式。

Result: 理论结果表明，即使输入输出数量增加，收敛速率不受整体维度限制，而是由最难的子逼近问题主导。在五族参数化偏微分方程上，KernelMO方法达到竞争性或最优预测精度，同时训练和推理成本显著低于神经算子架构及深度学习模型。

Conclusion: 该框架证明了基于核的方法可以在算子学习中作为高效、轻量级替代方案，在保持数学严谨性的同时提供闭式解，为算子学习提供了一条不依赖深度神经网络的高效路径。

Abstract: Learning mappings between infinite-dimensional objects is a central challenge in scientific machine learning. We introduce a general kernel-based encoder-decoder framework for operator learning that separates observation, representation, learning, and reconstruction. We develop this framework for multi-input, multi-output operator learning, where operators map between products of potentially distinct function spaces. Our approximation theory shows that, although the number of inputs and outputs can increase, the convergence rate is governed by the most challenging constituent approximation problem rather than the overall problem dimension. The framework leads to practical kernel methods with closed-form training and inference, combining mathematical tractability with computational efficiency. We further specialize the approach to multiple operator learning by introducing KernelMO, a family of kernel methods with complementary operator-valued and product-space formulations. Across five families of parametric partial differential equations, the proposed methods achieve competitive or state-of-the-art predictive accuracy while reducing training and inference costs relative to neural operator architectures and deep learning based models, offering an efficient and lightweight alternative.

</details>


### [322] [Air Quality Station Simulation via LSTM and Attention-Based Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11839)
*Alexander Kostadinov,Petar O. Hristov,Dessislava Petrova-Antonova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个名为SATADL的深度学习模型，用于模拟故障或断电导致数据不可用的空气质量监测站的测量数据，输出多小时前向的空气质量预报。该模型在全球四组空气质監站数据上进行了验证，在长达48小时的假设性故障期间模拟PM10浓度，在决定系数和均方根误差上均优于多个基线和已发表的深度学习模型，被证明适合作为虚拟替代站。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市空气质量监测依赖测量站的可靠持续运行，但硬件故障和停电会导致数据不可用，影响整体分析质量。为了在监测站恢复运行前填补数据空白，需要一个能够模拟不可用站点测量的模型，从而维持空气质量分析和决策的连续性。

Method: 设计了一个名为SATADL的深度学习模型，其架构能够从数据的不同方面提取信息，推断复杂的关系并输出多小时前向的空气质量预测。模型各组件被逐一详细描述和检验。实验在来自全球的四组空气质量站数据上进行，模拟单站长达48小时的假设性故障，以PM10浓度为预测目标，并与多个基线和已发表的深度学习模型进行对比。

Result: SATADL在不同预测窗口下均表现出优于基线模型和已发表深度学习模型的性能，无论是在决定系数（R²）还是均方根误差（RMSE）指标上，证明了其作为虚拟代理站的适用性。

Conclusion: SATADL作为一种深度学习模型，能够有效地在监测站故障期间生成可靠的替代预报数据，为城市空气质量监测系统的鲁棒性提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Poor air quality in urban areas is driven by a complex chain of processes and presents a significant public health concern. To better understand and control the mechanisms that determine air quality, cities deploy networks of measurement stations, and launch initiatives for collecting denser data about the concentration of pollutants in the atmosphere. Extracting insights from the stations relies on their reliable and uninterrupted operation. However, hardware is susceptible to faults and black- outs that may result in data unavailability, which affects the overall quality of analyses. In this paper, we present a deep-learning model, called SATADL, which can infer complex relations and output multiple-hour-ahead air-quality forecasts. The goal of the model is to simulate the mea- surements of an unresponsive station until its operation is restored. The architecture of the model, which allows it to extract information from different aspects of the data, is described in detail and a careful examination of all of its components is provided. We demonstrate the performance of SATADL on four sets of air quality stations from around the world, by using it to simulate the concentration of PM10 for periods of hypothetical failures of one of the measurement stations, lasting for as long as 48 hours. A selection of baseline and published deep learning models were trained and used as a benchmark. The results show that SATADL per- forms better across different prediction windows, for both coefficient of determination and root mean squared error, demonstrating its suitability as a virtual proxy station.

</details>


### [323] [Small-Scale Experiments: Are We There Yet?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11859)
*Nicholas Lourie,Kyunghyun Cho,Karen Ullrich,Sanae Lotfi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现超参数是导致小规模模型无法遵循规模定律（scaling laws）的关键混淆因素。小模型对超参数极其敏感，但这种敏感性随规模增大而减弱，超参数损失曲面在高维空间变得更低维。作者提出了一套"模型中心研究"的新方法论，从小规模实验中成功恢复了大规模实验的结论（预归一化在更大模型中效果更好），证明只要正确调整超参数，小规模实验可以兑现规模定律的承诺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规模定律曾承诺实现低成本实验，但六年过去了未能完全兑现。研究者发现规模定律在4M参数起的小规模模型上不可靠，因而得出结论认为必须使用大规模模型。作者质疑这一结论，认为真正的问题在于超参数这一被忽略的混淆因素。

Method: 1) 对基本规模定律配方进行消融实验，证明精心调优的超参数比其他任何成分都重要；2) 分析了超参数敏感性随规模变化的行为，揭示超参数损失曲面在高维空间变为低维的原因；3) 综合最新文献，提出一套新的"模型中心研究"方法论，并将其应用于一个经典问题——Transformer架构中归一化层的位置选择。

Result: 小模型对超参数高度敏感，但敏感性随规模增长而减弱；仅当模型处于完全调优的边界（fully tuned frontier）时规模定律才会显现。通过新方法论在小规模实验中恢复了大规模实验的结论：随着模型增大，预归一化（pre-normalization）效果更好。

Conclusion: 规模定律在小模型中确实存在，但外推存在统计局限性，需要整体性（holistic）的方法。只要使用正确的工具和理解超参数的作用，小规模实验能够兑现规模定律长期以来承诺的价值，无需依赖大规模模型。

Abstract: Scaling laws promised cost-effective experiments; six years later, they have yet to fully deliver. Instead, researchers have found them unreliable at small scales (starting at 4M parameters) and concluded that sizable models cannot be avoided. We show this is not the case: the confounding factor is hyperparameters. Small models are highly sensitive, but hyperparameter sensitivity fades with scale. This small-scale sensitivity makes scaling laws easy to miss because they only emerge on the fully tuned frontier, and reaching that frontier requires an extensive search far beyond what most ever run. By ablating the basic scaling law recipe, we show well-tuned hyperparameters matter more than any other ingredient. Further, we reveal why those hyperparameters become easier to find: as scale increases, the hyperparameter loss surface becomes lower dimensional. Nevertheless while scaling laws exist in small models, extrapolation hits statistical limitations. A holistic approach is required. Synthesizing our insights with the recent literature, we develop a new methodology for model-centric research and demonstrate it on a question that once took the field years to settle: where to place normalization layers in the transformer architecture. From small-scale experiments, we recover the large scale result: pre-normalization works better as models grow in size. With the right tools and a better understanding, small-scale experiments can deliver on scaling laws' long-awaited promise.

</details>


### [324] [Forward and Inverse Virtual Metrology for Phototransistor Gain: A Hierarchical, Uncertainty-Aware Approach for Small Production Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11868)
*Mahshid Amirabgir,Lorenza Ferrario,Paolo Conci,Mahdieh Amirabgir,Giancarlo Orengo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了硅双极光电晶体管制造中基于工艺参数预测器件增益的问题。在仅有13-14次工艺运行的真实小样本、分层结构制造历史上，作者发现器件增益约一半方差存在于工艺运行之间而非之内，据此开发了具有不确定性感知的前向增益预测器、反向配方搜索工具，以及针对制造嵌套实体（批次/晶圆/裸片）的多层级数据质量评估框架，并开源了数据与代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅双极光电晶体管的工艺流定制、优化和稳定化需要在洁净室中花费数月才能测出成品器件，因此一个能在运行前根据工艺参数预测器件增益的模型，其价值远超其精度本身。传统虚拟计量学依赖大规模语料库，而本问题面临的是小样本、分层结构的真实制造历史，亟需针对性方法。

Method: 首先对器件增益方差进行分解，发现约一半方差来自工艺运行之间的差异而非运行内部，说明仅基于配方（recipe）的预测存在理论上的性能上界。在此基础上构建了：(1) 前向增益预测器，提供相对且具有不确定性感知的信号；(2) 反向搜索工具，给定目标增益返回匹配的工艺配方；(3) 面向制造嵌套物理实体（批次、晶圆、裸片）的多层级数据质量评估框架，含显式的跨层级关联评分。数据集已归一化，分析代码全部开源以保证可复现性。

Result: 方差分解表明约一半的器件增益方差存在于工艺运行之间而非运行内部，这为配方驱动的预测设置了性能约束。作者成功实现了前向预测、反向配方搜索和多层级数据质量评估三大功能，并发布了标准化数据集与分析代码实现完全可复现。

Conclusion: 该工作证明在小样本、分层结构的半导体制造场景下，即使精度有限，基于工艺参数的前向预测、反向搜索和系统化数据质量评估仍具有重要工程价值，为虚拟计量学在小样本领域的拓展提供了范例。

Abstract: The customization, optimization and stabilization of the process flow of a silicon bipolar phototransistor commits months of cleanroom time before a finished device can be measured, so a model that predicts device gain from process parameters before a run has value out of proportion to its accuracy. We study this problem on a real fabrication history, thirteen to fourteen process runs of a single device: a small-sample, hierarchically structured setting unlike the large-corpus regime of conventional virtual metrology. Decomposing the variance of device gain, we find that roughly half of it lies between process runs rather than within them, so recipe-only prediction is bounded by construction. Building on these findings we provide a forward gain predictor with a relative, uncertainty-aware signal, an inverse search that returns recipes for a target gain, and, as the foundation for all of it, a multi-level data-quality assessment tailored to the nested physical entities of fabrication (batch, wafer, die) with an explicit cross-level linkage score. The normalized dataset and analysis code are released for full reproducibility.

</details>


### [325] [DCM Bandits: Multiplayer Information Asymmetric Cascading Bandits for Multiple Clicks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11873)
*Andy Wang,Charlton Shih,William Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将依赖点击模型(DCM)Bandits扩展到多玩家信息不对称设置，多个智能体与共享排名列表交互并可观察多次点击，研究行动和奖励两方面的不对称性，为三种至少含一种不对称性的设置提供了次线性遗憾保证，并证明在小终止概率下无需知道终止排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DCM Bandits研究主要针对单智能体或对称多智能体场景，尚未深入探讨多玩家信息不对称环境下（如行动不对称和奖励不对称）的探索与利用权衡问题。新的不对称性引入会对选择策略带来全新挑战。

Method: 扩展DCM Bandits到多玩家信息不对称环境，构建了三个分别包含至少一种不对称性（行动侧和/或奖励侧）的设置，设计相应的选择策略并给出次线性遗憾界分析；同时关注反馈结构（完整反馈vs首次点击反馈）对探索协调的影响。

Result: 为三种不对称设置建立了次线性遗憾保证；证明了在小终止概率条件下，终止排序的先验知识并非必需，改进了先前单智能体结果；实验验证算法在不对称环境中表现良好，并揭示了完整反馈与首次点击反馈之间的关键区别。

Conclusion: 多玩家信息不对称DCM Bandits是可行且可有效求解的，反馈结构对多智能体探索协调和遗憾最小化有决定性作用；为三个不对称设置建立匹配信息论下界仍为开放问题。

Abstract: In this work, we extend the Dependent Click Model (DCM) Bandits to a multiplayer information-asymmetric setting, where multiple agents interact with a shared ranked list and may observe multiple clicks per session, introducing new challenges for selection strategies. We study asymmetry in (1) actions and (2) rewards, providing sublinear regret guarantees for three settings where at least one asymmetry is present. Establishing matching information-theoretic lower bounds for these settings is left as an open problem. We further show that for small termination probabilities, the termination ranking need not be known, improving on prior single-agent results. Experiments confirm that our algorithms perform well across asymmetric environments and highlight the critical role of feedback structure, specifically the distinction between full versus first-click feedback, in coordinating exploration and minimizing regret.

</details>


### [326] [Disentangling the Expressivity of RoPE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11909)
*Selim Jerad,Anej Svete,Jiaoda Li,Ryan Cotterell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论形式化角度区分了两种关于旋转位置编码（RoPE）成功原因的解释：一种强调周期性位置信息对应模谓词表达力，另一种强调位置锚点与局部偏移机制。研究发现，若每个旋转分量都是周期的，RoPE transformers恰好可识别过去时间逻辑加模谓词可定义的语言；而常规RoPE的旋转从不重复，仅产生基于精度约束的有界局部回溯模拟而非全长度模特征。实验验证了这一分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoPE在Transformer中应用广泛且效果显著，但对其成功原因存在两种竞争性理论解释：表达力研究将其与模谓词（modular predicates）联系，机制与长上下文研究则强调位置锚点和局部偏移。作者希望形式化这两种解释，以统一理解RoPE的理论性质，并缩小理论表达力刻画与实际使用模型之间的差距。

Method: 作者在完全均匀、有限精度soft-attention transformer框架下对两种RoPE变体进行形式化分析。首先证明若每个旋转分量均为周期性的，RoPE transformers精确识别过去时间逻辑加模谓词（past temporal logic with modular predicates）可定义的语言类别；随后分析常规RoPE（旋转不重复）的性质，将其刻画为基于精度的有界固定偏移回溯算子模拟。最后通过构造式周期调度与常规RoPE的对照实验验证理论分离。

Result: 定理性地证明了周期性RoPE的表达力等价于过去时间逻辑加模谓词；而常规RoPE（旋转永不重复）不具备全长度模特征表征能力，仅能实现精度依赖的有界局部偏移回溯。实验表明周期型RoPE在模语言上能长度泛化，常规RoPE更类似有界局部性偏置，且会损害需要位置不变性访问远距离上下文的任务。

Conclusion: RoPE的理论表达力取决于旋转分量是否周期性重复：周期化RoPE获得模谓词级别的形式化语言刻画，而常规RoPE本质上是精度受限的有界局部回溯机制。该工作澄清了两类解释各自适用的条件，使RoPE的理论分析与实际应用更为贴近。

Abstract: Two accounts recur in explanations of the success of rotary position embeddings (RoPE). Expressivity studies associate periodic position information with modular predicates, whereas mechanistic and long-context studies emphasize positional anchors and local offsets. We formalize both accounts for fully uniform, finite-precision soft-attention transformers. We find that, if every rotary component is periodic, RoPE transformers recognize exactly the languages definable in past temporal logic with modular predicates. Conventional RoPE is different: The rotations it computes never repeat. This yields a precision-dependent bounded simulation of fixed-offset look-back operators, rather than an all-length modular characterization. Controlled experiments match this separation: Constructed periodic schedules length-generalize on modular languages, while conventional RoPE behaves more like a bounded locality bias and can impair tasks requiring position-invariant access to distant context. Altogether, our findings shed light on RoPE transformers, bringing theoretical expressivity characterizations closer to models used in practice.

</details>


### [327] [A Factor Graph Approach to Scalable Multi-Output Gaussian Process Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11917)
*Wouter W. L. Nuijten,Esther G. van Pelt,Albert Podusenko,İsmail Şenöz,Wouter M. Kouw*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将多输出高斯过程回归建模为Forney风格因子图，通过最近邻链将候选输入排序为一维序列，再利用线性高斯转移因子上的精确消息传递实现计算复杂度从O(N^3)降至O(C(DL^2+L^3))。在低输入维度下，该方法的后验与精确核矩阵基线高度吻合；在电力时间序列预测中，该方法在保持预测精度的同时实现线性扩展，且对缺失观测无需重构协方差矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多输出高斯过程回归在观测次数乘以输出维度上呈立方复杂度，且当不同输出在不同输入点被观测时，稠密核矩阵方法需要特殊处理。现有方法（如精确核矩阵、稀疏变分、最近邻）在可扩展性或精度上各有局限，需要一种既能处理缺失数据又能高效扩展的框架。

Method: 将多输出高斯过程回归表达为Forney风格因子图：(1) 用最近邻链将固定候选集C个输入排序为一维序列；(2) 沿链上潜变量Matérn过程通过线性高斯转移因子演化；(3) 线性模型核心化（LMC）将L个潜过程通过确定性混合因子混合为D个输出；(4) 每个输出有独立的标量观测因子。后验计算通过在链上进行精确高斯消息传递完成，成本为O(C(DL^2+L^3))，缺失观测只需省略局部因子，无需重构协方差矩阵。

Result: 在合成输入维度扫描中，低输入维度下因子图后验与精确核矩阵后验高度接近，随输入维度增大差距逐渐扩大，但仍与两个近似基线（稀疏变分、最近邻）保持竞争力。在电力时间序列预测中，因子图方法在预测精度上与所有三个基线持平，同时计算时间随数据点数线性扩展，而精确核矩阵方法已不可行，诱导点基线仍然明显更慢。

Conclusion: 该因子图公式在数据样本数量和缺失观测比例上具有良好的可扩展性，最适合低输入维度的候选集场景。它为多输出高斯过程回归提供了一种同时兼顾精确性（低维时接近精确核矩阵）与高效率（线性扩展）的替代方案。

Abstract: Multi-output Gaussian process regression scales cubically in the number of observations times outputs, and dense kernel-matrix methods need bespoke handling whenever different outputs are observed at different inputs. We express multi-output Gaussian process regression as a Forney-style factor graph in which a nearest-neighbor chain orders a fixed candidate set of $C$ inputs into a one-dimensional sequence. Along this chain, latent Matérn processes evolve through linear-Gaussian transition factors, while the linear model of coregionalization mixes $L$ latent processes into $D$ outputs through a deterministic mixing factor and per-output scalar observation factors. Posterior computation reduces to exact Gaussian message passing on the chain at cost $\mathcal{O}(C(DL^2 + L^3))$ after chain construction, and missing observations omit their local factor without any covariance-matrix restructuring. The formulation therefore scales in the number of data samples and in the rate of missing observations, while remaining best suited to candidate sets in low input dimension.We compare the factor-graph formulation against an exact kernel-matrix baseline, a sparse-variational inducing-point baseline, and a nearest-neighbor baseline on a synthetic input-dimension sweep and on electricity time series forecasting. At low input dimension the factor-graph posterior tracks the exact kernel-matrix posterior closely, and the gap grows gradually as input dimension increases while staying competitive with both approximate baselines. On the electricity time series our factor-graph formulation matches all three baselines in forecast accuracy while scaling linearly in the number of data points, where the exact kernel-matrix method becomes infeasible and the inducing-point baseline remains substantially slower.

</details>


### [328] [Distillation of Foundation Models for Time-dependent PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11937)
*Daniel Musekamp,Boshra Ariguib,Andrei Manolache,Mathias Niepert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TREX是一个知识蒸馏框架，将预训练基础模型（用于时间相关PDE）的能力迁移到紧凑高效的学生模型中，通过教师滚动生成长合成轨迹来增强有限的微调数据，使学生模型在参数减少数个数量级、推理速度提升一个数量级以上的情况下，达到或超越教师模型的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用于时间相关PDE的基础模型虽然在下游任务中表现优异，但模型规模大、计算成本高，难以作为数值求解器的快速替代品，限制了其在实际场景中的实用性。

Method: TREX包含三个关键组分：(1) 从微调后的教师模型出发，通过教师自回归滚动生成长期合成轨迹来增强有限的标注数据，可选地注入周期性噪声以模拟局部恢复行为；(2) 从教师诱导的滚动分布中采样，无需显式知道初始条件分布；(3) 学生模型可以融入任务特定的归纳偏置（如等变性），而教师不一定强制执行这些偏置。

Result: 在多个PDE基准上，TREX训练出的学生模型能够匹配或超越教师模型的精度，同时参数数量减少数个数量级，推理速度提升超过一个数量级。

Conclusion: TREX展示了一种将大规模PDE基础模型的预测能力有效蒸馏到紧凑学生模型的有效范式，使得这类模型的优势能够在低资源场景下被实际利用，同时还能通过引入任务特定的归纳偏置进一步提升性能。

Abstract: Foundation models for time-dependent partial differential equations (PDEs) are trained on large and diverse collections of physical systems and can generalize effectively to new downstream tasks. After fine-tuning on only a few trajectories from a target domain, they can achieve strong accuracy in low-data regimes. However, these models are typically large and computationally intensive, limiting their usefulness as fast surrogates for numerical solvers. We propose Teacher Rollout Extension (TREX), a knowledge distillation framework that transfers the predictive capability of a pretrained foundation model into a compact and efficient student. Starting from a fine-tuned teacher, TREX augments limited downstream data by generating long synthetic trajectories through teacher rollouts, optionally with periodic noise injection. This procedure samples from the teacher-induced rollout distribution without requiring explicit knowledge of the initial-condition distribution, while exposing the student to long-horizon states and local recovery behavior around states encountered during autoregressive prediction. The student can further incorporate task-specific inductive biases, such as equivariance, that the teacher does not necessarily enforce. We evaluate TREX on multiple PDE benchmarks. The resulting students can match or surpass the teacher's accuracy while reducing the number of parameters by several orders of magnitude and achieving more than an order-of-magnitude speedup in inference.

</details>


### [329] [TailBooster: A Dual-Layer Generative Framework for Extreme Value Augmentation with Operational Validity Enforcement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11951)
*Karim Aly,Alexei Sharpanskykh,Jacco Hoekstra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TailBooster，一个双层次生成式框架，用于航空运输极端事件（如严重到达延误和异常飞行时间）的表格式数据增强。该框架结合生成建模与双层异常检测：统计层通过四分位距提取尾部分布样本，供Tabular VAE学习；深度学习层通过自编码器清洗，剔除不符合历史运行包络的合成记录。在美航班数据上，该方法将极端飞行时间预测的MAE降低47-49%，极端到达延误预测降低29-57%，同时显著提升操作有效性和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空运输中的极端事件（严重延误、异常飞行时间）会造成级联网络中断和巨大运营、经济与安全成本。这类事件在历史记录中非常稀少，导致机器学习模型的训练信号不足。现有合成数据增强方法存在两大缺陷：传统生成模型对分布尾部表征不足，且无法保证生成运营上不可行的实例（如短飞行时间配长飞行距离）。尚无方法能同时解决混合类型表格数据上的这两个问题。

Method: TailBooster采用双层次生成框架：(1) 统计层利用四分位距（IQR）提取极端值，为专用生成模型（Tabular Variational Autoencoder，TVAE）提供尾部集中的训练信号；(2) 深度学习层应用基于自编码器的清洗机制，丢弃违反从历史数据学到的运营包络的合成记录。整个框架完全数据驱动且模型无关。

Result: 在美航班数据上从五个维度评估：多样性、统计相似性、保真度、操作有效性和实用性（后两者为主要改进目标）。数据驱动的清洗显著提升操作有效性，定向增强提升了极端事件预测的实用性。在六个回归算法上，与常规合成数据相比，训练集使用该框架生成的记录使得极端飞行时间的MAE降低47-49%，极端到达延误的MAE降低29-57%，当真实记录与合成极端值混合时获得同样提升。

Conclusion: TailBooster通过结合统计提取与深度学习清洗，有效解决了极端事件表格数据增强中尾部表征不足和运营不可行两个核心问题。由于其完全数据驱动和模型无关的特性，该方法可扩展到极端事件预测至关重要且缺乏领域特定规则的各类应用领域。

Abstract: Extreme events in air transport, such as severe arrival delays and abnormal air times, cause cascading network disruptions with substantial operational, economic, and safety costs. Such events are rare in historical records, leaving insufficient training signal for machine learning models. Synthetic data augmentation offers a principled solution, but conventional generative models under-represent distributional tails and give no guarantee against operationally infeasible instances, such as a short air time paired with a long flight distance. No existing approach addresses both limitations for mixed-type tabular records. We propose TailBooster, a dual-layer generative framework combining generative modelling with two anomaly detection layers. A statistical layer extracts extremes via the interquartile range, supplying tail-concentrated training signal to dedicated generative models, here a Tabular Variational Autoencoder. A deep learning layer then applies autoencoder-based cleaning, discarding synthetic records that violate the operational envelope learned from historical data. The framework was evaluated on US flight records across five dimensions: diversity, statistical similarity, fidelity, operational validity, and utility, the latter two being the primary improvement targets. Data-driven cleaning markedly improved operational validity, while targeted augmentation enhanced utility for extreme-event prediction. Across six regression algorithms, training on the framework's records reduced Mean Absolute Error by 47-49% on extreme air time and 29-57% on extreme arrival delay prediction relative to conventional synthetic data, with comparable gains when real records were enriched with synthetic extremes. Being fully data-driven and model-agnostic, TailBooster extends to domains where extreme-event prediction is critical and domain-specific rules are unavailable.

</details>


### [330] [LoongReflect: Boosting Long-Horizon Reflection in Search Agents via Global Perspective Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11967)
*Zhixin Zhang,Xinke Jiang,Zhibang Yang,Weixuan Xu,Guohong Qiu,Xu Chu,Junfeng Zhao,Yasha Wang*
